## 793b61 Third review: findings waiting on open questions

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent) found them and Orchestrator (Claude) held them. Owner (Joe) resolved each through the answers named below; Author (agent) applied them and awaits owner validation.
- **Note:** the review's original text is not kept in the repository. Each finding below is written from the question it raised and from the summary this entry carried before the answers, so its wording is the author's.
- **H1: lazy chunks versus chunk content.**
  - **Finding:** [792a14f One unified query](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14f-one-unified-query) requires every recall query to run with `--lazy-chunks`, which prints a chunk as its id and score only ([792a7s Lazy chunks](../../specs/product/memory-query.md#792a7s-lazy-chunks)). The product spec had the System run the query under its defaults, which print chunks with content, and had `793a2e1f A chunk shown without content is delivered with it` (retired) deliver a chunk's content, so the System and the instruction path would deliver different payloads. The volume baseline in [793c2 Are the volume bounds and latency budget accepted?](793c_open-questions.md#793c2-are-the-volume-bounds-and-latency-budget-accepted) was also measured with chunk content.
  - **Fix to be applied:** decide whether the System's chunks carry content, then make `793a2j Volume equals the depth's output`, [793a2e Nothing already in context is delivered again](793a_product-spec.md#793a2e-nothing-already-in-context-is-delivered-again) and the chunk scenarios say the same thing.
  - **Resolution:** [793c15 Does the System inject chunks with their content?](793c_open-questions.md#793c15-does-the-system-inject-chunks-with-their-content) answered (b), lazy chunks; applied in [793b62 Chunks are injected lazily, as stubs](#793b62-chunks-are-injected-lazily-as-stubs).
- **H2: a coverage write versus used-note activation.**
  - **Finding:** at a deep moment the coverage write (`793a3g Deep recall's writes are the System's`, [792a14j Coverage is judged by the agent and written once per cluster](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14j-coverage-is-judged-by-the-agent-and-written-once-per-cluster)) adds chunk sources to an existing note, and recall's covered outcome does so with `--activate`, which bumps the note's recency. `793a3k` reserves that bump for notes the agent used, so the two requirements could not both hold.
  - **Fix to be applied:** decide whether a coverage write activates the note it covers, and how the System knows a note was used.
  - **Resolution:** [793c16 At a deep moment, does the System's coverage write activate the note it covers?](793c_open-questions.md#793c16-at-a-deep-moment-does-the-systems-coverage-write-activate-the-note-it-covers) answered that the write attaches evidence and the covering note is injected but not activated, and [793c21 How does the System know a note was used?](793c_open-questions.md#793c21-how-does-the-system-know-a-note-was-used) answered that use is inferred from the transcript; applied in [793b63 A coverage write attaches evidence and injects, and never activates](#793b63-a-coverage-write-attaches-evidence-and-injects-and-never-activates) and [793b68 Use is inferred from the transcript and graded](#793b68-use-is-inferred-from-the-transcript-and-graded).
- **H3: the latency bound omitted the reading.**
  - **Finding:** `793a5g` bounded the added latency by generating the phrases and running the queries. Escalation from glance to deep (`793a2h Recall depth per moment`, [792a14d Glance escalates to deep for a recent standard](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14d-glance-escalates-to-deep-for-a-recent-standard)) and the recommendation check ([792a14o An emergent recommendation is checked against the vault](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14o-an-emergent-recommendation-is-checked-against-the-vault)) depend on reading the glance results, which takes agent time the bound left out, so the System could be held to less work than the instruction path does.
  - **Fix to be applied:** decide whether the bound includes that reading.
  - **Resolution:** [793c17 Does the latency bound include reading the glance results?](793c_open-questions.md#793c17-does-the-latency-bound-include-reading-the-glance-results) answered (a), include it; applied in [793b64 Latency includes reading the results](#793b64-latency-includes-reading-the-results).
- **M1: the integration was undefined.**
  - **Finding:** `793a4g` made the System active whenever "the engram integration" is installed and enabled, without saying what the integration is, how it gets installed, or how an operator turns it off.
  - **Fix to be applied:** define the term and specify installation and turning off.
  - **Resolution:** [793c18 How is the System installed and turned off?](793c_open-questions.md#793c18-how-is-the-system-installed-and-turned-off) answered (a) with installation in this spec, and [793c22 There is no uninstall command today](793c_open-questions.md#793c22-there-is-no-uninstall-command-today) answered (b), a general uninstall in its own change; applied in [793b65 Installation is part of this spec](#793b65-installation-is-part-of-this-spec) and [793b69 The uninstall command is a separate change](#793b69-the-uninstall-command-is-a-separate-change).
- **Rationale:** an answer may change the fix, so applying them first would risk rework.
- **Status:** resolved and applied in commit 1adbb77e. [793c19 Does the log keep the depth, each query's phrases and the glance escalation?](793c_open-questions.md#793c19-does-the-log-keep-the-depth-each-querys-phrases-and-the-glance-escalation) (keep the log fields) and [793c20 Must injected chunks identify their source like notes?](793c_open-questions.md#793c20-must-injected-chunks-identify-their-source-like-notes) (chunk sources identify themselves), raised by the same review, were answered and applied in [793b66 The log keeps depth, phrases and escalation](#793b66-the-log-keeps-depth-phrases-and-escalation) and [793b67 Every injected item names its source](#793b67-every-injected-item-names-its-source). [793c13 Does the System activate notes the agent retrieved itself?](793c_open-questions.md#793c13-does-the-system-activate-notes-the-agent-retrieved-itself) and [793c14 Should reinforcing an existing note be a MUST?](793c_open-questions.md#793c14-should-reinforcing-an-existing-note-be-a-must) were settled before this round and are not re-raised.

- **Status update:** superseded in part by [793c29 Is the System a delegate for the existing memory skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c29-is-the-system-a-delegate-for-the-existing-memory-skills) ([793b90 Restructure 793a as a delegation of the memory skills](#793b90-restructure-793a-as-a-delegation-of-the-memory-skills)): the restructure retired `793a2h`, `793a2j`, `793a3g`.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a3k`, `793a4g`, `793a5g`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b62 Chunks are injected lazily, as stubs

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c15 Does the System inject chunks with their content?](793c_open-questions.md#793c15-does-the-system-inject-chunks-with-their-content), chose (b): lazy chunks as recall runs the query, a rule against re-injecting the same chunk stub, and the chunk-content scenario retired. Author (agent), interpreting [793c15 Does the System inject chunks with their content?](793c_open-questions.md#793c15-does-the-system-inject-chunks-with-their-content), chose the wording below; awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** `793a2j Volume equals the depth's output` says the System's queries use `--lazy-chunks` at glance, at deep and for the recommendation check, names a note as injected with its content and a chunk as a chunk stub, and says each query runs with its default result limit and content budget. The terms in [793a1 Problem Statement](793a_product-spec.md#793a1-problem-statement) gain "Chunk stub", and "Injection" covers it. [793a2e Nothing already in context is delivered again](793a_product-spec.md#793a2e-nothing-already-in-context-is-delivered-again) counts a chunk stub already shown as present and forbids injecting it again, with scenario `793a2e1i`.
- **Choice:** `793a2e1f` is retired and not reused. `793a2e1h A chunk whose content the agent fetched is not injected` keeps its ID but now covers a chunk whose content the agent fetched itself, because under lazy chunks the System never delivers content; the scenario was retitled. `793a2j1e Chunks are delivered as stubs` asserts that a chunk arrives as id and score without content, and the scenarios for the incidental items and the depth's output say "chunk stubs".
- **Choice:** the volume figures of [793c2 Are the volume bounds and latency budget accepted?](793c_open-questions.md#793c2-are-the-volume-bounds-and-latency-budget-accepted) (135 KB, 45 items) were measured with chunk content, so under lazy chunks they are an upper bound. No normative text carries them.
- **Rationale:** the System should deliver what the instruction path's recall delivers, and recall runs lazy; a stub costs little and the agent can fetch the chunk if it needs it.
- **Alternatives:** (a) run the query without `--lazy-chunks` and deliver chunk content. Not chosen.

- **Status update:** superseded in part by [793c29 Is the System a delegate for the existing memory skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c29-is-the-system-a-delegate-for-the-existing-memory-skills) ([793b90 Restructure 793a as a delegation of the memory skills](#793b90-restructure-793a-as-a-delegation-of-the-memory-skills)): the restructure retired `793a2j`, `793a2j1e`.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a2e1i`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b63 A coverage write attaches evidence and injects, and never activates

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c16 At a deep moment, does the System's coverage write activate the note it covers?](793c_open-questions.md#793c16-at-a-deep-moment-does-the-systems-coverage-write-activate-the-note-it-covers), decided that when a note covers a cluster the cluster's chunks are attached to it as evidence and the covering note is injected into the working agent, subject to no re-injection, and is marked used only on the same transcript evidence as any injected note ([793c21 How does the System know a note was used?](793c_open-questions.md#793c21-how-does-the-system-know-a-note-was-used)). Author (agent), interpreting [793c16 At a deep moment, does the System's coverage write activate the note it covers?](793c_open-questions.md#793c16-at-a-deep-moment-does-the-systems-coverage-write-activate-the-note-it-covers), chose the wording below; awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** `793a3g Deep recall's writes are the System's` lists the three outcomes of [792a14j Coverage is judged by the agent and written once per cluster](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14j-coverage-is-judged-by-the-agent-and-written-once-per-cluster) (covered, near, absent) and refers to that requirement for their definitions. For covered it attaches the chunks as evidence without rewriting the note, injects the note subject to [793a2e Nothing already in context is delivered again](793a_product-spec.md#793a2e-nothing-already-in-context-is-delivered-again), and does not activate it. Near amends the note with the cluster's chunks and the re-synthesized content, and absent writes one new note, as before.
- **Choice:** for an amend, provenance is defined as Joe asked: the chunk sources added and the log entry name the session and turn, and the note's original source is unchanged. `793a3d` still governs notes the System writes.
- **Choice:** the near amend does not activate the note either. Joe's answer was about the covered outcome. The System never runs recall's own activation step ([793b68 Use is inferred from the transcript and graded](#793b68-use-is-inferred-from-the-transcript-and-graded)), so I read his rule that only transcript evidence activates a note as covering near, and asked about injecting a near note in [793c23 Is a note amended as near injected?](793c_open-questions.md#793c23-is-a-note-amended-as-near-injected). The overlap of the covering note's injection with `793a2j Volume equals the depth's output` is asked in [793c24 Does the covering note's injection add anything to the depth's output?](793c_open-questions.md#793c24-does-the-covering-notes-injection-add-anything-to-the-depths-output).
- **Choice:** scenarios `793a3g1c A covered cluster gets evidence and its note is injected`, `793a3g1d A covering note already in context is not injected again` and `793a3g1e A near cluster amends the note` are new; the covered and near ones assert that the note's recency is unchanged by the write.
- **Rationale:** a coverage write records that a note covers a cluster, not that the agent used it.
- **Alternatives:** (b) the coverage write activates the covered note. Not chosen.
- **Status:** the points it raised were answered: [793c23 Is a note amended as near injected?](793c_open-questions.md#793c23-is-a-note-amended-as-near-injected) in [793b71 A near-amended note is injected](#793b71-a-near-amended-note-is-injected) and [793c24 Does the covering note's injection add anything to the depth's output?](793c_open-questions.md#793c24-does-the-covering-notes-injection-add-anything-to-the-depths-output) in [793b72 Covering note and cluster are both injected](#793b72-covering-note-and-cluster-are-both-injected), applied in commit da69f814.

- **Status update:** superseded in part by [793c29 Is the System a delegate for the existing memory skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c29-is-the-system-a-delegate-for-the-existing-memory-skills) ([793b90 Restructure 793a as a delegation of the memory skills](#793b90-restructure-793a-as-a-delegation-of-the-memory-skills)): the restructure retired `793a2j`, `793a3g`, `793a3g1c`, `793a3g1d`, `793a3g1e`.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a3d`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b64 Latency includes reading the results

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c17 Does the latency bound include reading the glance results?](793c_open-questions.md#793c17-does-the-latency-bound-include-reading-the-glance-results), chose (a): the bound includes reading the results that escalation and the recommendation check depend on. Author (agent), interpreting [793c17 Does the latency bound include reading the glance results?](793c_open-questions.md#793c17-does-the-latency-bound-include-reading-the-glance-results), chose the wording below; awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** `793a5g` bounds the added latency by the instruction path's full lookup at that moment: generating the phrases, running the chosen depth's queries, and reading the results that escalation ([792a14d Glance escalates to deep for a recent standard](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14d-glance-escalates-to-deep-for-a-recent-standard)) and the recommendation check ([792a14o An emergent recommendation is checked against the vault](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14o-an-emergent-recommendation-is-checked-against-the-vault)) depend on. At an escalated moment that is both depths' queries and that reading. `793a5g1a` and `793a5g1b An escalated moment is compared with both depths` say the same.
- **Rationale:** it compares like with like and keeps the escalation triggers.
- **Alternatives:** (b) phrases and queries only, and (c) drop the result-dependent triggers. Not chosen.

- **Status update:** superseded in part by [793c29 Is the System a delegate for the existing memory skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c29-is-the-system-a-delegate-for-the-existing-memory-skills) ([793b90 Restructure 793a as a delegation of the memory skills](#793b90-restructure-793a-as-a-delegation-of-the-memory-skills)): the restructure retired `793a5g1b`.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a5g`, `793a5g1`, `793a5g1a`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b65 Installation is part of this spec

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c18 How is the System installed and turned off?](793c_open-questions.md#793c18-how-is-the-system-installed-and-turned-off), chose (a) with installation in this spec: `engram update` installs what the System needs, existing installs get it on their next update, and the System is turned off by uninstalling. Author (agent), interpreting [793c18 How is the System installed and turned off?](793c_open-questions.md#793c18-how-is-the-system-installed-and-turned-off), chose the wording below; awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** the Integration is defined in [793a1 Problem Statement](793a_product-spec.md#793a1-problem-statement) as what `engram update` installs for the System. New requirement `793a4h Installed by engram update and active in Claude Code` extends [install-and-update](../../specs/product/install-and-update.md), citing [792a11g The harnesses that are updated](../../specs/product/install-and-update.md#792a11g-the-harnesses-that-are-updated) (which harnesses are updated) and [792a11f Previewing an update](../../specs/product/install-and-update.md#792a11f-previewing-an-update) (a preview). A preview installing none of it follows from [792a11f Previewing an update](../../specs/product/install-and-update.md#792a11f-previewing-an-update)'s rule that a dry run changes nothing in a harness; it is stated because the Integration is new, with scenario `793a4h1c A preview installs none of the Integration`. "In which the System can run" ties it to [793a5c Other harnesses are unchanged](793a_product-spec.md#793a5c-other-harnesses-are-unchanged); the spec does not say whether Pi is such a harness. What the Integration consists of is not specified here.
- **Choice:** `793a4g` is rewritten: active in every session of a harness with the Integration installed, with no mode and no further step, and inert after the Integration is uninstalled. The "disabled via the harness's own mechanism" wording is gone. `793a4g1b` is retired; `793a4g1a` and `793a4g1c` remain. The scenario that an existing install gains the System on update is `793a4h1b An existing install gains the System and keeps what it had`, under the requirement that states the branch, so that two scenarios do not duplicate each other. Scenarios that spoke of the System "disabled and enabled" now say the Integration is uninstalled or installed (`793a5f1a`).
- **Rationale:** operators already run `engram update`, and one state is easier to reason about than a mode.
- **Alternatives:** (b) opt-in like guidance ([792a11l Guidance is managed only after opt-in](../../specs/product/install-and-update.md#792a11l-guidance-is-managed-only-after-opt-in)). Not chosen.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a4g`, `793a4g1`, `793a4g1a`, `793a4g1c`, `793a5f1`, `793a5f1a`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a4h`, `793a4h1b`, `793a4h1c`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b66 The log keeps depth, phrases and escalation

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c19 Does the log keep the depth, each query's phrases and the glance escalation?](793c_open-questions.md#793c19-does-the-log-keep-the-depth-each-querys-phrases-and-the-glance-escalation), chose (a): keep the fields. No spec change.
- **Choice:** `793a5e` logs the depth, the phrases of each query and whether glance escalated, as before. The only change to it is that each activation decision is logged with its evidence ([793b68 Use is inferred from the transcript and graded](#793b68-use-is-inferred-from-the-transcript-and-graded)).
- **Rationale:** the depth and escalation scenarios can be checked only from the operator-accessible log.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a5e`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b67 Every injected item names its source

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c20 Must injected chunks identify their source like notes?](793c_open-questions.md#793c20-must-injected-chunks-identify-their-source-like-notes), chose (a): every injected item identifies its source, a note by its file name and a chunk by its id. Author (agent), interpreting [793c20 Must injected chunks identify their source like notes?](793c_open-questions.md#793c20-must-injected-chunks-identify-their-source-like-notes), chose the wording below; awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** `793a2d Delivered memory is attributable` reads "Injected items MUST identify their source: a note by its file name and a chunk by its id". Its rationale notes that a chunk's id is what the agent fetches it by ([792a7t Reading one chunk](../../specs/product/memory-query.md#792a7t-reading-one-chunk)). `793a2d1a Delivered content names its source` now asserts the file name, and `793a2d1b A delivered chunk names its id` is new; its example id is illustrative, and the real form is `<source>#<anchor>` ([792a7b The payload](../../specs/product/memory-query.md#792a7b-the-payload)).
- **Rationale:** an item the agent cannot trace to its source cannot be weighed or looked up.

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a2d`, `793a2d1a`, `793a2d1b`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b68 Use is inferred from the transcript and graded

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c21 How does the System know a note was used?](793c_open-questions.md#793c21-how-does-the-system-know-a-note-was-used), chose (a): infer use from the transcript at turn-end capture, log each verdict with its evidence, and have the grader sample activation verdicts. Author (agent), interpreting [793c21 How does the System know a note was used?](793c_open-questions.md#793c21-how-does-the-system-know-a-note-was-used), chose the wording below; awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** `793a3k` has the System infer use from the transcript, with Joe's examples (quoting or citing the note, following its steps, taking the action it prescribes), log each verdict with its evidence, and not activate a note with no evidence. It adds that the System's recall does not run recall's own activation step (Step 2.7 of [792a14l Only the notes that were used are activated](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14l-only-the-notes-that-were-used-are-activated)), so its activations are the only ones the System makes; scenario `793a3k1e Recall's own activation step does not run` covers it. Scenarios `793a3k1a` and `793a3k1b` now speak of evidence in the transcript.
- **Choice:** grading is a sibling requirement, `793a3l`, not an extension of `793a3i`, which grades captures; the sibling keeps that title accurate. It samples verdicts, grades each against the transcript, and logs each grade, the sample size and the accuracy. The sample size is not fixed.
- **Choice:** `793a4f No delivery or capture in its own sessions` also covers the sessions that judge activation and grade its accuracy, and `793a5e` logs the evidence with each activation decision.
- **Rationale:** the System has no declaration from the agent, and asking for one would put memory bookkeeping back into the agent's context; inference from the transcript is measurable.
- **Alternatives:** (b) treat every injected note as used, and (c) do not activate at all. Not chosen.

- **Status update:** superseded in part by [793c29 Is the System a delegate for the existing memory skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c29-is-the-system-a-delegate-for-the-existing-memory-skills) ([793b90 Restructure 793a as a delegation of the memory skills](#793b90-restructure-793a-as-a-delegation-of-the-memory-skills)): the restructure retired `793a3k1e`.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites 7 IDs, each in the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction), which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a4f`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b69 The uninstall command is a separate change

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c22 There is no uninstall command today](793c_open-questions.md#793c22-there-is-no-uninstall-command-today), chose (b): a general `engram uninstall` in its own change, [#849](https://github.com/toejough/engram/issues/849).
- **Choice:** this change depends on #849. The uninstall branch of `793a4g` and scenario `793a4g1c` cannot be verified until #849 ships, and the System cannot be turned off before then. This spec specifies no uninstall behavior beyond "when the Integration has been uninstalled, the System injects, captures and records nothing". #849 is named only in the rationale of `793a4g` and here, not in normative text.
- **Rationale:** uninstalling is a capability of the install and update, not of this feature, and a general command gives every installed piece a removal path.
- **Alternatives:** (a) a command that removes only the System, and (c) documented manual steps. Not chosen.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a4g`, `793a4g1`, `793a4g1c`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b70 Questions raised while applying the answers

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** two points were ambiguous and were not decided silently: [793c23 Is a note amended as near injected?](793c_open-questions.md#793c23-is-a-note-amended-as-near-injected) (whether a note amended as near is injected) and [793c24 Does the covering note's injection add anything to the depth's output?](793c_open-questions.md#793c24-does-the-covering-notes-injection-add-anything-to-the-depths-output) (whether the covering note's injection adds anything beyond `793a2j Volume equals the depth's output`). Both carry a recommendation and block `793a3g Deep recall's writes are the System's`.
- **Rationale:** the answers to [793c16 At a deep moment, does the System's coverage write activate the note it covers?](793c_open-questions.md#793c16-at-a-deep-moment-does-the-systems-coverage-write-activate-the-note-it-covers) covered the covered outcome only, and the injection clause overlaps an existing rule.
- **Status:** both questions were answered: [793c23 Is a note amended as near injected?](793c_open-questions.md#793c23-is-a-note-amended-as-near-injected) in [793b71 A near-amended note is injected](#793b71-a-near-amended-note-is-injected) and [793c24 Does the covering note's injection add anything to the depth's output?](793c_open-questions.md#793c24-does-the-covering-notes-injection-add-anything-to-the-depths-output) in [793b72 Covering note and cluster are both injected](#793b72-covering-note-and-cluster-are-both-injected), applied in commit da69f814.

- **Status update:** superseded by [793c29 Is the System a delegate for the existing memory skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c29-is-the-system-a-delegate-for-the-existing-memory-skills) ([793b90 Restructure 793a as a delegation of the memory skills](#793b90-restructure-793a-as-a-delegation-of-the-memory-skills)): the restructure retired `793a2j`, `793a3g`.

## 793b71 A near-amended note is injected

- **Choice:** `793a3g Deep recall's writes are the System's` injects the amended note after a near amend, without activating it. [793a2e Nothing already in context is delivered again](793a_product-spec.md#793a2e-nothing-already-in-context-is-delivered-again) treats a note changed after delivery as not present in its changed form, with new scenario `793a2e1j`. `793a3g1e A near cluster amends the note` asserts the delivery.
- **Rationale:** in recall the agent doing the deep pass writes the amendment, so the amended content is in its context. Injecting it keeps that behavior.
- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c23 Is a note amended as near injected?](793c_open-questions.md#793c23-is-a-note-amended-as-near-injected); wording and the [793a2e Nothing already in context is delivered again](793a_product-spec.md#793a2e-nothing-already-in-context-is-delivered-again) clause by Orchestrator (Claude), awaiting owner validation.

- **Status update:** superseded in part by [793c29 Is the System a delegate for the existing memory skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c29-is-the-system-a-delegate-for-the-existing-memory-skills) ([793b90 Restructure 793a as a delegation of the memory skills](#793b90-restructure-793a-as-a-delegation-of-the-memory-skills)): the restructure retired `793a3g`, `793a3g1e`.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a2e1j`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b72 Covering note and cluster are both injected

- **Choice:** keep the explicit covering-note injection in `793a3g Deep recall's writes are the System's`, and inject the cluster's items as `793a2j Volume equals the depth's output` requires. Injecting the covering note in place of its cluster is a trim, tracked in #848.
- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c24 Does the covering note's injection add anything to the depth's output?](793c_open-questions.md#793c24-does-the-covering-notes-injection-add-anything-to-the-depths-output).

- **Status update:** superseded by [793c29 Is the System a delegate for the existing memory skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c29-is-the-system-a-delegate-for-the-existing-memory-skills) ([793b90 Restructure 793a as a delegation of the memory skills](#793b90-restructure-793a-as-a-delegation-of-the-memory-skills)): the restructure retired `793a2j`, `793a3g`.

## 793b73 H1: the volume ceiling excepts the notes the coverage writes inject

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Finding:** `793a2j Volume equals the depth's output` says the System MUST NOT inject more than the depth's queries return, but `793a3g Deep recall's writes are the System's` has the System inject the covering and the amended note, which the queries need not have returned ([793c24 Does the covering note's injection add anything to the depth's output?](793c_open-questions.md#793c24-does-the-covering-notes-injection-add-anything-to-the-depths-output) kept that clause). `793a2j1a Delivered items equal the depth's output` says "no other item is delivered" and so contradicts it.
- **Fix to be applied:** `793a2j Volume equals the depth's output`'s ceiling reads "MUST NOT inject more than those, except the covering and amended notes that `793a3g Deep recall's writes are the System's` injects". `793a2j1a Delivered items equal the depth's output` and `793a2j1d An escalated moment delivers both depths' output` read "no other item is delivered, except a covering or amended note injected under `793a3g`".
- **Status:** applied in commit 52eab1f0 (fourth review, round 4 of change 793).

- **Status update:** superseded by [793c29 Is the System a delegate for the existing memory skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c29-is-the-system-a-delegate-for-the-existing-memory-skills) ([793b90 Restructure 793a as a delegation of the memory skills](#793b90-restructure-793a-as-a-delegation-of-the-memory-skills)): the restructure retired `793a2j`, `793a2j1a`, `793a2j1d`, `793a3g`.

## 793b74 H2: a note is present unless its text changed

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Finding:** [793a2e Nothing already in context is delivered again](793a_product-spec.md#793a2e-nothing-already-in-context-is-delivered-again) treats a note whose "content changed" as not present in its changed form, but the covered outcome of `793a3g Deep recall's writes are the System's` attaches chunk sources to a note that the agent already holds, and a use activation changes its recency metadata. Read literally, either would make the note "changed" and inject it again, against `793a3g1d A covering note already in context is not injected again`. `793a3g1c A covered cluster gets evidence and its note is injected` said the covering note's "content is unchanged" although the attach adds sources.
- **Fix to be applied:** [793a2e Nothing already in context is delivered again](793a_product-spec.md#793a2e-nothing-already-in-context-is-delivered-again) reads: "A note whose text changed after the agent received it, other than in its sources or its recency metadata, is not present in its changed form." `793a3g1c A covered cluster gets evidence and its note is injected` reads "its text, other than its sources, is unchanged". New scenario `793a2e1k` covers a note whose sources and recency changed.
- **Consistency check:** `793a3g1d A covering note already in context is not injected again` holds: the attach changes only sources, so the note stays present and is delivered once. `793a3c No duplicates` reinforcement holds: reinforcing a note adds evidence and recency and rewrites no content (as in [792a22f A covered offer is reinforced and folded away](../../specs/product/curate.md#792a22f-a-covered-offer-is-reinforced-and-folded-away)), so a reinforced note stays present. `793a2e1j` (an amend that changes the text) still injects the changed note, and now says the text was changed by an amend that added a claim.
- **Status:** applied in commit 52eab1f0 (fourth review, round 4 of change 793).

- **Status update:** superseded in part by [793c29 Is the System a delegate for the existing memory skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c29-is-the-system-a-delegate-for-the-existing-memory-skills) ([793b90 Restructure 793a as a delegation of the memory skills](#793b90-restructure-793a-as-a-delegation-of-the-memory-skills)): the restructure retired `793a3c`, `793a3g`, `793a3g1c`, `793a3g1d`.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a2e1j`, `793a2e1k`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b75 H3: a parent-vault note is never amended or restated by the System

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Finding:** `793a3g Deep recall's writes are the System's` attaches evidence to, or amends, whatever note covers or nearly covers a cluster, and writes a new note when none does. [792a14k A note from the parent vault is activated, never amended](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14k-a-note-from-the-parent-vault-is-activated-never-amended) forbids amending a parent-vault note or its pending copy and forbids writing a note that restates it. `793a3g Deep recall's writes are the System's` did not carry that rule.
- **Fix to be applied:** `793a3g Deep recall's writes are the System's` gains a bullet: when the covering or near note is from the parent vault, or is its pending copy, the System MUST NOT amend it or write a note restating it, so the attach and the amend do not apply to it; it MUST inject it, subject to [793a2e Nothing already in context is delivered again](793a_product-spec.md#793a2e-nothing-already-in-context-is-delivered-again), and MUST activate it only under `793a3k`. New scenario `793a3g1f A parent-vault note is injected and never amended` covers a covering parent note, a near parent note and a covering pending copy, each unused by the agent.
- **Status:** applied in commit 52eab1f0 (fourth review, round 4 of change 793).

- **Status update:** superseded in part by [793c29 Is the System a delegate for the existing memory skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c29-is-the-system-a-delegate-for-the-existing-memory-skills) ([793b90 Restructure 793a as a delegation of the memory skills](#793b90-restructure-793a-as-a-delegation-of-the-memory-skills)): the restructure retired `793a3g`, `793a3g1f`.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a3k`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b76 The parent-note rule interprets 793c16 against 792a14k

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** [792a14k A note from the parent vault is activated, never amended](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14k-a-note-from-the-parent-vault-is-activated-never-amended) has three clauses: no amend, no restating note, and activate it when it covers a cluster. [793c16 At a deep moment, does the System's coverage write activate the note it covers?](793c_open-questions.md#793c16-at-a-deep-moment-does-the-systems-coverage-write-activate-the-note-it-covers) (answered by Joe) says the System's coverage write never activates a note; only transcript evidence of use does (`793a3k`). The new bullet of `793a3g Deep recall's writes are the System's` keeps the first two clauses and follows [793c16 At a deep moment, does the System's coverage write activate the note it covers?](793c_open-questions.md#793c16-at-a-deep-moment-does-the-systems-coverage-write-activate-the-note-it-covers) over the third, so a covering parent note is injected and activated only if the agent used it, and a used one is pulled down and left pending (`793a3k1d A used parent note is pulled down and left pending`).
- **Rationale:** Joe's answer to [793c16 At a deep moment, does the System's coverage write activate the note it covers?](793c_open-questions.md#793c16-at-a-deep-moment-does-the-systems-coverage-write-activate-the-note-it-covers) was about every covered note; a parent note is a covered note.
- **Alternatives:** activate a covering parent note at the coverage write, as [792a14k A note from the parent vault is activated, never amended](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14k-a-note-from-the-parent-vault-is-activated-never-amended) does. Not chosen, because it contradicts [793c16 At a deep moment, does the System's coverage write activate the note it covers?](793c_open-questions.md#793c16-at-a-deep-moment-does-the-systems-coverage-write-activate-the-note-it-covers).

- **Status update:** superseded in part by [793c29 Is the System a delegate for the existing memory skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c29-is-the-system-a-delegate-for-the-existing-memory-skills) ([793b90 Restructure 793a as a delegation of the memory skills](#793b90-restructure-793a-as-a-delegation-of-the-memory-skills)): the restructure retired `793a3g`, `793a3k1d`.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a3k`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b77 M1: the purview rule lists every effect outside the session

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Finding:** [793a4c Memory purview only](793a_product-spec.md#793a4c-memory-purview-only) lists vault notes and the log as the effects outside the session, but the System also writes each note's sidecar, pulls down pending copies of parent notes (`793a3k`), makes offers to the parent (`793a3j Direct writes go to the local vault`, [792a21n A local write is offered to the parent](../../specs/product/parent-sync.md#792a21n-a-local-write-is-offered-to-the-parent)) and starts System sessions (`793a4f No delivery or capture in its own sessions`).
- **Fix to be applied:** [793a4c Memory purview only](793a_product-spec.md#793a4c-memory-purview-only) lists them: vault notes with their sidecars, pending parent copies and parent offers, its log, and its System sessions. `793a4c1d` gives the scenario a parent vault and lists the same effects, and is retitled "Effects outside the session are the System's own writes".
- **Status:** applied in commit 52eab1f0 (fourth review, round 4 of change 793).

- **Status update:** superseded in part by [793c29 Is the System a delegate for the existing memory skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c29-is-the-system-a-delegate-for-the-existing-memory-skills) ([793b90 Restructure 793a as a delegation of the memory skills](#793b90-restructure-793a-as-a-delegation-of-the-memory-skills)): the restructure retired `793a3j`.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a3k`, `793a4c1d`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b78 M4: replay scenarios compare with the log, and the log is checked separately

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Finding:** `793a2j1a Delivered items equal the depth's output`, `793a2j1d An escalated moment delivers both depths' output`, `793a2a1c Only incidental items come back`, `793a5g1a` and `793a5g1b An escalated moment is compared with both depths` had the operator re-run the queries on "the same vault", but the vault may have changed since the moment, and the recency scaling of [792a7f Recency scaling](../../specs/product/memory-query.md#792a7f-recency-scaling) makes scores depend on when the query ran. A transcript cannot be compared reliably with a later re-run.
- **Fix to be applied:** the scenarios that compare the transcript with the items (`793a2j1a Delivered items equal the depth's output`, `793a2j1d An escalated moment delivers both depths' output`, `793a2a1c Only incidental items come back`) compare it with the items and scores the log records for that moment. New scenario `793a2j1f The log matches the depth's queries` separately checks the log against the depth's queries run on a copy of the vault as it was at that moment. `793a5g`, `793a5g1a` and `793a5g1b An escalated moment is compared with both depths` make the latency comparison "on a copy of the vault as it was at that moment, with the same input". `793a2a1c Only incidental items come back` also has its log reading after the When ([793b82 L3: the operator replay follows the When](#793b82-l3-the-operator-replay-follows-the-when)).
- **Open point:** the canonical query spec says the same vault and phrases give the same clusters ([792a7g Clusters](../../specs/product/memory-query.md#792a7g-clusters)); it does not say the explore picks repeat for the same vault and phrases. `793a2j1f The log matches the depth's queries` assumes the replay returns the logged items.
- **Status:** applied in commit 52eab1f0 (fourth review, round 4 of change 793).

- **Status update:** superseded in part by [793c29 Is the System a delegate for the existing memory skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c29-is-the-system-a-delegate-for-the-existing-memory-skills) ([793b90 Restructure 793a as a delegation of the memory skills](#793b90-restructure-793a-as-a-delegation-of-the-memory-skills)): the restructure retired `793a2a1c`, `793a2j1a`, `793a2j1d`, `793a2j1f`, `793a5g1b`.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a5g`, `793a5g1`, `793a5g1a`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b79 M5: a subagent dispatch prompt is a dispatch moment

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Finding:** `793a2i` applies prompt-time recall to every prompt, and a subagent's dispatch prompt is also the moment of `793a2c`, so the dispatch could be read as two moments.
- **Fix to be applied:** `793a2i` says the prompt that dispatches a subagent is the moment of `793a2c`, not a separate prompt moment. New scenario `793a2i1b` checks the log records one dispatch moment and no prompt moment for it.
- **Status:** applied in commit 52eab1f0 (fourth review, round 4 of change 793).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a2c`, `793a2i`, `793a2i1`, `793a2i1b`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b82 L3: the operator replay follows the When

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Finding:** `793a2a1c Only incidental items come back` put the operator's replay of the query in the Given, before the human submits the prompt.
- **Fix to be applied:** the operator reads the log after the When, and the Then asserts the delivery. See also [793b78 M4: replay scenarios compare with the log, and the log is checked separately](#793b78-m4-replay-scenarios-compare-with-the-log-and-the-log-is-checked-separately).

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Finding:** `793a2j Volume equals the depth's output` and `793a2j1b A vault holding more than 20 notes about the topic` cite the default "content budget", but under lazy queries a chunk carries no content to budget ([792a7r Chunk content budget](../../specs/product/memory-query.md#792a7r-chunk-content-budget), [792a7s Lazy chunks](../../specs/product/memory-query.md#792a7s-lazy-chunks)), and notes are never reduced.
- **Fix to be applied:** both say "default result limit" only.
- **Status:** applied in commit 52eab1f0 (fourth review, round 4 of change 793).

- **Status update:** superseded by [793c29 Is the System a delegate for the existing memory skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c29-is-the-system-a-delegate-for-the-existing-memory-skills) ([793b90 Restructure 793a as a delegation of the memory skills](#793b90-restructure-793a-as-a-delegation-of-the-memory-skills)): the restructure retired `793a2j`, `793a2j1b`.

- **Finding:** the Given of `793a3g1a A coverage write is made without the working agent` said "no existing note covers the cluster", which also holds in the near outcome, where an existing note addresses it.
- **Fix to be applied:** the Given reads "a cluster that no existing note addresses".

## 793b87 L8: no flag names or step numbers in the product spec

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Finding:** `793a2j Volume equals the depth's output` named `engram query --lazy-chunks`, and `793a3k` named "Step 2.7" of recall, which are implementation details a product spec should not carry.
- **Fix to be applied:** `793a2j Volume equals the depth's output` describes a chunk as a stub (id and score), as recall's lazy query returns it, and names "one lazy query" for each depth and for the recommendation check. `793a3k` says "recall's own activation step" and links [792a14l Only the notes that were used are activated](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14l-only-the-notes-that-were-used-are-activated).
- **Status:** applied in commit 52eab1f0 (fourth review, round 4 of change 793).
