- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a3k`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b88 L9: amend provenance is recorded per chunk-source entry

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Finding:** `793a3g Deep recall's writes are the System's` said "the chunk sources it adds ... MUST name the session and the turn", which does not say what carries the names.
- **Fix to be applied:** "each chunk-source entry the amend adds records the session id and the turn id that added it, the log entry names them, and the note's original source is unchanged". `793a3g1c A covered cluster gets evidence and its note is injected` and `793a3g1e A near cluster amends the note` say the same.
- **Status:** applied in commit 52eab1f0 (fourth review, round 4 of change 793).

- **Status update:** superseded by [793c29 Is the System a delegate for the existing memory skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c29-is-the-system-a-delegate-for-the-existing-memory-skills) ([793b90 Restructure 793a as a delegation of the memory skills](#793b90-restructure-793a-as-a-delegation-of-the-memory-skills)): the restructure retired `793a3g`, `793a3g1c`, `793a3g1e`.

## 793b89 Fourth review: findings waiting on open questions

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Note:** the review's original text is not kept in the repository. Each finding below is written from the question it raised, so its wording is the author's.
- **H4: when the covering and amended notes are injected.**
  - **Finding:** `793a3g Deep recall's writes are the System's` says capture performs the deep moment's writes and injects the covering and amended notes, but capture runs asynchronously at turn end ([793a3a Lessons are captured without the agent](793a_product-spec.md#793a3a-lessons-are-captured-without-the-agent), `793a3f Capture does not delay the session`) while the notes must reach the working agent. A finished subagent has no context left to receive them. The coverage judgment adds time that `793a5g`'s comparison does not include.
  - **Fix to be applied:** decide the injection time, then make `793a3g Deep recall's writes are the System's` and `793a5g` say it. With the recommendation (a): the notes are injected at the same moment, before the agent's next action, and the coverage judgment and writes are added to `793a5g`'s comparison.
  - **Waits on:** [793c25 When are the covering and amended notes from a deep moment injected?](793c_open-questions.md#793c25-when-are-the-covering-and-amended-notes-from-a-deep-moment-injected).
- **M2: the recommendation check has no plan to key on.**
  - **Finding:** [792a14o An emergent recommendation is checked against the vault](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14o-an-emergent-recommendation-is-checked-against-the-vault) fires on a recommendation not in the Step 0 plan. The System has no plan and ships no recommendation, yet `793a2j Volume equals the depth's output` and `793a5g` include the check.
  - **Fix to be applied:** decide what the check keys on. With the recommendation (b): drop the check at System moments and remove it from `793a2j Volume equals the depth's output` and `793a5g`.
  - **Waits on:** [793c26 What does the System's recommendation check (792a14o) key on?](793c_open-questions.md#793c26-what-does-the-systems-recommendation-check-792a14o-key-on).
- **M3: the chunk-index sweep is unspecified.**
  - **Finding:** [792a14b The index is swept once per session](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14b-the-index-is-swept-once-per-session) has recall sweep the index before its first query of a session, and recall's output depends on a current index. The product spec does not say whether the System sweeps, and a sweep takes time and writes to the index.
  - **Fix to be applied:** decide whether and when the System sweeps. With the recommendation (a): once per session before its first query, its time counted in `793a5g` and its index writes added to [793a4c Memory purview only](793a_product-spec.md#793a4c-memory-purview-only).
  - **Waits on:** [793c27 Does the System sweep the chunk index (792a14b)?](793c_open-questions.md#793c27-does-the-system-sweep-the-chunk-index-792a14b).
- **M6: the latency comparison is one noisy sample.**
  - **Finding:** `793a5g1a` and `793a5g1b An escalated moment is compared with both depths` compare one logged added latency with one timing of the instruction path's lookup. Both vary between runs, so a single pair cannot pass reliably.
  - **Fix to be applied:** decide how the comparison is measured. With the recommendation (a): median added latency against median lookup latency, over N repeats per moment kind.
  - **Waits on:** [793c28 How is 793a5g's latency comparison measured?](793c_open-questions.md#793c28-how-is-793a5gs-latency-comparison-measured).
- **Rationale:** an answer may change the fix, so applying them first would risk rework.
- **Status:** open; the questions are in [`793c_open-questions.md`](793c_open-questions.md).

- **Status update:** superseded in part by [793c29 Is the System a delegate for the existing memory skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c29-is-the-system-a-delegate-for-the-existing-memory-skills) ([793b90 Restructure 793a as a delegation of the memory skills](#793b90-restructure-793a-as-a-delegation-of-the-memory-skills)): the restructure retired `793a2j`, `793a3f`, `793a3g`, `793a5g1b`.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a5g`, `793a5g1`, `793a5g1a`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b90 Restructure 793a as a delegation of the memory skills

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c29 Is the System a delegate for the existing memory skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c29-is-the-system-a-delegate-for-the-existing-memory-skills), chose (a). The application below is Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.

- **Choice:** [Parallel Memory Agent - Product Spec](793a_product-spec.md#parallel-memory-agent---product-spec) now specifies the System as a delegate for [recall](../../specs/product/recall.md) and [learn](../../specs/product/learn.md). It performs them as their canonical specifications define, at the moments [ambient-guidance](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md) defines, on the working agent's behalf, and puts in front of the working agent only what the skill would have. The working agent no longer runs the skills. The document keeps the problem statement ([793a1 Problem Statement](793a_product-spec.md#793a1-problem-statement)), updated to describe the delegation, and then specifies only the delegation (`793a2a Recall is performed for the working agent`, [793a3a Lessons are captured without the agent](793a_product-spec.md#793a3a-lessons-are-captured-without-the-agent), `793a2k Intentional memory stays available and is never blocked`), the deliberate differences ([793c30 Which earlier decisions stay as deliberate differences from the skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c30-which-earlier-decisions-stay-as-deliberate-differences-from-the-skills)) and the new capabilities.
- **Choice:** the sections keep their numbers: [793a2 Added: automatic-memory (it surfaces)](793a_product-spec.md#793a2-added-automatic-memory-it-surfaces), [793a3 Added: automatic-memory (it captures)](793a_product-spec.md#793a3-added-automatic-memory-it-captures), [793a4 Added: automatic-memory (it keeps house, handles memory and not the task, and stays out of the way)](793a_product-spec.md#793a4-added-automatic-memory-it-keeps-house-handles-memory-and-not-the-task-and-stays-out-of-the-way) and [793a5 Added: automatic-memory (it is just there, and both channels stay)](793a_product-spec.md#793a5-added-automatic-memory-it-is-just-there-and-both-channels-stay) add to the one new capability ([793b1 One new capability, `automatic-memory`](#793b1-one-new-capability-automatic-memory)), and `793a6` is new.
- **Rationale:** Joe's intent is to offload the context, not to change what recall and learn do. Re-specifying their behavior produced the exceptions of [793c23 Is a note amended as near injected?](793c_open-questions.md#793c23-is-a-note-amended-as-near-injected) to [793c28 How is 793a5g's latency comparison measured?](793c_open-questions.md#793c28-how-is-793a5gs-latency-comparison-measured).
- **Alternatives:** (b) keep the structure and answer [793c25 When are the covering and amended notes from a deep moment injected?](793c_open-questions.md#793c25-when-are-the-covering-and-amended-notes-from-a-deep-moment-injected) to [793c28 How is 793a5g's latency comparison measured?](793c_open-questions.md#793c28-how-is-793a5gs-latency-comparison-measured) one by one. Not chosen.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a5g`, `793a6`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a2k`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b91 Which differences and new capabilities stay

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c30 Which earlier decisions stay as deliberate differences from the skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c30-which-earlier-decisions-stay-as-deliberate-differences-from-the-skills), decided what stays. The mapping to IDs is Author (agent), interpreting [793c30 Which earlier decisions stay as deliberate differences from the skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c30-which-earlier-decisions-stay-as-deliberate-differences-from-the-skills); awaiting owner validation.

| Item | Owner's answer | Where it lives |
| --- | --- | --- |
| D1 recall at every prompt regardless of source | keep | `793a2i`, in addition to the moments ambient-guidance defines |
| D2 never inject an item already in the receiving agent's context | keep | [793a2e Nothing already in context is delivered again](793a_product-spec.md#793a2e-nothing-already-in-context-is-delivered-again), with the definitions as they stood: per-agent context, compaction, chunk stubs, and a changed note is not present unless only its sources or recency changed |
| D3 capture from subagent sessions, and the LESSONS-line contract replaced | keep, and more | `793a3m` and the operations of `793a6` ([793b93 The LESSONS contract is modified, not removed](#793b93-the-lessons-contract-is-modified-not-removed)) |
| D4 the wider lesson definition | keep | `793a3n` and the Lesson term in [793a1 Problem Statement](793a_product-spec.md#793a1-problem-statement); #846 will align learn |
| D5 reinforcement as a MUST | drop | no requirement; `793a3c` is retired and learn's handling of an equivalent note applies |
| N1 moments noticed without the agent, including subagent dispatch | keep | `793a2c` |
| N2 no recall or capture in the System's own sessions | keep | `793a4f No delivery or capture in its own sessions` |
| N3 failures do not affect the working session | keep | [793a4b The session is never broken or stalled](793a_product-spec.md#793a4b-the-session-is-never-broken-or-stalled) |
| N4 `engram update` installs the Integration; uninstalling turns it off | keep | `793a4h Installed by engram update and active in Claude Code` and `793a4g`; #849 is named only in the rationale of `793a4g` and in [793b69 The uninstall command is a separate change](#793b69-the-uninstall-command-is-a-separate-change) |
| N5 the always-on measurement log | keep | `793a5e` |
| N6 capture rate above 5.6% | keep | `793a3h` |
| N7 the precision grader and the activation grader | keep | `793a3i` and `793a3l` |
| N8 injected items name their source | keep | `793a2d Delivered memory is attributable` |
| Inherent: use is inferred from the transcript | stays | `793a3k` |
| Inherent: the working agent no longer runs recall or learn | stays | `793a2k Intentional memory stays available and is never blocked` (new ID; Author, interpreting [793c30 Which earlier decisions stay as deliberate differences from the skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c30-which-earlier-decisions-stay-as-deliberate-differences-from-the-skills) and [793c29 Is the System a delegate for the existing memory skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c29-is-the-system-a-delegate-for-the-existing-memory-skills)) |
| Kept as they stand: the System does not make or override decisions, and its effects outside the session | stays | [793a4c Memory purview only](793a_product-spec.md#793a4c-memory-purview-only) |

- **Choice:** [793a4c Memory purview only](793a_product-spec.md#793a4c-memory-purview-only) is kept as it stood, except that a link to a retired requirement became a link to the canonical parent-sync requirement, and the chunk index was added to the effects outside the session, because the System sweeps it (`793a2l Indexing and curation happen without the agent`). Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Rationale:** each row follows the owner's answer; the rest is bookkeeping.
- **Alternatives:** for each of D1 to D5, drop instead of keep; the owner dropped D5 only. Any N item could have been dropped; none was ([793c30 Which earlier decisions stay as deliberate differences from the skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c30-which-earlier-decisions-stay-as-deliberate-differences-from-the-skills)).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites 11 IDs, each in the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction), which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a2d`, `793a2k`, `793a2l`, `793a4f`, `793a4h`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b92 Requirements that re-specified skill behavior are retired

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c29 Is the System a delegate for the existing memory skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c29-is-the-system-a-delegate-for-the-existing-memory-skills), retired [793c25 When are the covering and amended notes from a deep moment injected?](793c_open-questions.md#793c25-when-are-the-covering-and-amended-notes-from-a-deep-moment-injected), [793c26 What does the System's recommendation check (792a14o) key on?](793c_open-questions.md#793c26-what-does-the-systems-recommendation-check-792a14o-key-on) and [793c28 How is 793a5g's latency comparison measured?](793c_open-questions.md#793c28-how-is-793a5gs-latency-comparison-measured) and named the re-specified behavior as retired by delegation; Author (agent) applied it. Which requirement counts as re-specified is Author's reading; awaiting owner validation.

- **Choice:** retired, because the skill's own specification governs: depth choice and its triggers (`793a2h`: [792a14c Recall has two depths](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14c-recall-has-two-depths), [792a14d Glance escalates to deep for a recent standard](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14d-glance-escalates-to-deep-for-a-recent-standard), [792a19b Recall escalates from glance to deep](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19b-recall-escalates-from-glance-to-deep)); volume equals the depth's output (`793a2j`: [792a7b The payload](../../specs/product/memory-query.md#792a7b-the-payload), [792a14f One unified query](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14f-one-unified-query)); the coverage-write branches, the persisted conclusion and the parent-note rule (`793a3g`: [792a14j Coverage is judged by the agent and written once per cluster](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14j-coverage-is-judged-by-the-agent-and-written-once-per-cluster), [792a14k A note from the parent vault is activated, never amended](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14k-a-note-from-the-parent-vault-is-activated-never-amended), [792a14p A sound conclusion is persisted as a derived note](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14p-a-sound-conclusion-is-persisted-as-a-derived-note)); the recommendation check ([792a14o An emergent recommendation is checked against the vault](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14o-an-emergent-recommendation-is-checked-against-the-vault)); the latency formulas (`793a5g`, replaced by `793a5g`); sweep timing ([792a14b The index is swept once per session](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14b-the-index-is-swept-once-per-session), [792a15b The sweep comes first](../../specs/product/learn.md#792a15b-the-sweep-comes-first); the difference is `793a2l Indexing and curation happen without the agent`); confirmed lessons only (`793a3b`: [792a15e The four kinds of explicit lesson](../../specs/product/learn.md#792a15e-the-four-kinds-of-explicit-lesson), [792a19d The capture is learn's fast path](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19d-the-capture-is-learns-fast-path)); no duplicates and reinforcement (`793a3c`; D5 dropped); direct writes to the local vault (`793a3j`: [792a21n A local write is offered to the parent](../../specs/product/parent-sync.md#792a21n-a-local-write-is-offered-to-the-parent)); recall after each tool result (`793a2b`: not a moment the guidance defines); independence from agent compliance and coexistence (`793a5a`, `793a5b`: covered by `793a2k Intentional memory stays available and is never blocked` and [793a2e Nothing already in context is delivered again](793a_product-spec.md#793a2e-nothing-already-in-context-is-delivered-again)).
- **Choice:** the earlier owner decisions about the deep moment's writes ([793c16 At a deep moment, does the System's coverage write activate the note it covers?](793c_open-questions.md#793c16-at-a-deep-moment-does-the-systems-coverage-write-activate-the-note-it-covers), [793c23 Is a note amended as near injected?](793c_open-questions.md#793c23-is-a-note-amended-as-near-injected), [793c24 Does the covering note's injection add anything to the depth's output?](793c_open-questions.md#793c24-does-the-covering-notes-injection-add-anything-to-the-depths-output): attach evidence, inject the covering and the amended note, never activate on a coverage write) are not in the [793c30 Which earlier decisions stay as deliberate differences from the skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c30-which-earlier-decisions-stay-as-deliberate-differences-from-the-skills) lists, so they are treated as retired by delegation: what the skill puts in front of its agent after those writes is what the System puts in front of the working agent (`793a2a Recall is performed for the working agent`). Author (agent), interpreting [793c29 Is the System a delegate for the existing memory skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c29-is-the-system-a-delegate-for-the-existing-memory-skills) and [793c30 Which earlier decisions stay as deliberate differences from the skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c30-which-earlier-decisions-stay-as-deliberate-differences-from-the-skills); awaiting owner validation ([793c33 Do the requirements outside the D1-D5 and N1-N8 lists stay?](793c_open-questions.md#793c33-do-the-requirements-outside-the-d1-d5-and-n1-n8-lists-stay)).
- **Rationale:** a requirement that restates a skill's behavior goes stale when the skill changes, and the exceptions it produced are what [793c29 Is the System a delegate for the existing memory skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c29-is-the-system-a-delegate-for-the-existing-memory-skills) asked to remove.
- **Alternatives:** keep the re-specified requirements and answer [793c25 When are the covering and amended notes from a deep moment injected?](793c_open-questions.md#793c25-when-are-the-covering-and-amended-notes-from-a-deep-moment-injected) to [793c28 How is 793a5g's latency comparison measured?](793c_open-questions.md#793c28-how-is-793a5gs-latency-comparison-measured) one by one ([793c29 Is the System a delegate for the existing memory skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c29-is-the-system-a-delegate-for-the-existing-memory-skills), option b). Not chosen by the owner.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a5g`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a2k`, `793a2l`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b93 The LESSONS contract is modified, not removed

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c30 Which earlier decisions stay as deliberate differences from the skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c30-which-earlier-decisions-stay-as-deliberate-differences-from-the-skills), decided that the LESSONS-line contract is replaced, not supplemented, while the Integration is installed. The choice between Modified and Removed operations is Author (agent), interpreting [793c30 Which earlier decisions stay as deliberate differences from the skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c30-which-earlier-decisions-stay-as-deliberate-differences-from-the-skills) D3; awaiting owner validation.

- **Choice:** `793a6` holds Modified operations, each against exactly one canonical node and conditional on the Integration. The contract still holds in harnesses that cannot run the System and on an install not yet updated ([793a5c Other harnesses are unchanged](793a_product-spec.md#793a5c-other-harnesses-are-unchanged)), so a Removed operation would delete a contract that is still in force there. The replacement text is the Integration branch followed by the target's text unchanged.
- **Choice:** the operations are `793a6a` ([792a16a A completion report ends with a LESSONS line](../../specs/product/lessons-contract.md#792a16a-a-completion-report-ends-with-a-lessons-line)), `793a6b` ([792a16b Every dispatch is told to report lessons](../../specs/product/lessons-contract.md#792a16b-every-dispatch-is-told-to-report-lessons)), `793a6c` ([792a16c A missing line is asked for once](../../specs/product/lessons-contract.md#792a16c-a-missing-line-is-asked-for-once)), `793a6d` ([792a16d Every line is recorded as it returns](../../specs/product/lessons-contract.md#792a16d-every-line-is-recorded-as-it-returns)), `793a6e` ([792a16e Each lesson is judged when it returns](../../specs/product/lessons-contract.md#792a16e-each-lesson-is-judged-when-it-returns)), `793a6f` ([792a16f The closing learn gets the whole list](../../specs/product/lessons-contract.md#792a16f-the-closing-learn-gets-the-whole-list)), `793a6g` ([792a16g A confirmed approach is looked for at the moment of confirmation](../../specs/product/lessons-contract.md#792a16g-a-confirmed-approach-is-looked-for-at-the-moment-of-confirmation)), `793a6h` ([792a17g Every dispatch hands over an exact spec](../../specs/product/route.md#792a17g-every-dispatch-hands-over-an-exact-spec)), `793a6i` ([792a18r The closing learn gets the lessons and the audit](../../specs/product/please.md#792a18r-the-closing-learn-gets-the-lessons-and-the-audit)), `793a6j` ([792a19e A returned subagent lesson is captured before the next dispatch](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19e-a-returned-subagent-lesson-is-captured-before-the-next-dispatch)) and `793a6k` ([792a19f A returned line that fails the bar is not captured](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19f-a-returned-line-that-fails-the-bar-is-not-captured)).
- **Choice:** other canonical capabilities checked for the LESSONS line: route (one node, [792a17g Every dispatch hands over an exact spec](../../specs/product/route.md#792a17g-every-dispatch-hands-over-an-exact-spec)), please (one node, [792a18r The closing learn gets the lessons and the audit](../../specs/product/please.md#792a18r-the-closing-learn-gets-the-lessons-and-the-audit); the audit of [792a18p The closing learn is preceded by a lessons audit](../../specs/product/please.md#792a18p-the-closing-learn-is-preceded-by-a-lessons-audit) is unaffected) and ambient-guidance (two nodes). Learn has no normative node that names the LESSONS line: only its introduction and the rationale of [792a15c A correction moment gets a single-note capture](../../specs/product/learn.md#792a15c-a-correction-moment-gets-a-single-note-capture) mention the contract, so there is no operation against learn. At promotion those two sentences need to say the contract applies without the Integration.
- **Choice:** nodes that ask the agent to run recall or learn at all (for example [792a19a Recall fires at four decision moments](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19a-recall-fires-at-four-decision-moments), [792a19c Learn fires at the correction moment](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19c-learn-fires-at-the-correction-moment), [792a17h Every subagent recalls first, and large units are decomposed](../../specs/product/route.md#792a17h-every-subagent-recalls-first-and-large-units-are-decomposed)) are not changed here. See [793c31 Which canonical nodes that ask the agent to run recall or learn change while the Integration is installed?](793c_open-questions.md#793c31-which-canonical-nodes-that-ask-the-agent-to-run-recall-or-learn-change-while-the-integration-is-installed).
- **Implementation note:** the skill and guidance text that carry the contract change at implementation: `agent-instructions/skills/route/SKILL.md`, `agent-instructions/skills/please/SKILL.md`, `agent-instructions/skills/learn/SKILL.md` and `agent-instructions/guidance/learn.md`. Each is a SKILL.md or guidance edit and needs the writing-skills cycle (RED, GREEN, REFACTOR, pressure tests).
- **Rationale:** the System captures subagent lessons from their sessions, so a line the subagent offers would repeat them and the orchestrator's collecting and judging would duplicate the System's work.
- **Alternatives:** Removed operations for all eleven nodes, with the System's contract added separately. Not chosen, for the reason above.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites 12 IDs, each in the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction), which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b94 No added delay, and how it is measured

- **Decided by:** Author (agent), interpreting [793c29 Is the System a delegate for the existing memory skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c29-is-the-system-a-delegate-for-the-existing-memory-skills) and the owner's follow-up on [793c28 How is 793a5g's latency comparison measured?](793c_open-questions.md#793c28-how-is-793a5gs-latency-comparison-measured) ("what is being measured?"); awaiting owner validation. Owner (Joe) retired [793c28 How is 793a5g's latency comparison measured?](793c_open-questions.md#793c28-how-is-793a5gs-latency-comparison-measured) and asked to keep one simple non-blocking requirement only if it is genuinely new.

- **Choice:** `793a5g` reads "The System's work MUST NOT add delay to the working agent beyond what the agent would spend performing the same skill itself." What is measured is the time the working agent waits on the System (before it receives a moment's recall, or before its next prompt is processed), recorded as the latency added in `793a5e` and compared with the time the same recall takes when the agent performs it inline (`793a5g1a`). Work the agent does not wait on, such as capture, grading and the sweep, adds none (`793a5g1c`).
- **Choice:** the requirement reuses `793a5g`'s ID because its meaning, a bound on the latency the System adds, survives. `793a3f` (capture does not delay the session) is retired into it. `793a5g1b` (the escalated moment compared with both depths) is retired because depth choice is recall's.
- **Rationale:** the skill's own specification fixes what the work is, so the only new promise is that the delegation costs the working agent no more time than doing the work.
- **Alternatives:** keep the full formula (generating phrases, running the queries, reading the results); drop the requirement. Not chosen. The noise of comparing two timings is [793c35 How is the delay comparison in 793a5g1a made given timing noise?](793c_open-questions.md#793c35-how-is-the-delay-comparison-in-793a5g1a-made-given-timing-noise).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a5e`, `793a5g`, `793a5g1`, `793a5g1a`, `793a5g1c`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b95 The sweep runs after the turn

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c27 Does the System sweep the chunk index (792a14b)?](793c_open-questions.md#793c27-does-the-system-sweep-the-chunk-index-792a14b) (c): sweep asynchronously at turn-end capture. Kept as `793a2l Indexing and curation happen without the agent` by Author (agent) under the instruction to keep it only if it differs from recall's own sweep; awaiting owner validation.

- **Choice:** it differs. [792a14b The index is swept once per session](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14b-the-index-is-swept-once-per-session) sweeps once per session before recall's first query, and [792a15b The sweep comes first](../../specs/product/learn.md#792a15b-the-sweep-comes-first) sweeps first in learn. The System runs neither, and sweeps after the turn without making the working agent wait.
- **Consequence:** the first moment of a session may query an index that lacks the previous turn, where the skill would have sweeped first. This is the cost of not delaying the working agent.
- **Rationale:** a sweep is slow and the working agent need not wait for it.
- **Alternatives:** sweep once per session before the first query, as recall does, or never sweep ([793c27 Does the System sweep the chunk index (792a14b)?](793c_open-questions.md#793c27-does-the-system-sweep-the-chunk-index-792a14b), options a and b). Not chosen by the owner.

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a2l`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b96 Other interpretations made in the restructure

- **Decided by:** Author (agent), interpreting [793c29 Is the System a delegate for the existing memory skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c29-is-the-system-a-delegate-for-the-existing-memory-skills) and [793c30 Which earlier decisions stay as deliberate differences from the skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c30-which-earlier-decisions-stay-as-deliberate-differences-from-the-skills); awaiting owner validation.

- **Choice:** learn's closing learn has no counterpart for the System, so [793a3a Lessons are captured without the agent](793a_product-spec.md#793a3a-lessons-are-captured-without-the-agent) runs learn "in place of the closing learn, after each completed turn", following the draft's turn-end evaluation ([793c34 What stands in for learn's closing learn?](793c_open-questions.md#793c34-what-stands-in-for-learns-closing-learn)).
- **Choice:** the draft's "evaluate every completed turn" is no longer a requirement of its own; [793a3a Lessons are captured without the agent](793a_product-spec.md#793a3a-lessons-are-captured-without-the-agent) keeps [793a3a Lessons are captured without the agent](793a_product-spec.md#793a3a-lessons-are-captured-without-the-agent)'s ID because capturing without the agent survives, and the subagent scenarios moved to `793a3m` and `793a3n` with new IDs.
- **Choice:** `793a2c` keeps its ID for noticing moments, dispatch included, because the dispatch moment survives; the requirement for performing recall kept its ID `793a2a` for the same reason (recall at a prompt survives) until the sixth review replaced it with [793a2n Relevant memory reaches the agent at the moments](793a_product-spec.md#793a2n-relevant-memory-reaches-the-agent-at-the-moments) ([793b160 L3: three nodes get new IDs](#793b160-l3-three-nodes-get-new-ids)).
- **Choice:** `793a3k` keeps `793a3k`'s ID. Its chunk, parent-note and recall's-own-step scenarios are retired because recall's own specification covers the chunk and parent-note rules, and the requirement says the System's inference replaces recall's own activation.
- **Choice:** `793a5e` describes the capture decision as "what learn did with the lesson, or that it was discarded", because D5 dropped the System's own "reinforced" outcome.
- **Choice:** `793a2k Intentional memory stays available and is never blocked` and `793a2l Indexing and curation happen without the agent` are new. `793a3d`, `793a4d`, [793a5c Other harnesses are unchanged](793a_product-spec.md#793a5c-other-harnesses-are-unchanged) and `793a5f` stay as they stood and are open to confirmation in [793c33 Do the requirements outside the D1-D5 and N1-N8 lists stay?](793c_open-questions.md#793c33-do-the-requirements-outside-the-d1-d5-and-n1-n8-lists-stay). Concurrent duplicate captures are kept in `793a3m` ([793c32 Does the System keep a rule against concurrent duplicate captures?](793c_open-questions.md#793c32-does-the-system-keep-a-rule-against-concurrent-duplicate-captures)).
- **Alternatives:** end of session or end of each unit of work as the stand-in for the closing learn ([793c34 What stands in for learn's closing learn?](793c_open-questions.md#793c34-what-stands-in-for-learns-closing-learn), options b and c); keep the draft's turn evaluation as a requirement of its own; give the restructured requirements fresh IDs. Not chosen.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites 8 IDs, each in the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction), which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b97 ID map for the restructure

- **Decided by:** Author (agent), interpreting [793c29 Is the System a delegate for the existing memory skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c29-is-the-system-a-delegate-for-the-existing-memory-skills) and [793c30 Which earlier decisions stay as deliberate differences from the skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c30-which-earlier-decisions-stay-as-deliberate-differences-from-the-skills); awaiting owner validation.

Retired in the restructure: 10 requirements and 38 scenarios, 48 IDs in all. Retired IDs are never reused. IDs retired earlier ([793b14 ID handling](#793b14-id-handling)) stay retired.

| Retired | Replacement | Why |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `793a2b`, `793a2b1a`, `793a2b1b` | none | recall fires at the moments ambient-guidance defines, and after every tool result is not one; the prompt moments are `793a2i` |
| `793a2h`, `793a2h1a`..`793a2h1e` | [792a14c Recall has two depths](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14c-recall-has-two-depths), [792a19b Recall escalates from glance to deep](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19b-recall-escalates-from-glance-to-deep), [792a14d Glance escalates to deep for a recent standard](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14d-glance-escalates-to-deep-for-a-recent-standard) | depth choice and its triggers are recall's; the log keeps the depth and the escalation (`793a5e`, `793a5e1f`) |
| `793a2j`, `793a2j1a`..`793a2j1f` | `793a2a Recall is performed for the working agent`; [792a7b The payload](../../specs/product/memory-query.md#792a7b-the-payload), [792a14f One unified query](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14f-one-unified-query) | volume equals what the skill would put in front of the agent; the skill's payload is memory-query's |
| `793a2a1c` | none | the incidental items are part of the query's payload ([792a7b The payload](../../specs/product/memory-query.md#792a7b-the-payload)) |
| `793a3a1c` | `793a3n1a` | the wider lesson definition (D4) has its own requirement |
| `793a3a1d`, `793a3a1e` | `793a3m1a`, `793a3m1b` | capture from subagent sessions (D3) has its own requirement |
| `793a3b`, `793a3b1a`..`793a3b1c` | [792a15e The four kinds of explicit lesson](../../specs/product/learn.md#792a15e-the-four-kinds-of-explicit-lesson), [792a15a Learn writes only explicit lessons](../../specs/product/learn.md#792a15a-learn-writes-only-explicit-lessons), [792a19d The capture is learn's fast path](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19d-the-capture-is-learns-fast-path) | what is worth saving is learn's judgment |
| `793a3c`, `793a3c1a` | none | D5 dropped: learn's handling of an equivalent note applies |
| `793a3c1b` | `793a3m1c` | the parent no longer collects the subagent's lessons |
| `793a3c1c` | `793a3m1d` | concurrent duplicates are kept under D3 ([793c32 Does the System keep a rule against concurrent duplicate captures?](793c_open-questions.md#793c32-does-the-system-keep-a-rule-against-concurrent-duplicate-captures)) |
| `793a3f`, `793a3f1a` | `793a5g`, `793a5g1c` | folded into the one non-blocking requirement |
| `793a3g`, `793a3g1a` | `793a2a Recall is performed for the working agent`, `793a2a1d Recall's vault writes happen without the agent` | recall's deep writes are recall's own; one scenario shows they happen without the agent |
| `793a3g1b`..`793a3g1f` | [792a14j Coverage is judged by the agent and written once per cluster](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14j-coverage-is-judged-by-the-agent-and-written-once-per-cluster), [792a14k A note from the parent vault is activated, never amended](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14k-a-note-from-the-parent-vault-is-activated-never-amended), [792a14p A sound conclusion is persisted as a derived note](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14p-a-sound-conclusion-is-persisted-as-a-derived-note) | the coverage, parent-note and persisted-conclusion branches are recall's own |
| `793a3j`, `793a3j1a`, `793a3j1b` | [792a21n A local write is offered to the parent](../../specs/product/parent-sync.md#792a21n-a-local-write-is-offered-to-the-parent) | the System writes as learn and recall write |
| `793a3k1c`..`793a3k1e` | [792a14l Only the notes that were used are activated](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14l-only-the-notes-that-were-used-are-activated) | recall's own rules on chunks and parent notes; `793a3k` says the inference replaces recall's own activation |
| `793a5a`, `793a5a1a` | `793a2k Intentional memory stays available and is never blocked`, `793a2a1a Recall happens without the agent running it` | a bare configuration is the condition of the delegation scenarios |
| `793a5b`, `793a5b1a` | `793a2k Intentional memory stays available and is never blocked`, [793a2e Nothing already in context is delivered again](793a_product-spec.md#793a2e-nothing-already-in-context-is-delivered-again) | the agent does not run the skills, and nothing already in its context is injected ([793c31 Which canonical nodes that ask the agent to run recall or learn change while the Integration is installed?](793c_open-questions.md#793c31-which-canonical-nodes-that-ask-the-agent-to-run-recall-or-learn-change-while-the-integration-is-installed)) |
| `793a5g1b` | `793a5g1a` | depth choice is recall's; the one comparison covers each moment kind |

- **Choice:** new IDs: `793a2k Intentional memory stays available and is never blocked`, `793a2l Indexing and curation happen without the agent`, `793a3m`, `793a3n`; scenarios `793a2a1d Recall's vault writes happen without the agent`, `793a2c1b`, `793a2c1c`, `793a2k1a The agent is neither required nor asked to run a memory command`, `793a2l1a The index catches up after the turn`, `793a2l1b`, `793a3m1a`, `793a3m1b`, `793a3m1c`, `793a3m1d`, `793a3n1a`, `793a3n1b`, `793a5g1c`; and the operations `793a6` (`793a6a`, `793a6b`, `793a6c`, `793a6d`, `793a6e`, `793a6f`, `793a6g`, `793a6h`, `793a6i`, `793a6j`, `793a6k`) with one scenario each.
- **Choice:** kept with their IDs and modified: `793a2a Recall is performed for the working agent`, `793a2c`, [793a3a Lessons are captured without the agent](793a_product-spec.md#793a3a-lessons-are-captured-without-the-agent), `793a3k`, `793a4f No delivery or capture in its own sessions`, `793a5g` (retitled), and in place without retitling `793a2d Delivered memory is attributable`, [793a2e Nothing already in context is delivered again](793a_product-spec.md#793a2e-nothing-already-in-context-is-delivered-again), `793a2i`, `793a3d`, `793a3h`, `793a3i`, `793a3l`, [793a4b The session is never broken or stalled](793a_product-spec.md#793a4b-the-session-is-never-broken-or-stalled), [793a4c Memory purview only](793a_product-spec.md#793a4c-memory-purview-only), `793a4d`, `793a4g`, `793a4h Installed by engram update and active in Claude Code`, [793a5c Other harnesses are unchanged](793a_product-spec.md#793a5c-other-harnesses-are-unchanged), `793a5e`, `793a5f`. Scenarios retitled in place: `793a2a1a Recall happens without the agent running it`, `793a4f1a No note records the System's own work`, `793a4f1b A session of its own receives no delivery`, `793a5g1a`.
- **Rationale:** the lifecycle guide keeps a node's ID while its meaning survives and gives a materially new node a new ID.
- **Status update:** the scenario that the restructure called `793a2a1b` has the ID `793a2a1d Recall's vault writes happen without the agent`, because `793a2a1b` was used in the draft for another scenario ([793b125 M9: a reused scenario ID gets a fresh one](#793b125-m9-a-reused-scenario-id-gets-a-fresh-one)).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites 43 IDs, each in the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction), which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites 9 IDs, each in the ID map, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b98 Earlier entries made moot by the restructure

- **Decided by:** Author (agent), applying [793c29 Is the System a delegate for the existing memory skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c29-is-the-system-a-delegate-for-the-existing-memory-skills); awaiting owner validation.

- **Choice:** every earlier entry that names a requirement or scenario retired by the restructure now carries a status line. 19 entries name only IDs that are no longer live and are marked superseded; 31 also name live IDs and are marked superseded in part. The decisions in them are history of how the retired requirements came to be; they do not bind the restructured specification, and the owner decisions among them survive only as [793c30 Which earlier decisions stay as deliberate differences from the skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c30-which-earlier-decisions-stay-as-deliberate-differences-from-the-skills) says.
- **Rationale:** the log keeps its entries, so that the owner can see what was decided, and says which no longer govern.

## 793b99 Conditional operations against every node that tells the agent to run recall or learn

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c31 Which canonical nodes that ask the agent to run recall or learn change while the Integration is installed?](793c_open-questions.md#793c31-which-canonical-nodes-that-ask-the-agent-to-run-recall-or-learn-change-while-the-integration-is-installed), chose (a): conditional Modified operations, effective while the Integration is installed, against every canonical node that tells the working agent to run recall or learn.

- **Choice:** `793a7` holds one Modified operation for each node that does so, `793a7a` to `793a7p`. Each preserves the target's ID, carries the Integration branch first and the target's text unchanged for an install without the Integration, and has scenarios for both conditions.
- **Rationale:** without the change a harness that imports the guidance still tells the agent to do what the System now does, which contradicts `793a2k Intentional memory stays available and is never blocked`.
- **Alternatives:** (b) do only the LESSONS nodes and decide the rest later; (c) change nothing canonical. Not chosen by the owner.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a7`, `793a7a`, `793a7p`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a2k`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b100 The operations are a new section, 793a7

- **Decided by:** Author (agent), interpreting [793c31 Which canonical nodes that ask the agent to run recall or learn change while the Integration is installed?](793c_open-questions.md#793c31-which-canonical-nodes-that-ask-the-agent-to-run-recall-or-learn-change-while-the-integration-is-installed); awaiting owner validation.

- **Choice:** the new operations are the new section `793a7` and not extensions of `793a6`. `793a6` is about the LESSONS line and is titled so; its four nodes that also name the recall-first instruction or the closing learn are edited in place ([793b102 Consequent edits to the LESSONS operations](#793b102-consequent-edits-to-the-lessons-operations)), and each target keeps exactly one operation.
- **Rationale:** the two sections have different subjects and different canonical targets, and the lifecycle guide allows one operation per target.
- **Alternatives:** extend `793a6`. Not chosen: it would make a section about the LESSONS contract hold unrelated nodes.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a6`, `793a7`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

Each row is a judgment about whether the node tells the working agent to run recall or learn, or to perform their steps. The test applied: a node is changed when it tells the agent to run either, or to do a step of either, or lists them among what the agent does; a node is left when it describes what a skill does once run (the System runs those procedures), what a binary does, or how the agent treats something it already has.

| [792a18n Findings are argued to resolution](../../specs/product/please.md#792a18n-findings-are-argued-to-resolution) | changed (`793a7n`) | each reviewer recalls first |
| [792a18p The closing learn is preceded by a lessons audit](../../specs/product/please.md#792a18p-the-closing-learn-is-preceded-by-a-lessons-audit), [792a18q A lesson about to be captured asks what should have surfaced it](../../specs/product/please.md#792a18q-a-lesson-about-to-be-captured-asks-what-should-have-surfaced-it) | changed (`793a7o`, `793a7p`) | the audit and the surfacing question are steps of capturing a lesson, which the System performs (`793a3o`) |
| [792a18r The closing learn gets the lessons and the audit](../../specs/product/please.md#792a18r-the-closing-learn-gets-the-lessons-and-the-audit) | already modified (`793a6i`), edited ([793b102 Consequent edits to the LESSONS operations](#793b102-consequent-edits-to-the-lessons-operations)) | the closing learn |
| [792a16a A completion report ends with a LESSONS line](../../specs/product/lessons-contract.md#792a16a-a-completion-report-ends-with-a-lessons-line) to [792a16g A confirmed approach is looked for at the moment of confirmation](../../specs/product/lessons-contract.md#792a16g-a-confirmed-approach-is-looked-for-at-the-moment-of-confirmation) | already modified (`793a6a` to `793a6g`) | the LESSONS contract; [792a16b Every dispatch is told to report lessons](../../specs/product/lessons-contract.md#792a16b-every-dispatch-is-told-to-report-lessons) and [792a16f The closing learn gets the whole list](../../specs/product/lessons-contract.md#792a16f-the-closing-learn-gets-the-whole-list) edited ([793b102 Consequent edits to the LESSONS operations](#793b102-consequent-edits-to-the-lessons-operations)) |
| learn ([792a15a Learn writes only explicit lessons](../../specs/product/learn.md#792a15a-learn-writes-only-explicit-lessons) to [792a15l The re-parent batch mode](../../specs/product/learn.md#792a15l-the-re-parent-batch-mode)) | left | learn's own procedure: what to write, the sweep, the vocabulary check, placement; the System runs it. [792a15l The re-parent batch mode](../../specs/product/learn.md#792a15l-the-re-parent-batch-mode) is the user-directed re-parent batch |
| recall ([792a14a The plan is stated before anything is retrieved](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14a-the-plan-is-stated-before-anything-is-retrieved) to [792a14q A cited synthesis is also captured as a question and answer](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14q-a-cited-synthesis-is-also-captured-as-a-question-and-answer)) | left | recall's own procedure: the plan, the query, the reading, the synthesis, the writes; the System runs it |
| write-memory ([792a13a The worker runs only on a handoff and never re-judges it](../../specs/product/write-memory.md#792a13a-the-worker-runs-only-on-a-handoff-and-never-re-judges-it) to [792a13n The write is verified and reported](../../specs/product/write-memory.md#792a13n-the-write-is-verified-and-reported)), curate ([792a22a Pending offers are left out of normal query results](../../specs/product/curate.md#792a22a-pending-offers-are-left-out-of-normal-query-results) to [792a22o Pending offers are surfaced at three points](../../specs/product/curate.md#792a22o-pending-offers-are-surfaced-at-three-points)) | left | skill-to-skill hand-offs and the review of offers; [792a22o Pending offers are surfaced at three points](../../specs/product/curate.md#792a22o-pending-offers-are-surfaced-at-three-points) is the payload flag of `engram query` |
| vault-migration ([792a10c Candidates to judge](../../specs/product/vault-migration.md#792a10c-candidates-to-judge)), install-and-update, harness-deploy-sync, agent-instruction-bundle | left | a user-directed migration notice that names learn's batch mode, the deploy of the skills and guidance files, and the authoring rules for skills; none is a moment at which recall or learn fires |
| memory-query, parent-sync, note-writing and the other binary specifications; the eval harness and loop auditor | left | what the binary does, or measurement; they tell no agent to run recall or learn |

- **Rationale:** the System runs the skills' own procedures (`793a2a Recall is performed for the working agent`, [793a3a Lessons are captured without the agent](793a_product-spec.md#793a3a-lessons-are-captured-without-the-agent)), so a node that only describes a procedure still holds as written.
- **Alternatives:** change every node that mentions recall or learn. Not chosen: it would rewrite the procedures that the System still follows.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites 25 IDs, each in the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction), which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b102 Consequent edits to the LESSONS operations

- **Decided by:** Author (agent), interpreting [793c31 Which canonical nodes that ask the agent to run recall or learn change while the Integration is installed?](793c_open-questions.md#793c31-which-canonical-nodes-that-ask-the-agent-to-run-recall-or-learn-change-while-the-integration-is-installed); awaiting owner validation.

- **Choice:** four operations of `793a6` are edited, because their Integration branches said things that the new operations make untrue. `793a6b` and `793a6h` now also drop the recall-first instruction from the handoff, which `793a7j` removes. `793a6f` and `793a6i` no longer have please hand the audit's map to a closing learn, because there is none (`793a7l`); the audit's result is in the closing report (`793a7o`).
- **Rationale:** each target has one operation, and two operations may not give one node two replacement texts.
- **Alternatives:** leave `793a6` as it was and let the two sections disagree. Rejected: the replacement text of a node must be one text.
- **Status update:** the audit's result is no longer in the closing report; it is the System's (`793a3o`), as [793b108 Interpretations made in applying 793c37](#793b108-interpretations-made-in-applying-793c37) records.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites 9 IDs, each in the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction), which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b103 Other judgments in the Integration branches

- **Decided by:** Author (agent), interpreting [793c31 Which canonical nodes that ask the agent to run recall or learn change while the Integration is installed?](793c_open-questions.md#793c31-which-canonical-nodes-that-ask-the-agent-to-run-recall-or-learn-change-while-the-integration-is-installed); awaiting owner validation.

- **Choice:**
  - Where a target also fixes when recall or learn fires or at which depth ([792a19a Recall fires at four decision moments](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19a-recall-fires-at-four-decision-moments), [792a19b Recall escalates from glance to deep](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19b-recall-escalates-from-glance-to-deep), [792a19c Learn fires at the correction moment](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19c-learn-fires-at-the-correction-moment), [792a19d The capture is learn's fast path](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19d-the-capture-is-learns-fast-path)), the Integration branch restates the moments and criteria and says the System performs them, because `793a2a Recall is performed for the working agent` and [793a3a Lessons are captured without the agent](793a_product-spec.md#793a3a-lessons-are-captured-without-the-agent) refer to those nodes for them.
  - The shim still tells the agent to use a matched runbook's deep procedure when the decision is weighty (`793a7g`), because that is the runbook's procedure and not recall.
  - Please keeps its audit, and reports an unmapped item instead of handing it to a learn (`793a7o`). It drops the which-artifact-should-have-surfaced-it question, the covering-note search and the reword (`793a7p`), and keeps the rule that a repository change is never filed automatically. These are [793c37 What is the shape of please's workflow while the Integration is installed?](793c_open-questions.md#793c37-what-is-the-shape-of-pleases-workflow-while-the-integration-is-installed).
  - The orchestrator uses only the tier evidence the System has already delivered (`793a7i`); this is [793c39 Does tier evidence reach the orchestrator before it routes?](793c_open-questions.md#793c39-does-tier-evidence-reach-the-orchestrator-before-it-routes).
  - How a harness's static guidance text becomes conditional on the Integration is a technical design question, not specified here. The product behavior is that, while the Integration is installed, the agent is not told to run recall or learn.
  - [793c32 Does the System keep a rule against concurrent duplicate captures?](793c_open-questions.md#793c32-does-the-system-keep-a-rule-against-concurrent-duplicate-captures) to [793c35 How is the delay comparison in 793a5g1a made given timing noise?](793c_open-questions.md#793c35-how-is-the-delay-comparison-in-793a5g1a-made-given-timing-noise) were answered by accepting the specification as written. Verified: `793a3m1` holds the concurrent-duplicate scenario, [793a3a Lessons are captured without the agent](793a_product-spec.md#793a3a-lessons-are-captured-without-the-agent) captures after each completed turn, the requirements of [793c33 Do the requirements outside the D1-D5 and N1-N8 lists stay?](793c_open-questions.md#793c33-do-the-requirements-outside-the-d1-d5-and-n1-n8-lists-stay) are in place, and `793a5g1` compares one logged latency with one inline timing. Nothing else changed for them.
- **Rationale:** each is the smallest reading that keeps the operation consistent with `793a2a Recall is performed for the working agent`, [793a3a Lessons are captured without the agent](793a_product-spec.md#793a3a-lessons-are-captured-without-the-agent) and `793a2k Intentional memory stays available and is never blocked`.
- **Alternatives:** as listed in [793c36 Does the shim's follow-frame apply to notes the System delivers?](793c_open-questions.md#793c36-does-the-shims-follow-frame-apply-to-notes-the-system-delivers) to [793c39 Does tier evidence reach the orchestrator before it routes?](793c_open-questions.md#793c39-does-tier-evidence-reach-the-orchestrator-before-it-routes).
- **Status update:** the third bullet (please keeps its audit) and the fourth (the orchestrator uses only evidence already delivered) are superseded by the answers to [793c37 What is the shape of please's workflow while the Integration is installed?](793c_open-questions.md#793c37-what-is-the-shape-of-pleases-workflow-while-the-integration-is-installed) and [793c39 Does tier evidence reach the orchestrator before it routes?](793c_open-questions.md#793c39-does-tier-evidence-reach-the-orchestrator-before-it-routes), as [793b108 Interpretations made in applying 793c37](#793b108-interpretations-made-in-applying-793c37) and [793b112 Interpretations made in applying 793c39](#793b112-interpretations-made-in-applying-793c39) record.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites 7 IDs, each in the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction), which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a2k`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b104 The skill and guidance text changes need the writing-skills cycle

- **Decided by:** Author (agent), interpreting [793c31 Which canonical nodes that ask the agent to run recall or learn change while the Integration is installed?](793c_open-questions.md#793c31-which-canonical-nodes-that-ask-the-agent-to-run-recall-or-learn-change-while-the-integration-is-installed); awaiting owner validation.

- **Choice:** at implementation, every change to a SKILL.md or a reference under `agent-instructions/skills/`, and to a guidance document under `agent-instructions/guidance/` (`recall.md`, `learn.md`, `delegate.md`, `shim.md`), that carries the Integration branch of `793a7`, `793a6` and the rest of this change goes through the `superpowers:writing-skills` cycle: a baseline behavior test that fails (RED), the edit (GREEN), a verified change in behavior, and pressure tests, as the project instructions require.
- **Rationale:** the project instructions require it for any SKILL.md edit, and guidance documents are the same kind of artifact. The operations here change what the skills' and guidance's text must say.
- **Alternatives:** none; this is a standing rule.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a6`, `793a7`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).
