## 793b106 793c36: the shim follow-frame nodes stay unmodified

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c36 Does the shim's follow-frame apply to notes the System delivers?](793c_open-questions.md#793c36-does-the-shims-follow-frame-apply-to-notes-the-system-delivers), chose (a): leave the nodes unchanged.

- **Choice:** [792a19l Each returned kind is treated for what it is](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19l-each-returned-kind-is-treated-for-what-it-is) to [792a19q Linked and truncated runbooks get the same frame](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19q-linked-and-truncated-runbooks-get-the-same-frame) stay unmodified. They govern how the agent treats an item it has, whoever retrieved it. Verified against `793a7`: none of its operations targets them. The row for them in [793b101 Candidates examined, changed and left](#793b101-candidates-examined-changed-and-left) ("left") stands.
- **Rationale:** the spec nodes describe the treatment, not the retrieval; the behavior asked of the agent is the same.
- **Alternatives:** (b) widen each to "a query returns or the System delivers". Not chosen by the owner.
- **Amended by [793b178 B1: the reduced shim exempts a delivered memory runbook from the follow-frame](#793b178-b1-the-reduced-shim-exempts-a-delivered-memory-runbook-from-the-follow-frame) (2026-10-11):** [792a19l Each returned kind is treated for what it is](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19l-each-returned-kind-is-treated-for-what-it-is) and [792a19q Linked and truncated runbooks get the same frame](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19q-linked-and-truncated-runbooks-get-the-same-frame) are now modified, to exempt a delivered memory runbook. The original choice above stands as recorded.
- **Follow-up (the owner's option text):** when the shim and guidance text is rewritten at implementation to say "returned or delivered", that rewrite goes through the writing-skills cycle ([793b104 The skill and guidance text changes need the writing-skills cycle](#793b104-the-skill-and-guidance-text-changes-need-the-writing-skills-cycle)).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a7`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b107 793c37: please drops its learn steps, its audit and its surfacing question

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c37 What is the shape of please's workflow while the Integration is installed?](793c_open-questions.md#793c37-what-is-the-shape-of-pleases-workflow-while-the-integration-is-installed), chose (c): the System runs please's lessons audit and the which-artifact-should-have-surfaced-it question and reword from the transcript, as part of capture; please drops them. Please also drops its opening and closing learn.

- **Choice:** `793a3o` is new and says the System performs the audit and the question per [792a18p The closing learn is preceded by a lessons audit](../../specs/product/please.md#792a18p-the-closing-learn-is-preceded-by-a-lessons-audit) and [792a18q A lesson about to be captured asks what should have surfaced it](../../specs/product/please.md#792a18q-a-lesson-about-to-be-captured-asks-what-should-have-surfaced-it) without restating their procedure. `793a7l`, `793a7o` and `793a7p` now say please performs none of them. `793a6i` no longer mentions an audit map in the closing report.
- **Rationale:** the audit and the question exist to feed a capture, and the System now captures.
- **Alternatives:** (a) keep the audit in please and drop the question; (b) keep step 7 as a report-only step. Not chosen by the owner.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a3o`, `793a6i`, `793a7l`, `793a7o`, `793a7p`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b108 Interpretations made in applying 793c37

- **Decided by:** Author (agent), interpreting [793c37 What is the shape of please's workflow while the Integration is installed?](793c_open-questions.md#793c37-what-is-the-shape-of-pleases-workflow-while-the-integration-is-installed); awaiting owner validation.

- **Choice:** the System performs the audit and the question in its capture after the turn in which the please run completes ([793a3a Lessons are captured without the agent](793a_product-spec.md#793a3a-lessons-are-captured-without-the-agent)); the answer said "as part of capture" and the capture runs after each completed turn.
- **Choice:** the audit list is no longer shown in please's closing report; its outcome is in the log and the vault, which is what the answer asked the scenarios to show. An item with no existing note is captured by the System as a reversal, which is what [792a18p The closing learn is preceded by a lessons audit](../../specs/product/please.md#792a18p-the-closing-learn-is-preceded-by-a-lessons-audit) says the closing learn does with it. Whether the user must also be shown the list is [793c40 Does the user still see please's audit list?](793c_open-questions.md#793c40-does-the-user-still-see-pleases-audit-list).
- **Choice:** the rule that a change shipped from the repository is proposed only as a last resort and never filed automatically ([792a18q A lesson about to be captured asks what should have surfaced it](../../specs/product/please.md#792a18q-a-lesson-about-to-be-captured-asks-what-should-have-surfaced-it)) is kept for the System (`793a3o1d`).
- **Choice:** scenarios `793a7o1a` and `793a7o1b` (the Integration's please audits and reports an unmapped item) and `793a6i1a` (the audit map is in the closing report) reverse their meaning and are retired and never reused. Their replacements are `793a7o1d`, `793a7o1e Without the Integration the audit enumerates the corpus and maps each item` and `793a6i1b`.
- **Choice:** the third bullet of [793b103 Other judgments in the Integration branches](#793b103-other-judgments-in-the-integration-branches) (please keeps its audit) is superseded.
- **Rationale:** each is the smallest reading that applies the answer.
- **Alternatives:** keep the retired scenario IDs with their new meaning; rejected, because a node whose meaning reversed gets a new ID ([793b14 ID handling](#793b14-id-handling)).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a3o1`, `793a3o1d`, `793a6i1`, `793a6i1b`, `793a7o1`, `793a7o1d`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b109 793c38: the System writes route's evidence notes

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c38 Do the orchestrator's route-evidence writes stay?](793c_open-questions.md#793c38-do-the-orchestrators-route-evidence-writes-stay), chose (b): the System writes route's evidence notes and the per-work-kind aggregate, reading tier, model and outcome from route's report row in the transcript; the orchestrator writes none.

- **Choice:** `793a3p` is new, and `793a7q` and `793a7r` are Modified operations against [792a17k Each dispatch is also written to the vault as a tagged evidence note](../../specs/product/route.md#792a17k-each-dispatch-is-also-written-to-the-vault-as-a-tagged-evidence-note) and [792a17l Each work-kind keeps one aggregate note](../../specs/product/route.md#792a17l-each-work-kind-keeps-one-aggregate-note), conditional on the Integration, with the target IDs preserved and scenarios for both conditions. `793a2k Intentional memory stays available and is never blocked` keeps its wording ("write any memory"), which now holds.
- **Rationale:** the notes are measurements of dispatches taken from the row the orchestrator already produces ([792a17i Each dispatch is recorded in a mini-report row](../../specs/product/route.md#792a17i-each-dispatch-is-recorded-in-a-mini-report-row)); the System reads them there.
- **Alternatives:** (a) keep the orchestrator's writes and narrow `793a2k Intentional memory stays available and is never blocked`; (c) end the tier-evidence loop. Not chosen by the owner.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a7q`, `793a7r`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

- **Amended by:** [793b227 793a3p is removed: route's evidence write stays with the orchestrator](#793b227-793a3p-is-removed-routes-evidence-write-stays-with-the-orchestrator): the System no longer writes route's evidence notes; `793a3p` is retired and the orchestrator keeps writing them as route defines.

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a2k`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b110 A report row must be in the transcript

- **Decided by:** Author (agent), interpreting [793c38 Do the orchestrator's route-evidence writes stay?](793c_open-questions.md#793c38-do-the-orchestrators-route-evidence-writes-stay); awaiting owner validation.

- **Choice:** the System writes the evidence note and the aggregate only for a dispatch whose row ([792a17i Each dispatch is recorded in a mini-report row](../../specs/product/route.md#792a17i-each-dispatch-is-recorded-in-a-mini-report-row)) is present in the transcript, because the row is the only place the tier, model and outcome are stated. When the row is absent, the System writes nothing for that dispatch and the log records that the row was missing. The scenarios do not cover the absent row; it is an interpretation, not a requirement, until the owner validates it.
- **Choice:** In the Integration branch of `793a7r`: the System composes and runs the aggregate write itself and does not hand it to write-memory, as the canonical text has the orchestrator do; the sentence that this is a structurally forced exception the skill must state, and the sentence that the orchestrator must state both candidate commands before the lookup has run, are dropped because both address the orchestrator. The `Where the Integration is not installed` branch keeps them.
- **Rationale:** the outcome is the review or gate verdict, not the subagent's report ([792a17i Each dispatch is recorded in a mini-report row](../../specs/product/route.md#792a17i-each-dispatch-is-recorded-in-a-mini-report-row)), so it cannot be inferred from the subagent's session; writing a guessed note would put false evidence into the loop that routes on it.
- **Status update:** the second bullet of Choice is superseded by [793b123 M7: the evidence-note branches follow the delegation pattern](#793b123-m7-the-evidence-note-branches-follow-the-delegation-pattern): the Integration branch of `793a7r` now follows the branch below with the System in the orchestrator's place.
- **Alternatives:** write a note with the outcome inferred from the subagent's report. Rejected: that is the confabulation [792a17i Each dispatch is recorded in a mini-report row](../../specs/product/route.md#792a17i-each-dispatch-is-recorded-in-a-mini-report-row) exists to avoid. Ask the orchestrator for the row. Rejected: it asks the working agent to handle memory bookkeeping, against `793a2k Intentional memory stays available and is never blocked`.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a7r`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

- **Amended by:** [793b227 793a3p is removed: route's evidence write stays with the orchestrator](#793b227-793a3p-is-removed-routes-evidence-write-stays-with-the-orchestrator): the rule concerned `793a3p`, which is retired; the System writes no evidence note, so no row is read or logged.

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a2k`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b111 793c39: the decision to dispatch is a recall moment

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c39 Does tier evidence reach the orchestrator before it routes?](793c_open-questions.md#793c39-does-tier-evidence-reach-the-orchestrator-before-it-routes), chose (b): the orchestrator's decision to dispatch is a recall moment; the System delivers the work-kind's tier evidence before the orchestrator routes.

- **Choice:** `793a2c` names the decision to dispatch as a moment of its own kind, with `793a2c1d`; `793a7i` says the tier comes from the evidence the System delivered at that moment, otherwise the cheapest tier.
- **Rationale:** without the moment, a work-kind's aggregate is delivered only if it happens to match another moment, and an evidenced escalation is missed.
- **Alternatives:** (a) use only what is already delivered; (c) let the orchestrator look the aggregate up. Not chosen by the owner.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a2c`, `793a2c1`, `793a2c1d`, `793a7i`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b112 Interpretations made in applying 793c39

- **Decided by:** Author (agent), interpreting [793c39 Does tier evidence reach the orchestrator before it routes?](793c_open-questions.md#793c39-does-tier-evidence-reach-the-orchestrator-before-it-routes); awaiting owner validation.

- **Choice:** how the System detects that the orchestrator is about to dispatch, and how it determines the unit's kind of work, is for the technical specification and is not specified here (`793a2c` says so in its Rationale). The product behavior is that the evidence is in the transcript before the orchestrator routes.
- **Choice:** "the work-kind's tier evidence" is what recall returns for the unit's kind of work; `793a7i` keeps its rule that no special query or counting is used on the read path.
- **Choice:** the depth of this recall is chosen as for any other moment; this entry sets none.
- **Choice:** each decision to dispatch is a moment, so a unit re-dispatched at a higher tier after a failure is another one; evidence already in the orchestrator's context is not delivered again ([793a2e Nothing already in context is delivered again](793a_product-spec.md#793a2e-nothing-already-in-context-is-delivered-again)).
- **Choice:** the dispatch decision is distinct from the subagent-dispatch moment of `793a2c`: one delivers to the orchestrator before it routes, the other to the subagent before it starts. The log kind is "dispatch decision".
- **Rationale:** each is the smallest reading that applies the answer without specifying the mechanism.
- **Alternatives:** make the decision to dispatch and the subagent's dispatch one moment. Rejected: they deliver to different agents at different times.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a2c`, `793a7i`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b114 A memory-activity summary is shown to the user

- **Choice:** a new capability, to be added to 793a as a requirement with its own ID. After each completed turn, the System shows the user, outside the working agent's context, the counts of memory events since its previous summary:
  - items surfaced (injected);
  - notes used (activated);
  - notes added;
  - notes amended;
  - captures discarded;
  - evidence notes written.

  The please audit's per-item list and the reason for each event stay in the log and the vault. A turn with no memory events shows nothing.
- **Rationale:** the user keeps visibility of the memory work the System took over, including what please's closing report used to show, without the working agent relaying it.
- **Alternatives:** the options of [793c40 Does the user still see please's audit list?](793c_open-questions.md#793c40-does-the-user-still-see-pleases-audit-list): (a) the list in the vault and the log only; (b) the System also presents the list to the user at the end of the run; (c) please reads the System's log and includes the list in its closing report. Not chosen; the owner chose (d), the counts.
- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c40 Does the user still see please's audit list?](793c_open-questions.md#793c40-does-the-user-still-see-pleases-audit-list) (d). Orchestrator (Claude) interpreted "every opportunity" as after each completed turn, chose the counted kinds, and chose to stay silent when nothing happened. Awaiting owner validation.
- **Status:** applied in commit 9295802e (new requirement [793a5h The user sees a summary of the memory activity](793a_product-spec.md#793a5h-the-user-sees-a-summary-of-the-memory-activity) with scenarios [793a5h1a The summary shows the counts after a turn with events](793a_product-spec.md#793a5h1-uat-scenarios) to [793a5h1e The counts cover the events since the previous summary](793a_product-spec.md#793a5h1-uat-scenarios); the please-related operations `793a3o` and `793a7o` reference it).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a3o`, `793a7o`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b115 Fifth review: findings held for 793c41 to 793c44

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Note:** the review's original text is not kept in the repository. Each finding below is written from the question it raised, so its wording is the author's, and so is the pairing of each finding with its question.
- **H1: what the agent's context gets at a recall moment.**
  - **Finding:** `793a2a Recall is performed for the working agent` says the System puts in front of the agent what recall would have put in front of it, while `793a2d Delivered memory is attributable` and [793a2e Nothing already in context is delivered again](793a_product-spec.md#793a2e-nothing-already-in-context-is-delivered-again) describe raw items named by source. Recall also gives its agent a plan ([792a14a The plan is stated before anything is retrieved](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14a-the-plan-is-stated-before-anything-is-retrieved)), a closing synthesis ([792a14m The closing synthesis says whether memory changed the plan](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14m-the-closing-synthesis-says-whether-memory-changed-the-plan), kept at glance by [792a14c Recall has two depths](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14c-recall-has-two-depths)) and a reconciliation ([792a14n A recommendation is reconciled against the asked task](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14n-a-recommendation-is-reconciled-against-the-asked-task), [792a14o An emergent recommendation is checked against the vault](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14o-an-emergent-recommendation-is-checked-against-the-vault)), so the delegation as written has an exception whichever reading is taken.
  - **Fix to be applied:** decide what the agent's context gets, then make the three requirements and their scenarios say it. With the recommendation (b): the results plus the closing synthesis, with the plan inferred from the transcript.
  - **Waits on:** [793c41 At a recall moment, what does the System put in front of the working agent?](793c_open-questions.md#793c41-at-a-recall-moment-what-does-the-system-put-in-front-of-the-working-agent).
- **H2: what runs at every prompt.**
  - **Finding:** `793a2i` says the System performs recall at every prompt, but recall has no per-prompt moment, and the depth that would run there changes what is written to the vault.
  - **Fix to be applied:** decide the per-prompt action. With the recommendation (a): the shim's lookup of [792a19j The first action on every request is an engram query](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19j-the-first-action-on-every-request-is-an-engram-query), delegated exactly.
  - **Waits on:** [793c42 What runs at every prompt (D1)?](793c_open-questions.md#793c42-what-runs-at-every-prompt-d1).
- **M2: pending offers and the curate step.**
  - **Finding:** [792a14l Only the notes that were used are activated](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14l-only-the-notes-that-were-used-are-activated) and [792a22o Pending offers are surfaced at three points](../../specs/product/curate.md#792a22o-pending-offers-are-surfaced-at-three-points) hand the curate step to the agent, and [792a19s A signal of unrequested work is a standing instruction](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19s-a-signal-of-unrequested-work-is-a-standing-instruction) tells the agent to obey the pending-offers hint, so the delegated agent is still asked for memory work, against `793a2k Intentional memory stays available and is never blocked`.
  - **Fix to be applied:** decide who curates. With the recommendation (a): the System runs curate after its recall and the hint is removed from what the agent sees, with the effects listed in [793a4c Memory purview only](793a_product-spec.md#793a4c-memory-purview-only).
  - **Waits on:** [793c43 Who handles pending offers and the curate step?](793c_open-questions.md#793c43-who-handles-pending-offers-and-the-curate-step).
- **Q4-dependent parts: where the skills' messages to the user go.**
  - **Finding:** recall's synthesis ([792a14m The closing synthesis says whether memory changed the plan](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14m-the-closing-synthesis-says-whether-memory-changed-the-plan)), learn's saved or queued report ([792a15j A write to a parent is reported as done](../../specs/product/learn.md#792a15j-a-write-to-a-parent-is-reported-as-done)), please's repository-change proposal ([792a18q A lesson about to be captured asks what should have surfaced it](../../specs/product/please.md#792a18q-a-lesson-about-to-be-captured-asks-what-should-have-surfaced-it)) and route's drowning report ([792a17n Drowning is reported, not patched](../../specs/product/route.md#792a17n-drowning-is-reported-not-patched)) address the user through the agent, and [793a5h The user sees a summary of the memory activity](793a_product-spec.md#793a5h-the-user-sees-a-summary-of-the-memory-activity) shows counts only.
  - **Fix to be applied:** decide the destination. With the recommendation (b) for items that ask the user to act and (a) for routine reports. Meanwhile only the drowning report is applied, as `793a7u`; `793a7p` is held.
  - **Waits on:** [793c44 Where do the delegated skills' messages to the user go?](793c_open-questions.md#793c44-where-do-the-delegated-skills-messages-to-the-user-go).
- **Rationale:** an answer may change the fix, so applying them first would risk rework.
- **Alternatives:** apply the recommendations now and amend on the answers. Not chosen.
- **Status:** open; the questions are in [`793c_open-questions.md`](793c_open-questions.md).
- **Status update:** the four questions are answered and applied ([793b137 793c41: recall is split between the System and the agent](#793b137-793c41-recall-is-split-between-the-system-and-the-agent) to [793b140 793c44: the skills' messages are shown to the user](#793b140-793c44-the-skills-messages-are-shown-to-the-user)). `793a7p`, which was held, is applied.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a2i`, `793a7p`, `793a7u`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a2d`, `793a2k`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b116 H3: the count audit, the aggregate amend and the drowning lookup are the System's

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Finding:** `793a3p` moves the aggregate to the System, but [792a17m Counting audits the tallies and never routes](../../specs/product/route.md#792a17m-counting-audits-the-tallies-and-never-routes) still has the orchestrator run `engram count` and amend the aggregate, and [792a17n Drowning is reported, not patched](../../specs/product/route.md#792a17n-drowning-is-reported-not-patched) still has it run the aggregate lookup and report drowning.
- **Fix applied:** conditional Modified operations `793a7t` and `793a7u`, with Integration scenarios and the canonical scenarios (`793a7t2`, `793a7u2`). Where the drowning report goes waited on [793c44 Where do the delegated skills' messages to the user go?](793c_open-questions.md#793c44-where-do-the-delegated-skills-messages-to-the-user-go); meanwhile its recommendation was applied and the report was shown to the user, outside the working agent's context, as [793a5h The user sees a summary of the memory activity](793a_product-spec.md#793a5h-the-user-sees-a-summary-of-the-memory-activity) shows its summary. The owner's answer confirms it, and the report is now one of the messages of `793a5i`.
- **Rationale:** an aggregate the System keeps cannot be audited or repaired by an orchestrator that is barred from writing it.
- **Alternatives:** leave the audit with the orchestrator and narrow `793a3p`. Not chosen: the orchestrator would amend an aggregate the System also keeps.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this review ([793b135 ID map for the fifth review](#793b135-id-map-for-the-fifth-review)).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a5i`, `793a7t`, `793a7t2`, `793a7u`, `793a7u2`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

- **Amended by:** [793b227 793a3p is removed: route's evidence write stays with the orchestrator](#793b227-793a3p-is-removed-routes-evidence-write-stays-with-the-orchestrator): `793a3p` is retired; the aggregate stays with the orchestrator.

## 793b117 H4: every Modified target keeps its canonical scenarios

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Finding:** the operations of `793a6` and `793a7` replace each target's text, but only the Integration branch had scenarios; the canonical scenarios of the not-installed branch were not carried, and nineteen "Without the Integration" scenarios restated canonical scenarios under new IDs.
- **Fix applied:** each operation gets a group `<operation>2` holding one Modified operation per canonical scenario of its target, presented as a table: the target scenario and its canonical text prefixed with "Given the Integration is not installed". The groups are one group for each operation, from `793a6a2` to `793a7u2`; 110 rows. The nineteen restating scenarios are retired and their IDs are never reused: `793a7a1e`, `793a7b1b`, `793a7c1d`, `793a7d1c`, `793a7e1b`, `793a7f1b`, `793a7g1c`, `793a7h1b`, `793a7i1c`, `793a7j1c`, `793a7k1b`, `793a7l1c`, `793a7m1c`, `793a7n1c`, `793a7o1c`, `793a7o1e`, `793a7p1c`, `793a7q1e`, `793a7r1f`. The preambles of `793a6` and `793a7` say so.
- **Choice:** the prefix is a leading Given and the canonical first Given becomes And, so the canonical steps are otherwise unchanged. Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Rationale:** the lifecycle guide requires a Modified operation to give the target's complete replacement text, and the canonical scenarios are part of it.
- **Alternatives:** keep the "Without the Integration" scenarios as the not-installed coverage. Not chosen: they duplicated canonical scenarios and covered only some of them.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this review ([793b135 ID map for the fifth review](#793b135-id-map-for-the-fifth-review)).
- **Status update:** superseded by [793b147 H4: only the canonical scenarios whose outcome differs by branch are modified (revisits 793b117)](#793b147-h4-only-the-canonical-scenarios-whose-outcome-differs-by-branch-are-modified-revisits-793b117): only the canonical scenarios whose outcome differs by branch are modified, and the Integration branches are trimmed to what differs.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a6`, `793a6a2`, `793a7`, `793a7u2`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b118 M1: a coverage write's activation is not an inference verdict

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Finding:** `793a3k` says the System activates used notes in place of recall's activation, but recall's coverage write also activates ([792a14j Coverage is judged by the agent and written once per cluster](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14j-coverage-is-judged-by-the-agent-and-written-once-per-cluster)), and the requirement could be read to make that a verdict.
- **Fix applied:** `793a3k` governs only the activation of injected notes in place of [792a14l Only the notes that were used are activated](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14l-only-the-notes-that-were-used-are-activated); the coverage write's activation is done as recall defines it. Scenario `793a3k1f`.
- **Rationale:** a coverage write is a vault write that recall performs, not a judgment of use.
- **Alternatives:** let the inference cover every activation. Not chosen: the transcript has no evidence of use for a covered cluster's candidate.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this review ([793b135 ID map for the fifth review](#793b135-id-map-for-the-fifth-review)).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a3k`, `793a3k1`, `793a3k1f`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b119 M3: the dispatch decision delivers recall's result, and the orchestrator uses what is in its context

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Finding:** `793a2c` delivered only the tier evidence at the decision to dispatch, `793a7i` said the orchestrator uses what the System delivered, and [792a17d A memory-backed unit drops one tier](../../specs/product/route.md#792a17d-a-memory-backed-unit-drops-one-tier) still discounts a unit whose knowledge is "recallable", which the orchestrator can no longer check.
- **Fix applied:** `793a2c`: at the decision the System performs recall for the unit and puts what it returns in front of the orchestrator, including the tier evidence. `793a7i`: the orchestrator uses the tier evidence in its context, including what was delivered at that decision. New conditional operation `793a7s` for [792a17d A memory-backed unit drops one tier](../../specs/product/route.md#792a17d-a-memory-backed-unit-drops-one-tier): "recallable" becomes "in the orchestrator's context from the System's recall".
- **Rationale:** the orchestrator runs no recall, so only what is in its context can drive routing.
- **Alternatives:** deliver tier evidence only. Not chosen: the discount and any other recalled knowledge would be missed.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this review ([793b135 ID map for the fifth review](#793b135-id-map-for-the-fifth-review)).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a2c`, `793a7i`, `793a7s`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b120 M4: every recall moment is listed

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Finding:** `793a2a Recall is performed for the working agent` listed the guidance moments and every prompt, not the subagent dispatch or the decision to dispatch (`793a2c`), and `793a5g1a` compared no dispatch-decision moment.
- **Fix applied:** `793a2a Recall is performed for the working agent` lists the guidance moments ([792a19a Recall fires at four decision moments](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19a-recall-fires-at-four-decision-moments), [792a19b Recall escalates from glance to deep](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19b-recall-escalates-from-glance-to-deep)), every prompt (`793a2i`) and the two moments of `793a2c`; the Examples of `793a5g1a` gain a dispatch decision row.
- **Rationale:** the requirement that says when recall is performed must name every moment.
- **Alternatives:** refer to `793a2c` for its moments only. Not chosen: it left the list incomplete.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this review ([793b135 ID map for the fifth review](#793b135-id-map-for-the-fifth-review)).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a2c`, `793a2i`, `793a5g1`, `793a5g1a`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b121 M5: the summary covers the session and its subagents, after capture

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Finding:** [793a5h The user sees a summary of the memory activity](793a_product-spec.md#793a5h-the-user-sees-a-summary-of-the-memory-activity) could be shown before the turn's capture finished, leaving its events for the next summary, did not say whether subagent sessions count, and `793a5h1d` asked the log for events "for that turn" while the counts run from the previous summary.
- **Fix applied:** [793a5h The user sees a summary of the memory activity](793a_product-spec.md#793a5h-the-user-sees-a-summary-of-the-memory-activity): after each completed top-level turn, once the System's work for that turn (capture included) has finished, the counts of events recorded since its previous summary, across the session and its subagent sessions. `793a5h1d`: "since the previous summary". New scenarios `793a5h1f` and `793a5h1g The summary follows the capture`.
- **Rationale:** a summary that races the capture misstates the turn.
- **Alternatives:** show the summary at the end of the turn and count late events in the next one. Not chosen: the user would see a count that the log later contradicts.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this review ([793b135 ID map for the fifth review](#793b135-id-map-for-the-fifth-review)).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a5h1d`, `793a5h1f`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a5h1g`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b122 M6: the purview rule is about the working agent's context

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Finding:** [793a4c Memory purview only](793a_product-spec.md#793a4c-memory-purview-only) said its only effect on the working session is the injected items, but the System also shows the user the summary ([793a5h The user sees a summary of the memory activity](793a_product-spec.md#793a5h-the-user-sees-a-summary-of-the-memory-activity)).
- **Fix applied:** [793a4c Memory purview only](793a_product-spec.md#793a4c-memory-purview-only): its only effect on the working agent's context is the items it injects; outside that context it shows the user the summary and does the writes it listed. [793a4c1c The only effect on the working agent's context is what is delivered](793a_product-spec.md#793a4c1-uat-scenarios) says "to the working agent's context".
- **Rationale:** the summary is outside the agent's context by [793a5h The user sees a summary of the memory activity](793a_product-spec.md#793a5h-the-user-sees-a-summary-of-the-memory-activity).
- **Alternatives:** list the summary as an effect on the session. Not chosen: it is not in the agent's context.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this review ([793b135 ID map for the fifth review](#793b135-id-map-for-the-fifth-review)).

## 793b123 M7: the evidence-note branches follow the delegation pattern

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Finding:** the Integration branches of `793a7q` and `793a7r` restated the whole procedure, and `793a3p` pointed back to them ("as `793a7q` and `793a7r` state"), a circle.
- **Fix applied:** both branches now read "While the Integration is installed, the System MUST write this note as `793a3p` says, following the branch below with the System in the orchestrator's place (except its sentences addressed to the orchestrator), and the orchestrator MUST NOT write it." (`793a7r` says "keep this aggregate"). `793a3p` no longer points back. The second bullet of [793b110 A report row must be in the transcript](#793b110-a-report-row-must-be-in-the-transcript) is superseded.
- **Rationale:** one statement of the procedure, in the canonical branch.
- **Alternatives:** keep the restated branches. Not chosen: two copies drift.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this review ([793b135 ID map for the fifth review](#793b135-id-map-for-the-fifth-review)).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a7q`, `793a7r`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

- **Amended by:** [793b227 793a3p is removed: route's evidence write stays with the orchestrator](#793b227-793a3p-is-removed-routes-evidence-write-stays-with-the-orchestrator): `793a3p` is retired; route's evidence write follows route's own text.

## 793b124 M8: provenance adds to the source the skill defines

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Finding:** `793a3d` and `793a3d1a` said the source field identifies automatic capture, which conflicts with the source a skill defines for a note (for example route's "route dispatch record, <project>, <date>").
- **Fix applied:** `793a3d`: every note the System writes automatically records, in addition to the source the skill defines, that it was written automatically, and the session and turn. `793a3d1a` checks both.
- **Rationale:** the System writes as the skills write.
- **Alternatives:** replace the skill's source. Not chosen: the skill's source is part of the skill's contract.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this review ([793b135 ID map for the fifth review](#793b135-id-map-for-the-fifth-review)).

## 793b133 793c45: the Integration is installed for Claude Code only

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c45 Is the Integration installed for every harness, or only for Claude Code?](793c_open-questions.md#793c45-is-the-integration-installed-for-every-harness-or-only-for-claude-code): the existing skills stay for non-Claude agents, the spec says so, and init and update do different things on Claude harnesses than on others.
- **Choice:** `793a4h Installed by engram update and active in Claude Code`, `793a4g` and [793a5c Other harnesses are unchanged](793a_product-spec.md#793a5c-other-harnesses-are-unchanged) are per harness. The conditions of `793a6` and `793a7` are per harness and per agent session (their preambles and the Integration term of [793a1 Problem Statement](793a_product-spec.md#793a1-problem-statement)). Scenarios `793a4g1d`, `793a4h1d One update serves both harnesses differently` and [793a5c1b A Pi session on a machine whose Claude Code has the System](793a_product-spec.md#793a5c1-uat-scenarios) cover one machine with both harnesses.
- **Rationale:** a harness the System does not run in must keep working as before.
- **Alternatives:** install for every harness in which the System can run. Not chosen by the owner.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this review ([793b135 ID map for the fifth review](#793b135-id-map-for-the-fifth-review)).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a4g`, `793a4g1`, `793a4g1d`, `793a6`, `793a7`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a4h`, `793a4h1d`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b134 Interpretations made in applying 793c45, 793c46 and 793c47

- **Decided by:** Author (agent), interpreting [793c45 Is the Integration installed for every harness, or only for Claude Code?](793c_open-questions.md#793c45-is-the-integration-installed-for-every-harness-or-only-for-claude-code), [793c46 On Claude Code with the Integration installed, does engram update still deploy the recall and learn skills and guidance files?](793c_open-questions.md#793c46-on-claude-code-with-the-integration-installed-does-engram-update-still-deploy-the-recall-and-learn-skills-and-guidance-files) and [793c47 Does "init" mean `engram init` or the first-time install by `engram update`?](793c_open-questions.md#793c47-does-init-mean-engram-init-or-the-first-time-install-by-engram-update); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** "Claude harnesses" is the Claude Code harness; Pi is the only other harness today, and every other harness is treated as Pi.
- **Choice:** [793c46 On Claude Code with the Integration installed, does engram update still deploy the recall and learn skills and guidance files?](793c_open-questions.md#793c46-on-claude-code-with-the-integration-installed-does-engram-update-still-deploy-the-recall-and-learn-skills-and-guidance-files) (b) is read as: on Claude Code with the Integration installed, the recall and learn skills, the guidance documents, the guidance imports and the shim are not deployed, and `engram update` removes what an earlier install deployed. Whether the user keeps any explicit recall or learn command there is not specified.
- **Choice:** [793c47 Does "init" mean `engram init` or the first-time install by `engram update`?](793c_open-questions.md#793c47-does-init-mean-engram-init-or-the-first-time-install-by-engram-update) (b) is read as: `engram init` does all setup, the vault included, idempotently, and `engram update` runs it for each harness it updates. `793a4h Installed by engram update and active in Claude Code` says so; `--dry-run` still installs none of it.
- **Choice:** the answer's "Init and update should do different things on Claude harnesses than on others" is read as the per-harness split of what they install.
- **Rationale:** each is the smallest reading that applies the answers.
- **Alternatives:** the options of [793c46 On Claude Code with the Integration installed, does engram update still deploy the recall and learn skills and guidance files?](793c_open-questions.md#793c46-on-claude-code-with-the-integration-installed-does-engram-update-still-deploy-the-recall-and-learn-skills-and-guidance-files) and [793c47 Does "init" mean `engram init` or the first-time install by `engram update`?](793c_open-questions.md#793c47-does-init-mean-engram-init-or-the-first-time-install-by-engram-update); the owner chose (b) for both.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this review ([793b135 ID map for the fifth review](#793b135-id-map-for-the-fifth-review)).

- **Amended by:** [793b186 793a4h is delivered by this change and does not depend on #795](793b_decision-log.md#793b186-793a4h-is-delivered-by-this-change-and-does-not-depend-on-795): 793a4h no longer makes `engram init` the owner of the Integration install; `engram update` installs it directly (the entry itself is left as written).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a4h`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b136 Change 793 depends on change 795 being widened

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c48 How is the widening of `engram init` (change #795) sequenced with this change?](793c_open-questions.md#793c48-how-is-the-widening-of-engram-init-change-795-sequenced-with-this-change) (a): widen #795. Recorded first by Author (agent), interpreting [793c47 Does "init" mean `engram init` or the first-time install by `engram update`?](793c_open-questions.md#793c47-does-init-mean-engram-init-or-the-first-time-install-by-engram-update); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** `793a4h Installed by engram update and active in Claude Code` needs `engram init` to initialize everything idempotently and `engram update` to run it. Change 795 ([795a_product-spec.md](../795_vault-init-command/795a_product-spec.md), [795b_decision-log.md](../795_vault-init-command/795b_decision-log.md)) scoped init to vault creation only and says `engram update` with no vault skips its vault steps and never creates one. The decision is to widen change 795: its issue is retitled and its scope extended so that init initializes everything idempotently and update runs it; its specification is widened as part of #795's own work, and change 793 builds on it. This change records the dependency and does not edit those files, so until #795's files are widened they still describe the vault-only init, and the conflict with update running init is #795's to resolve.
- **Rationale:** a change directory belongs to its own issue, and one change owns `engram init`, so the two specifications cannot disagree once #795 is widened ([793b141 793c48: change 795 is widened and this change builds on it](#793b141-793c48-change-795-is-widened-and-this-change-builds-on-it)).
- **Alternatives:** the options of [793c48 How is the widening of `engram init` (change #795) sequenced with this change?](793c_open-questions.md#793c48-how-is-the-widening-of-engram-init-change-795-sequenced-with-this-change): (b) this change specifies the harness part of init itself and #795 stays vault-only; (c) a new issue for "init sets up everything". Not chosen by the owner.
- **Status:** applied in commit b2eceed0; decided by the owner's answer to [793c48 How is the widening of `engram init` (change #795) sequenced with this change?](793c_open-questions.md#793c48-how-is-the-widening-of-engram-init-change-795-sequenced-with-this-change); the dependency stays until #795 is widened.

- **Amended by:** [793b186 793a4h is delivered by this change and does not depend on #795](793b_decision-log.md#793b186-793a4h-is-delivered-by-this-change-and-does-not-depend-on-795): the dependency on change 795 is withdrawn; 793a4h is delivered by this change on today's `engram update` (the entry itself is left as written).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a4h`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b137 793c41: recall is split between the System and the agent

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c41 At a recall moment, what does the System put in front of the working agent?](793c_open-questions.md#793c41-at-a-recall-moment-what-does-the-system-put-in-front-of-the-working-agent), his own option: recall's planning part is what the agent does with what was recalled. The System returns the results with an instruction to work them into the agent's plan, or to replan entirely, and the agent does recall's plan, synthesis and reconciliation itself, in its own reasoning. Joe: "The watcher won't have all of the same context as the main agent. Its job is to handle memory, not play puppet master."
- **Interpretation:** Author (agent), interpreting [793c41 At a recall moment, what does the System put in front of the working agent?](793c_open-questions.md#793c41-at-a-recall-moment-what-does-the-system-put-in-front-of-the-working-agent); awaiting owner validation.
  - The four nodes the answer names are [792a14a The plan is stated before anything is retrieved](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14a-the-plan-is-stated-before-anything-is-retrieved), [792a14m The closing synthesis says whether memory changed the plan](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14m-the-closing-synthesis-says-whether-memory-changed-the-plan), [792a14n A recommendation is reconciled against the asked task](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14n-a-recommendation-is-reconciled-against-the-asked-task) and [792a14o An emergent recommendation is checked against the vault](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14o-an-emergent-recommendation-is-checked-against-the-vault). `793a2a Recall is performed for the working agent` says the agent does them in its own reasoning, from what the System delivered, and the System authors none of them.
  - The instruction that accompanies the results is named the plan instruction (a term in [793a1 Problem Statement](793a_product-spec.md#793a1-problem-statement)). It tells the agent to work the results into its plan, or to replan entirely where they change what it should do. It is delivered with the results of a recall or a lookup, at every moment, and not otherwise.
  - When no result remains to deliver ([793a2e Nothing already in context is delivered again](793a_product-spec.md#793a2e-nothing-already-in-context-is-delivered-again) included), nothing is delivered, the plan instruction included (`793a2a1g No result, nothing delivered`). Joe's answer does not say; an instruction with nothing to apply would only add noise.
  - Option (b), the System inferring the agent's plan from the transcript and delivering the synthesis, is not chosen: it is the System steering the agent.
  - What is delivered stays the raw items named by their source, with a chunk's id and score (`793a2d Delivered memory is attributable`). The rule of [792a14m The closing synthesis says whether memory changed the plan](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14m-the-closing-synthesis-says-whether-memory-changed-the-plan) against raw scores and paths governs what the agent tells the user, so the two do not conflict.
  - `793a2k Intentional memory stays available and is never blocked` keeps its rule that the agent is not asked to run or handle memory; the plan instruction asks it only to apply what was delivered.
  - [793a4c Memory purview only](793a_product-spec.md#793a4c-memory-purview-only) counts the plan instruction among the content the System adds to the agent's context.
  - Two points the answer leaves open are [793c49 Does the agent run recall's re-entry query (792a14o) while the Integration is installed?](793c_open-questions.md#793c49-does-the-agent-run-recalls-re-entry-query-792a14o-while-the-integration-is-installed) and [793c50 Does recall's synthesis to the user (792a14m) still apply, and who shows it?](793c_open-questions.md#793c50-does-recalls-synthesis-to-the-user-792a14m-still-apply-and-who-shows-it); their recommendations are applied meanwhile.
- **Choice (deliberate split):** memory handling goes to the System: the lookup, the writes, the activation, the curation and the bookkeeping. Applying memory stays with the agent: the plan, the synthesis and the reconciliation, which need the agent's full context.
- **Rationale:** the System does not have the agent's context, so it cannot plan, synthesize or recommend for the agent; its job is to handle memory.
- **Alternatives:** the options of [793c41 At a recall moment, what does the System put in front of the working agent?](793c_open-questions.md#793c41-at-a-recall-moment-what-does-the-system-put-in-front-of-the-working-agent): (a) the query results only, a deliberate difference from the skill; (b) the results plus the closing synthesis, with the plan inferred from the transcript (the recommendation); (c) the synthesis only. Not chosen; the owner chose his own option.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records the ID map ([793b142 Interpretations made in applying 793c41 to 793c44 and 793c48](#793b142-interpretations-made-in-applying-793c41-to-793c44-and-793c48)).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a2d`, `793a2k`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b138 793c42: the shim's lookup runs at every prompt

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c42 What runs at every prompt (D1)?](793c_open-questions.md#793c42-what-runs-at-every-prompt-d1) (a): the shim's lookup at every prompt.
- **Interpretation:** Author (agent), interpreting [793c42 What runs at every prompt (D1)?](793c_open-questions.md#793c42-what-runs-at-every-prompt-d1); awaiting owner validation.
  - The lookup is delegated exactly from [792a19j The first action on every request is an engram query](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19j-the-first-action-on-every-request-is-an-engram-query): one `engram query` with its phrase shapes, the System composing its own phrases (`793a7f`), and the user's message as its text. Its results are delivered with the plan instruction, as at every other moment.
  - Recall at a named depth stays at the guidance moments and the two dispatch moments (`793a2a Recall is performed for the working agent`). A prompt gets no depth recall, so no vault write by recall happens at a prompt (`793a2i1c`).
  - A prompt is therefore a lookup moment and not a recall moment: the Moment term of [793a1 Problem Statement](793a_product-spec.md#793a1-problem-statement) says so, and the log records no recall depth for it.
  - `793a2i` keeps its title, so that the links to it hold.
  - `793a2i1b` says "no moment of kind prompt" where it said "no recall moment of kind prompt", and `793a2a1a Recall happens without the agent running it` now starts from a mid-task moment, because a prompt is no longer a recall moment.
  - `793a5g1a` compares a prompt's added latency with the inline lookup.
- **Rationale:** today the per-prompt action is the shim's query, so delegating it changes only who runs it, and the depth that would write to the vault stays where the guidance puts it.
- **Alternatives:** the options of [793c42 What runs at every prompt (D1)?](793c_open-questions.md#793c42-what-runs-at-every-prompt-d1): (b) a glance recall; (c) recall at its canonical default depth, deep, with vault writes on every prompt. Not chosen.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records the ID map ([793b142 Interpretations made in applying 793c41 to 793c44 and 793c48](#793b142-interpretations-made-in-applying-793c41-to-793c44-and-793c48)).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites 7 IDs, each in the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction), which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b139 793c43: the System curates after its recall and lookup

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c43 Who handles pending offers and the curate step?](793c_open-questions.md#793c43-who-handles-pending-offers-and-the-curate-step) (a): the System runs curate after its recall, and the hint is removed from what the agent sees.
- **Interpretation:** Author (agent), interpreting [793c43 Who handles pending offers and the curate step?](793c_open-questions.md#793c43-who-handles-pending-offers-and-the-curate-step); awaiting owner validation.
  - "After its recall" is read as after each recall and each lookup it performs, whenever a pending offer exists, because a lookup is an `engram query` and carries the hint too. `793a2m` adds it as a requirement.
  - It runs after the results are delivered, and the agent does not wait for it (`793a2m1c`), in the way the sweep of `793a2l Indexing and curation happen without the agent` does. The answer does not say; curation as recall defines it is a step after the request.
  - The answer says the hint is removed from what the agent sees. [792a22o Pending offers are surfaced at three points](../../specs/product/curate.md#792a22o-pending-offers-are-surfaced-at-three-points) and [792a14l Only the notes that were used are activated](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14l-only-the-notes-that-were-used-are-activated) are left unmodified: the binary's payload still carries the flag and hint, and the System does not deliver them. The only node modified is the shim's, as `793a7v`, conditional on the Integration, with the canonical scenarios in `793a7v2`.
  - [793a4c Memory purview only](793a_product-spec.md#793a4c-memory-purview-only) lists curation's effects: accepting, folding into existing notes and discarding pending offers.
  - Curation's outcomes are not among the counts of [793a5h The user sees a summary of the memory activity](793a_product-spec.md#793a5h-the-user-sees-a-summary-of-the-memory-activity); the answer does not add one.
- **Rationale:** it keeps recall's behavior and the agent's context free of the work.
- **Alternatives:** the options of [793c43 Who handles pending offers and the curate step?](793c_open-questions.md#793c43-who-handles-pending-offers-and-the-curate-step): (b) the working agent still curates, with `793a2k Intentional memory stays available and is never blocked` narrowed; (c) nobody curates during the session and the summary counts pending offers. Not chosen.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records the ID map ([793b142 Interpretations made in applying 793c41 to 793c44 and 793c48](#793b142-interpretations-made-in-applying-793c41-to-793c44-and-793c48)).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a2m`, `793a2m1`, `793a2m1c`, `793a7v`, `793a7v2`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a2k`, `793a2l`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b140 793c44: the skills' messages are shown to the user

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c44 Where do the delegated skills' messages to the user go?](793c_open-questions.md#793c44-where-do-the-delegated-skills-messages-to-the-user-go) (b): all of these messages are shown to the user on the summary's display surface. Joe will open a new change if it is too much. That last sentence is recorded here only; no requirement or scenario carries it.
- **Interpretation:** Author (agent), interpreting [793c44 Where do the delegated skills' messages to the user go?](793c_open-questions.md#793c44-where-do-the-delegated-skills-messages-to-the-user-go); awaiting owner validation.
  - A sibling requirement, `793a5i`, carries the messages, and [793a5h The user sees a summary of the memory activity](793a_product-spec.md#793a5h-the-user-sees-a-summary-of-the-memory-activity) points to it. [793a5h The user sees a summary of the memory activity](793a_product-spec.md#793a5h-the-user-sees-a-summary-of-the-memory-activity) is counts, shown for events recorded since the previous summary; the messages are single events with their own wording.
  - The messages are those the answer names that the System now authors: learn's saved-and-queued report ([792a15j A write to a parent is reported as done](../../specs/product/learn.md#792a15j-a-write-to-a-parent-is-reported-as-done)), please's repository-change proposal ([792a18q A lesson about to be captured asks what should have surfaced it](../../specs/product/please.md#792a18q-a-lesson-about-to-be-captured-asks-what-should-have-surfaced-it)) and route's drowning report ([792a17n Drowning is reported, not patched](../../specs/product/route.md#792a17n-drowning-is-reported-not-patched)). Learn's report is the only user-facing report of learn that the canonical specification defines.
  - Recall's synthesis is not among them. The answer to [793c41 At a recall moment, what does the System put in front of the working agent?](793c_open-questions.md#793c41-at-a-recall-moment-what-does-the-system-put-in-front-of-the-working-agent) makes the agent, not the System, the author of the synthesis, so there is none for the System to show. [793c50 Does recall's synthesis to the user (792a14m) still apply, and who shows it?](793c_open-questions.md#793c50-does-recalls-synthesis-to-the-user-792a14m-still-apply-and-who-shows-it) asks the owner, and its recommendation is applied meanwhile.
  - A message is shown after the completed top-level turn in which it arose, once the System's work for the turn has finished, as the summary is, and is shown even when the turn has no counts. The answer does not say; it follows the summary's timing.
  - A message is shown only when its event occurred (`793a5i1e`).
  - The recommendation of [793c44 Where do the delegated skills' messages to the user go?](793c_open-questions.md#793c44-where-do-the-delegated-skills-messages-to-the-user-go), the display for items that ask the user to act and log only for routine reports, is not followed: the owner chose all of them.
  - `793a3o`, `793a7p` and `793a7u` point to `793a5i`, and `793a7u` no longer waits on the question.
- **Rationale:** the working agent is no longer the speaker of these messages, and the user still has to hear them.
- **Alternatives:** the options of [793c44 Where do the delegated skills' messages to the user go?](793c_open-questions.md#793c44-where-do-the-delegated-skills-messages-to-the-user-go): (a) log only, plus counts in the summary; (c) injected for the agent to relay, which puts memory bookkeeping back into the agent's context. Not chosen.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records the ID map ([793b142 Interpretations made in applying 793c41 to 793c44 and 793c48](#793b142-interpretations-made-in-applying-793c41-to-793c44-and-793c48)).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a3o`, `793a5i`, `793a5i1`, `793a5i1e`, `793a7p`, `793a7u`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b144 H1: Claude Code keeps delegate.md and a reduced shim, and loses the recall and learn skills and guidance

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation. For the 793c59 to 793c62 choice this entry carries: Decided by: author, shown to the owner in the walkthrough ([793b173 793c59 to 793c62: author decisions shown to the owner](#793b173-793c59-to-793c62-author-decisions-shown-to-the-owner)).
- **Finding:** [793c46 On Claude Code with the Integration installed, does engram update still deploy the recall and learn skills and guidance files?](793c_open-questions.md#793c46-on-claude-code-with-the-integration-installed-does-engram-update-still-deploy-the-recall-and-learn-skills-and-guidance-files) removes the recall and learn skills, their guidance and the shim from Claude Code, but [793c36 Does the shim's follow-frame apply to notes the System delivers?](793c_open-questions.md#793c36-does-the-shims-follow-frame-apply-to-notes-the-system-delivers) kept [792a19l Each returned kind is treated for what it is](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19l-each-returned-kind-is-treated-for-what-it-is) to [792a19s A signal of unrequested work is a standing instruction](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19s-a-signal-of-unrequested-work-is-a-standing-instruction) (the item-treatment rules, the follow-frame, the floor and the unrequested-work rule), which exist only in the shim, so nothing told the Claude Code agent how to apply what the System delivers. The setups of `793a7e`, `793a7f`, `793a7g` and `793a7v` gave the agent "the shim", whose lookup instructions the System replaces, and nothing said how a delivered chunk is fetched.
- **Fix applied:** applies [793c51 How does the Claude Code agent learn to apply delivered memory once the shim is gone?](793c_open-questions.md#793c51-how-does-the-claude-code-agent-learn-to-apply-delivered-memory-once-the-shim-is-gone) (a) and [793c52 Is delegate.md removed from Claude Code with recall.md and learn.md?](793c_open-questions.md#793c52-is-delegatemd-removed-from-claude-code-with-recallmd-and-learnmd) (a). `793a4h Installed by engram update and active in Claude Code`: on Claude Code, `engram init` and `engram update` do not deploy the recall and learn skills, `recall.md`, `learn.md` or the shim's lookup instructions ([792a19j The first action on every request is an engram query](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19j-the-first-action-on-every-request-is-an-engram-query), [792a19k The second phrase names a kind of work-handling decision](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19k-the-second-phrase-names-a-kind-of-work-handling-decision), [792a19t The query runs again at four moments mid-task](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19t-the-query-runs-again-at-four-moments-mid-task)), remove them where an earlier install deployed them, and deploy `delegate.md` and the reduced shim ([792a19l Each returned kind is treated for what it is](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19l-each-returned-kind-is-treated-for-what-it-is) to [792a19s A signal of unrequested work is a standing instruction](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19s-a-signal-of-unrequested-work-is-a-standing-instruction)). The term Reduced shim is defined in [793a1 Problem Statement](793a_product-spec.md#793a1-problem-statement). The setups of `793a7e`, `793a7f` and `793a7v` give the agent the reduced shim. The shim's escalation to a matched runbook's `deep` procedure is in [792a19t The query runs again at four moments mid-task](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19t-the-query-runs-again-at-four-moments-mid-task), which leaves the shim, so `793a7g` no longer has the shim tell the agent to use it, and scenario `793a7g1b` asserts the log's deep recall instead. `793a2d Delivered memory is attributable` says a delivered chunk is fetched by its id and that fetching a delivered item is not a memory command ([793b150 M2: a memory command is defined](#793b150-m2-a-memory-command-is-defined)), with scenario `793a2d1c`. Two points the finding leaves open, decided here by the author: `793a2d Delivered memory is attributable` also requires a delivered note to show its kind, because [792a19l Each returned kind is treated for what it is](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19l-each-returned-kind-is-treated-for-what-it-is) keys the treatment of an item on its kind; and `793a9b` keeps the opt-in of [792a11l Guidance is managed only after opt-in](../../specs/product/install-and-update.md#792a11l-guidance-is-managed-only-after-opt-in) for `delegate.md` and `shim.md`, so the reduced shim reaches an agent through the import the user adds ([793c58 Does engram init deploy Pi's guidance without --with-guidance?](793c_open-questions.md#793c58-does-engram-init-deploy-pis-guidance-without---with-guidance)).
- **Rationale:** the delegation of [793c29 Is the System a delegate for the existing memory skills?](793c_open-questions.md#793c29-is-the-system-a-delegate-for-the-existing-memory-skills) and [793c41 At a recall moment, what does the System put in front of the working agent?](793c_open-questions.md#793c41-at-a-recall-moment-what-does-the-system-put-in-front-of-the-working-agent) moves memory handling to the System and leaves applying memory with the agent, and the rules for applying must reach the agent. Routing (`delegate.md`) is not memory handling.
- **Alternatives:** the options of [793c51 How does the Claude Code agent learn to apply delivered memory once the shim is gone?](793c_open-questions.md#793c51-how-does-the-claude-code-agent-learn-to-apply-delivered-memory-once-the-shim-is-gone) ((b) the plan instruction carries the rules; (c) drop them on Claude Code) and [793c52 Is delegate.md removed from Claude Code with recall.md and learn.md?](793c_open-questions.md#793c52-is-delegatemd-removed-from-claude-code-with-recallmd-and-learnmd) ((b) remove all the guidance). Not chosen. Joe answered both (a) ([793b165 793c51: Claude Code keeps a reduced shim](#793b165-793c51-claude-code-keeps-a-reduced-shim) and [793b166 793c52: delegate.md stays on Claude Code](#793b166-793c52-delegatemd-stays-on-claude-code)).
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this review ([793b164 ID map for the sixth review](#793b164-id-map-for-the-sixth-review)).
- **Amended by [793b175 Memory is a dual system: automatic and intentional](#793b175-memory-is-a-dual-system-automatic-and-intentional) and [793b176 Dual memory: author decisions made in applying 793b175](#793b176-dual-memory-author-decisions-made-in-applying-793b175) (2026-10-11):** Claude Code keeps the recall and learn skills, and the shim keeps its query instructions as optional wording; the guidance documents `recall.md` and `learn.md` stay undeployed.
