## 793b145 H2: the install and guidance nodes get conditional operations, and the report names the imports to remove

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation. For the 793c59 to 793c62 choice this entry carries: Decided by: author, shown to the owner in the walkthrough ([793b173 793c59 to 793c62: author decisions shown to the owner](#793b173-793c59-to-793c62-author-decisions-shown-to-the-owner)).
- **Finding:** the deployment nodes [792a11h Skills are deployed to an engram-owned root and linked](../../specs/product/install-and-update.md#792a11h-skills-are-deployed-to-an-engram-owned-root-and-linked), [792a11l Guidance is managed only after opt-in](../../specs/product/install-and-update.md#792a11l-guidance-is-managed-only-after-opt-in) (and its scenario [792a11l1g One harness's import manages guidance for every harness](../../specs/product/install-and-update.md#792a11l1-uat-scenarios), by which one harness's import manages guidance for every harness), [792a11m Claude import paths keep working](../../specs/product/install-and-update.md#792a11m-claude-import-paths-keep-working), [792a19u The shim joins the other guidance additively](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19u-the-shim-joins-the-other-guidance-additively) and [792a19v The update report says how to activate guidance](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19v-the-update-report-says-how-to-activate-guidance) describe a Claude Code that deploys the recall and learn skills and guidance, which `793a4h Installed by engram update and active in Claude Code` now contradicts. The user's `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md` may still import the removed documents, and engram never edits that file.
- **Fix applied:** a section of conditional Modified operations, `793a9`: `793a9a` (retired) to `793a9e`, one for each node, each with an Integration branch, the canonical text as its "not installed" branch, an Integration scenario group and the canonical scenarios whose outcome differs ([793b147 H4: only the canonical scenarios whose outcome differs by branch are modified (revisits 793b117)](#793b147-h4-only-the-canonical-scenarios-whose-outcome-differs-by-branch-are-modified-revisits-793b117)). `793a9b` makes one harness's import (or the flag) unable to deploy `recall.md` or `learn.md` to Claude Code, and its scenario `793a9b1b` covers a Pi import. A new requirement, `793a4i`, has the update report name each import line of `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md` for a document no longer deployed there, and not edit that file. Decided here by the author: removal reaches `recall.md` and `learn.md` even where guidance is otherwise unmanaged (`793a9b1c`), because engram deployed them; and a real file at a flat path ([792a11m Claude import paths keep working](../../specs/product/install-and-update.md#792a11m-claude-import-paths-keep-working)) is left alone, as [792a11j Only what engram owns is deleted](../../specs/product/install-and-update.md#792a11j-only-what-engram-owns-is-deleted) requires (`793a9c1c`).
- **Rationale:** a Modified operation names one canonical target and keeps its ID, and the user must be told which lines to remove because only the user can edit the file.
- **Alternatives:** edit the user's `CLAUDE.md` for them. Not chosen: engram never edits it ([792a19v The update report says how to activate guidance](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19v-the-update-report-says-how-to-activate-guidance)).
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this review ([793b164 ID map for the sixth review](#793b164-id-map-for-the-sixth-review)).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites 9 IDs, each in the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction), which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a4h`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b146 H3: the System does not state recall's plan or perform its synthesis, reconciliation or re-entry query

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Finding:** [793a2n Relevant memory reaches the agent at the moments](793a_product-spec.md#793a2n-relevant-memory-reaches-the-agent-at-the-moments) (then `793a2a`) said the System "does not plan, synthesize or reconcile", but recall's re-entry query ([792a14o An emergent recommendation is checked against the vault](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14o-an-emergent-recommendation-is-checked-against-the-vault)), its persisting of conclusions ([792a14p A sound conclusion is persisted as a derived note](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14p-a-sound-conclusion-is-persisted-as-a-derived-note), [792a14q A cited synthesis is also captured as a question and answer](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14q-a-cited-synthesis-is-also-captured-as-a-question-and-answer)) and its curation statement ([792a14l Only the notes that were used are activated](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14l-only-the-notes-that-were-used-are-activated)) had no owner, and the recall skill that would have asked the agent for the synthesis is not deployed on Claude Code.
- **Fix applied:** applies [793c53 What asks the agent for the synthesis now that the recall skill is gone, and what becomes of the Re-entry line?](793c_open-questions.md#793c53-what-asks-the-agent-for-the-synthesis-now-that-the-recall-skill-is-gone-and-what-becomes-of-the-re-entry-line) (a) and [793c54 Who persists sound conclusions and cited question-and-answer notes at deep moments?](793c_open-questions.md#793c54-who-persists-sound-conclusions-and-cited-question-and-answer-notes-at-deep-moments) (a). [793a2n Relevant memory reaches the agent at the moments](793a_product-spec.md#793a2n-relevant-memory-reaches-the-agent-at-the-moments) carries the sentence "The System does not state recall's plan ([792a14a The plan is stated before anything is retrieved](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14a-the-plan-is-stated-before-anything-is-retrieved)) and does not perform its synthesis ([792a14m The closing synthesis says whether memory changed the plan](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14m-the-closing-synthesis-says-whether-memory-changed-the-plan)), reconciliation ([792a14n A recommendation is reconciled against the asked task](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14n-a-recommendation-is-reconciled-against-the-asked-task)) or re-entry query ([792a14o An emergent recommendation is checked against the vault](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14o-an-emergent-recommendation-is-checked-against-the-vault)); the working agent applies the delivered results in its own reasoning, as the plan instruction asks", and the plan instruction asks the agent to say in its response how the results changed its plan, without a count, raw scores or paths; the `Re-entry:` line is dropped. A new requirement, `793a3q Sound conclusions are persisted by the System`, has the System persist the agent's sound conclusions and cited question-and-answer notes at turn-end capture. The summary of [793a5h The user sees a summary of the memory activity](793a_product-spec.md#793a5h-the-user-sees-a-summary-of-the-memory-activity) gains an offers-curated count, and `793a5i` says it carries what [792a14l Only the notes that were used are activated](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14l-only-the-notes-that-were-used-are-activated) says about curation. Conditional Modified operations `793a8a` ([792a14a The plan is stated before anything is retrieved](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14a-the-plan-is-stated-before-anything-is-retrieved)), `793a8b` ([792a14m The closing synthesis says whether memory changed the plan](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14m-the-closing-synthesis-says-whether-memory-changed-the-plan)), `793a8c` ([792a14o An emergent recommendation is checked against the vault](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14o-an-emergent-recommendation-is-checked-against-the-vault)), `793a8d` ([792a14p A sound conclusion is persisted as a derived note](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14p-a-sound-conclusion-is-persisted-as-a-derived-note)) and `793a8e` ([792a14q A cited synthesis is also captured as a question and answer](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14q-a-cited-synthesis-is-also-captured-as-a-question-and-answer)) remove the contradictions. [792a14n A recommendation is reconciled against the asked task](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14n-a-recommendation-is-reconciled-against-the-asked-task) is not modified: the agent reconciles in its own reasoning under the target's text as written. [792a14l Only the notes that were used are activated](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14l-only-the-notes-that-were-used-are-activated) is not modified: the System curates after its recall (`793a2m`) and the count in the summary is what recall's reply would have said. Decided here by the author: the carried-over rules of [792a14o An emergent recommendation is checked against the vault](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14o-an-emergent-recommendation-is-checked-against-the-vault) for a recommendation that a delivered note records as closed apply to the agent's response (`793a8c`).
- **Rationale:** the System does not have the agent's context, so it cannot plan, synthesize or reconcile for the agent ([793b137 793c41: recall is split between the System and the agent](#793b137-793c41-recall-is-split-between-the-system-and-the-agent)); the agent does, and the System persists what is sound in the agent's response because the write is memory handling.
- **Alternatives:** the options of [793c53 What asks the agent for the synthesis now that the recall skill is gone, and what becomes of the Re-entry line?](793c_open-questions.md#793c53-what-asks-the-agent-for-the-synthesis-now-that-the-recall-skill-is-gone-and-what-becomes-of-the-re-entry-line) ((b) no synthesis is asked; (c) the plan instruction also requires the `Re-entry:` line) and [793c54 Who persists sound conclusions and cited question-and-answer notes at deep moments?](793c_open-questions.md#793c54-who-persists-sound-conclusions-and-cited-question-and-answer-notes-at-deep-moments) ((b) nobody persists while the Integration is installed). Not chosen. Joe answered both (a) ([793b167 793c53: the plan instruction asks for the account of the plan, and the Re-entry line is dropped](#793b167-793c53-the-plan-instruction-asks-for-the-account-of-the-plan-and-the-re-entry-line-is-dropped) and [793b168 793c54: the System persists deep conclusions at turn-end capture](#793b168-793c54-the-system-persists-deep-conclusions-at-turn-end-capture)).
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this review ([793b164 ID map for the sixth review](#793b164-id-map-for-the-sixth-review)).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites 7 IDs, each in the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction), which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a3q`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b147 H4: only the canonical scenarios whose outcome differs by branch are modified (revisits 793b117)

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Finding:** [793b117 H4: every Modified target keeps its canonical scenarios](#793b117-h4-every-modified-target-keeps-its-canonical-scenarios) gave every operation of `793a6` and `793a7` a group of Modified operations for all of its target's canonical scenarios, prefixing each with "Given the Integration is not installed". That modified scenarios whose outcome is the same in both branches, and the Integration branches restated the target's text.
- **Fix applied:** a canonical scenario is modified only when it cannot hold as written when the Integration is installed: its steps name a mechanism the Integration removes or hands to the System (a `LESSONS:` line, the closing learn, the agent's or the orchestrator's recall, learn, query, count or write), or its outcome differs. The rows of every other scenario are removed from the tables, so the scenario, unmodified, covers both branches. Of 113 rows, 91 remain and 22 are retired, and their operation IDs are never reused: `793a6h` `793a6h2b`; `793a6i` `793a6i2b`; `793a7h` `793a7h2a`, `793a7h2b`, `793a7h2c` (with its group `793a7h2`); `793a7i` `793a7i2a`, `793a7i2b`, `793a7i2d`; `793a7j` `793a7j2b`; `793a7l` `793a7l2c`, `793a7l2d`, `793a7l2e`, `793a7l2f`; `793a7m` `793a7m2c`; `793a7n` `793a7n2b`, `793a7n2c`, `793a7n2d`; `793a7p` `793a7p2c`; `793a7q` `793a7q2c`; `793a7s` `793a7s2b`; `793a7v` `793a7v2b`, `793a7v2c`. The same criterion fixed the groups of the new operations `793a8` and `793a9`. Each Integration branch is trimmed to what differs, and ends with "The rest of the target applies unchanged." where something of the target remains; the sentence of the `793a7` preamble that had each branch restate the moments and criteria that decide when recall or learn fires is removed, because the branches say who performs them and the target keeps the moments. The preambles of `793a6` and `793a7` state the rule.
- **Rationale:** the lifecycle guide requires a Modified operation to give the target's complete replacement text, and a scenario that holds in both branches needs no second copy.
- **Alternatives:** keep all 113 rows. Not chosen. Keep the Integration branches complete. Not chosen: two copies of the target's text drift.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this review ([793b164 ID map for the sixth review](#793b164-id-map-for-the-sixth-review)). [793b117 H4: every Modified target keeps its canonical scenarios](#793b117-h4-every-modified-target-keeps-its-canonical-scenarios) is superseded by this entry.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites 16 IDs, each in the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction), which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b148 H5: 793a4h is trimmed to the Claude Code delta, and change 795 is referenced

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Finding:** `793a4h Installed by engram update and active in Claude Code` specified what `engram init` does (the vault, idempotence, a second run changing nothing), which change #795 owns ([793b136 Change 793 depends on change 795 being widened](#793b136-change-793-depends-on-change-795-being-widened), [793b141 793c48: change 795 is widened and this change builds on it](#793b141-793c48-change-795-is-widened-and-this-change-builds-on-it)).
- **Fix applied:** `793a4h Installed by engram update and active in Claude Code` states only what differs on the Claude Code harness. Scenario `793a4h1f` drops its vault clause, and `793a4h1g Init gives Claude Code the Integration` drops its second-run clause. The rationale cites #795's change directory, `docs/changes/795_vault-init-command/`, and the requirements to be widened, [795a2a Init creates a new vault](../795_vault-init-command/795a_product-spec.md#795a2a-init-creates-a-new-vault), [795a3a Existing vaults are left alone by init](../795_vault-init-command/795a_product-spec.md#795a3a-existing-vaults-are-left-alone-by-init), [795a6a Update skips its vault steps](../795_vault-init-command/795a_product-spec.md#795a6a-update-skips-its-vault-steps) and [795a6b Update never creates or repairs a vault](../795_vault-init-command/795a_product-spec.md#795a6b-update-never-creates-or-repairs-a-vault). #795 is not edited.
- **Blocker:** `793a4h Installed by engram update and active in Claude Code` cannot be promoted until #795 widens those four requirements so that `engram init` sets up everything idempotently and `engram update` runs it: until then #795 still says init creates only the vault and update never creates one.
- **Rationale:** one change owns `engram init` ([793b141 793c48: change 795 is widened and this change builds on it](#793b141-793c48-change-795-is-widened-and-this-change-builds-on-it)), and a requirement stated in two changes would disagree once either changes.
- **Alternatives:** keep init's behavior in this change. Not chosen: the owner chose to widen #795 ([793c48 How is the widening of `engram init` (change #795) sequenced with this change?](793c_open-questions.md#793c48-how-is-the-widening-of-engram-init-change-795-sequenced-with-this-change)).
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this review ([793b164 ID map for the sixth review](#793b164-id-map-for-the-sixth-review)).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a4h1f`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

- **Amended by:** [793b186 793a4h is delivered by this change and does not depend on #795](793b_decision-log.md#793b186-793a4h-is-delivered-by-this-change-and-does-not-depend-on-795): the Blocker line and the reference to scenario `793a4h1g` no longer hold: 793a4h is not blocked on #795 and `793a4h1g` is retired (the entry itself is left as written).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a4h`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b149 M1: please's orientation is a recall moment

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.

- **Rationale:** the requirement that says when recall is performed names every moment ([793b120 M4: every recall moment is listed](#793b120-m4-every-recall-moment-is-listed)).

## 793b150 M2: a memory command is defined

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Finding:** `793a2k Intentional memory stays available and is never blocked` says the agent runs no "memory command" without defining it, while [792a19l Each returned kind is treated for what it is](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19l-each-returned-kind-is-treated-for-what-it-is) has the agent fetch a chunk with `engram show-chunk`.
- **Fix applied:** the term Memory command in [793a1 Problem Statement](793a_product-spec.md#793a1-problem-statement): running recall, learn, curate or write-memory, or an engram command that queries, writes, amends, activates or sweeps; reading an item that was already delivered, such as fetching a delivered chunk by its id, is not one. `793a2d Delivered memory is attributable` and scenario `793a2d1c` use it.
- **Rationale:** the agent must be able to look at what the System delivered.
- **Alternatives:** forbid every engram command. Not chosen: it contradicts [792a19l Each returned kind is treated for what it is](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19l-each-returned-kind-is-treated-for-what-it-is).
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this review ([793b164 ID map for the sixth review](#793b164-id-map-for-the-sixth-review)).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a2d1c`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a2d`, `793a2k`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b151 M3: the Integration branches state outcomes, not the System in the agent's place

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Finding:** the Integration branches of `793a7e`, `793a7q`, `793a7r`, `793a7t` and `793a7u` said the System follows the branch below "in the agent's place (except its sentences addressed to" the agent), which makes the System obey sentences written for an agent, and their scenarios asserted internals.
- **Fix applied:** each branch names the outcomes that carry over (the lookup's text and phrases, the evidence note's slug, tags and fields, the aggregate's uniqueness, tally and links, the recount and the lookup after each aggregate write). Their scenarios assert vault and log outcomes: `793a7q1c`, `793a7q1d`, `793a7r1c`, `793a7r1d` and `793a7r1e` are rewritten, `793a7e1d` is added, and `793a7t1a`, `793a7u1a` and `793a7u1b` are retimed ([793b152 M4: the count audit and the drowning check run after each aggregate write](#793b152-m4-the-count-audit-and-the-drowning-check-run-after-each-aggregate-write)).
- **Rationale:** a scenario states what a user can inspect, and a requirement should not depend on how the target happens to be worded.
- **Alternatives:** keep the formula. Not chosen.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this review ([793b164 ID map for the sixth review](#793b164-id-map-for-the-sixth-review)).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites 19 IDs, each in the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction), which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b152 M4: the count audit and the drowning check run after each aggregate write

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Finding:** `793a7t` and `793a7u` said the System audits "at the same moments as" the orchestrator would, which are when a tally is doubted and at periodic consolidation, and the orchestrator no longer decides either.
- **Fix applied:** applies [793c56 When does the System run route's count audit and drowning check?](793c_open-questions.md#793c56-when-does-the-system-run-routes-count-audit-and-drowning-check) (a): the System recounts and checks the lookup after each aggregate write.
- **Rationale:** an aggregate goes wrong, or is drowned, only after a write.
- **Alternatives:** the options of [793c56 When does the System run route's count audit and drowning check?](793c_open-questions.md#793c56-when-does-the-system-run-routes-count-audit-and-drowning-check) ((b) once per session; (c) on a schedule). Not chosen. Joe answered (a) ([793b170 793c56: the count audit and the drowning check follow each aggregate write](#793b170-793c56-the-count-audit-and-the-drowning-check-follow-each-aggregate-write)).
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this review ([793b164 ID map for the sixth review](#793b164-id-map-for-the-sixth-review)).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a7t`, `793a7u`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b153 M5: a moment noticed late is delivered and logged late

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Finding:** `793a2c` had the System notice moments without the agent but did not say what happens to a moment it notices only after the agent has acted.
- **Fix applied:** applies [793c55 What happens when the System notices a recall moment only after the agent has acted?](793c_open-questions.md#793c55-what-happens-when-the-system-notices-a-recall-moment-only-after-the-agent-has-acted) (a). `793a2c` delivers the results with the plan instruction before the agent's next response or tool call and logs the moment as late; `793a5e` records whether the moment was late; scenario `793a2c1g`.
- **Rationale:** the results still bear on the agent's next step.
- **Alternatives:** the option of [793c55 What happens when the System notices a recall moment only after the agent has acted?](793c_open-questions.md#793c55-what-happens-when-the-system-notices-a-recall-moment-only-after-the-agent-has-acted) (b), drop it and log it. Not chosen. Joe answered (a) ([793b169 793c55: a moment noticed late is delivered and logged late](#793b169-793c55-a-moment-noticed-late-is-delivered-and-logged-late)).
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this review ([793b164 ID map for the sixth review](#793b164-id-map-for-the-sixth-review)).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a2c`, `793a2c1`, `793a2c1g`, `793a5e`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b154 M6: runbook notes that mirror the memory skills are not delivered

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Finding:** a vault can hold runbook notes that mirror the recall and learn skills, such as `pi:recall`, and a delivered runbook is executed by the agent, so it would ask the agent to run memory work.
- **Fix applied:** applies [793c57 While the Integration is installed, are runbook notes that mirror the recall and learn skills delivered?](793c_open-questions.md#793c57-while-the-integration-is-installed-are-runbook-notes-that-mirror-the-recall-and-learn-skills-delivered) (a). `793a2k Intentional memory stays available and is never blocked` says the System does not deliver a runbook note that mirrors the recall, learn, curate or write-memory skill; scenario `793a2k1b` (retired).
- **Rationale:** `793a2k Intentional memory stays available and is never blocked` keeps the agent free of memory work.
- **Alternatives:** the option of [793c57 While the Integration is installed, are runbook notes that mirror the recall and learn skills delivered?](793c_open-questions.md#793c57-while-the-integration-is-installed-are-runbook-notes-that-mirror-the-recall-and-learn-skills-delivered) (b), deliver them. Not chosen. Joe answered (a) ([793b171 793c57: runbook notes that mirror the memory skills are not delivered](#793b171-793c57-runbook-notes-that-mirror-the-memory-skills-are-not-delivered)).
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this review ([793b164 ID map for the sixth review](#793b164-id-map-for-the-sixth-review)).
- **Amended by [793b175 Memory is a dual system: automatic and intentional](#793b175-memory-is-a-dual-system-automatic-and-intentional) (2026-10-11):** the fix applied here is reversed: `793a2k Intentional memory stays available and is never blocked` delivers such a note like any other.

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a2k`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b155 M7: Pi scenarios state that Pi imports the guidance

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Finding:** `793a4g1d`, `793a4h1d One update serves both harnesses differently` and `793a4h1g Init gives Claude Code the Integration` assert that Pi has the guidance, but guidance is deployed only after an opt-in ([792a11l Guidance is managed only after opt-in](../../specs/product/install-and-update.md#792a11l-guidance-is-managed-only-after-opt-in)).
- **Fix applied:** their setups state that `~/.pi/agent/AGENTS.md` imports the recall, learn and shim guidance. This applies [793c58 Does engram init deploy Pi's guidance without --with-guidance?](793c_open-questions.md#793c58-does-engram-init-deploy-pis-guidance-without---with-guidance) (a): [792a11l Guidance is managed only after opt-in](../../specs/product/install-and-update.md#792a11l-guidance-is-managed-only-after-opt-in) keeps its opt-in for Pi.
- **Rationale:** a scenario's setup must make its outcome true.
- **Alternatives:** the option of [793c58 Does engram init deploy Pi's guidance without --with-guidance?](793c_open-questions.md#793c58-does-engram-init-deploy-pis-guidance-without---with-guidance) (b), init deploys Pi's guidance without the opt-in, and [792a11l Guidance is managed only after opt-in](../../specs/product/install-and-update.md#792a11l-guidance-is-managed-only-after-opt-in) is modified. Not chosen. Joe answered (a) ([793b172 793c58: Pi's guidance stays opt-in](#793b172-793c58-pis-guidance-stays-opt-in)).
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this review ([793b164 ID map for the sixth review](#793b164-id-map-for-the-sixth-review)).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a4g1`, `793a4g1d`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a4h1d`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b157 M9: recall at the dispatch moments runs at its default depth, and the tier evidence is what recall surfaces

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation. For the 793c59 to 793c62 choice this entry carries: Decided by: author, shown to the owner in the walkthrough ([793b173 793c59 to 793c62: author decisions shown to the owner](#793b173-793c59-to-793c62-author-decisions-shown-to-the-owner)).
- **Finding:** `793a2c` did not say at what depth the System recalls at the two dispatch moments, and promised the work-kind's tier evidence even when recall did not surface it.
- **Fix applied:** `793a2c` delivers "what recall returns for the unit, which includes the work-kind's tier evidence whenever recall surfaces it" and states that recall at please's orientation and at the two dispatch moments runs at recall's default depth ([792a14c Recall has two depths](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14c-recall-has-two-depths): deep); scenario `793a2c1f`. Decided here by the author: please's orientation is stated at the default depth too, because [792a18d The workflow opens with learn and orients with recall](../../specs/product/please.md#792a18d-the-workflow-opens-with-learn-and-orients-with-recall) names no depth.
- **Rationale:** the System cannot promise evidence that is not in the vault.
- **Alternatives:** recall at glance depth at the dispatch moments. Not chosen: the finding states the default.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this review ([793b164 ID map for the sixth review](#793b164-id-map-for-the-sixth-review)).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a2c`, `793a2c1`, `793a2c1f`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b161 L4: scenarios say what the requirements say

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation.
- **Finding:** `793a4h1g Init gives Claude Code the Integration` asserted that the second run "says so" where the requirement did not; `793a3m1c` and `793a6k1a` asserted that a report carries no LESSONS line where the requirement says it is not asked for; `793a7c`'s "does not wait" had no scenario.
- **Fix applied:** `793a4h1g Init gives Claude Code the Integration`'s second-run clause is gone with the init behavior that #795 owns ([793b148 H5: 793a4h is trimmed to the Claude Code delta, and change 795 is referenced](#793b148-h5-793a4h-is-trimmed-to-the-claude-code-delta-and-change-795-is-referenced)); `793a3m1c` says the subagent's instructions do not ask for a LESSONS line and its report is not required to carry one; `793a6k1a` says the subagent was not asked for one; scenario `793a7c1e` covers "does not wait".
- **Rationale:** a scenario must not assert more than its requirement.
- **Alternatives:** none.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this review ([793b164 ID map for the sixth review](#793b164-id-map-for-the-sixth-review)).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites 7 IDs, each in the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction), which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b165 793c51: Claude Code keeps a reduced shim

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c51 How does the Claude Code agent learn to apply delivered memory once the shim is gone?](793c_open-questions.md#793c51-how-does-the-claude-code-agent-learn-to-apply-delivered-memory-once-the-shim-is-gone) (a): Claude Code gets a reduced shim containing only the item-treatment, follow-frame, floor and unrequested-work rules; the lookup instructions are removed.
- **Choice:** the recommended option is kept as written. Affected requirements: `793a4h Installed by engram update and active in Claude Code`, `793a9d`, `793a9e`, `793a7e`, `793a7f`, `793a7g`, `793a7v`. They already state it ([793b144 H1: Claude Code keeps delegate.md and a reduced shim, and loses the recall and learn skills and guidance](#793b144-h1-claude-code-keeps-delegatemd-and-a-reduced-shim-and-loses-the-recall-and-learn-skills-and-guidance)), so recording the answer changes none of them.
- **Rationale:** the owner chose the recommended option: the rules for applying a delivered item stay where the follow-frame already lives, and only what the System took over is removed.
- **Alternatives:** (b) the plan instruction carries the rules; (c) drop them on Claude Code. Not chosen by the owner.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records the answers ([793b173 793c59 to 793c62: author decisions shown to the owner](#793b173-793c59-to-793c62-author-decisions-shown-to-the-owner)).
- **Amended by [793b175 Memory is a dual system: automatic and intentional](#793b175-memory-is-a-dual-system-automatic-and-intentional) (2026-10-11):** the reduced shim keeps the lookup instructions, as optional wording, instead of removing them. The original choice above stands as recorded.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a7e`, `793a7f`, `793a7g`, `793a7v`, `793a9d`, `793a9e`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b167 793c53: the plan instruction asks for the account of the plan, and the Re-entry line is dropped

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c53 What asks the agent for the synthesis now that the recall skill is gone, and what becomes of the Re-entry line?](793c_open-questions.md#793c53-what-asks-the-agent-for-the-synthesis-now-that-the-recall-skill-is-gone-and-what-becomes-of-the-re-entry-line) (a): the plan instruction also asks the agent to say in its response how the results changed its plan (no count, raw scores or paths), and the `Re-entry:` line is dropped.
- **Choice:** the recommended option is kept as written. Affected requirements: [793a2n Relevant memory reaches the agent at the moments](793a_product-spec.md#793a2n-relevant-memory-reaches-the-agent-at-the-moments), `793a8b`, `793a8c`. - **Rationale:** the agent has the context to give the account the synthesis gave, and the count is the System's.

- **Status:** applied in the commit that records the answers ([793b173 793c59 to 793c62: author decisions shown to the owner](#793b173-793c59-to-793c62-author-decisions-shown-to-the-owner)).

## 793b169 793c55: a moment noticed late is delivered and logged late

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c55 What happens when the System notices a recall moment only after the agent has acted?](793c_open-questions.md#793c55-what-happens-when-the-system-notices-a-recall-moment-only-after-the-agent-has-acted) (a): a moment noticed only after the agent has acted is delivered, with the plan instruction, before the agent's next response or tool call, and logged as late.
- **Choice:** the recommended option is kept as written. Affected requirements: `793a2c`, `793a5e`. They already state it ([793b153 M5: a moment noticed late is delivered and logged late](#793b153-m5-a-moment-noticed-late-is-delivered-and-logged-late)), so recording the answer changes none of them.
- **Rationale:** the results still bear on the agent's next step, and the late record shows how often it happens.
- **Alternatives:** (b) drop it and log it. Not chosen by the owner.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records the answers ([793b173 793c59 to 793c62: author decisions shown to the owner](#793b173-793c59-to-793c62-author-decisions-shown-to-the-owner)).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a2c`, `793a5e`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b170 793c56: the count audit and the drowning check follow each aggregate write

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), [793c56 When does the System run route's count audit and drowning check?](793c_open-questions.md#793c56-when-does-the-system-run-routes-count-audit-and-drowning-check) (a): the System runs the count audit and the drowning check after each aggregate write.

They already state it ([793b152 M4: the count audit and the drowning check run after each aggregate write](#793b152-m4-the-count-audit-and-the-drowning-check-run-after-each-aggregate-write)), so recording the answer changes none of them.
- **Rationale:** an aggregate is wrong, or drowned, only after a write, so the check follows the event that can cause it.
