## 793b173 793c59 to 793c62: author decisions shown to the owner

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); shown to the owner in the walkthrough, who can overrule it. [793c59 Does a delivered note show its kind?](793c_open-questions.md#793c59-does-a-delivered-note-show-its-kind) to [793c62 At what depth does please's orientation recall run?](793c_open-questions.md#793c62-at-what-depth-does-pleases-orientation-recall-run) were withdrawn as questions under R3 ([793b174 Review rules R1 to R3 apply to this change](#793b174-review-rules-r1-to-r3-apply-to-this-change)).
- **Choice:** four decisions made beyond the brief, first raised as [793c59 Does a delivered note show its kind?](793c_open-questions.md#793c59-does-a-delivered-note-show-its-kind) to [793c62 At what depth does please's orientation recall run?](793c_open-questions.md#793c62-at-what-depth-does-pleases-orientation-recall-run), each with the choice already applied as its recommendation: a delivered note shows its kind ([793b144 H1: Claude Code keeps delegate.md and a reduced shim, and loses the recall and learn skills and guidance](#793b144-h1-claude-code-keeps-delegatemd-and-a-reduced-shim-and-loses-the-recall-and-learn-skills-and-guidance), `793a2d Delivered memory is attributable`); the sentence of [792a19t The query runs again at four moments mid-task](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19t-the-query-runs-again-at-four-moments-mid-task) that escalates to a runbook's deep procedure leaves the shim with the lookup ([793b144 H1: Claude Code keeps delegate.md and a reduced shim, and loses the recall and learn skills and guidance](#793b144-h1-claude-code-keeps-delegatemd-and-a-reduced-shim-and-loses-the-recall-and-learn-skills-and-guidance), `793a7g`); a real file at a flat guidance path is left alone by install ([793b145 H2: the install and guidance nodes get conditional operations, and the report names the imports to remove](#793b145-h2-the-install-and-guidance-nodes-get-conditional-operations-and-the-report-names-the-imports-to-remove), `793a9c`); and please's orientation recall runs at recall's default depth ([793b157 M9: recall at the dispatch moments runs at its default depth, and the tier evidence is what recall surfaces](#793b157-m9-recall-at-the-dispatch-moments-runs-at-its-default-depth-and-the-tier-evidence-is-what-recall-surfaces), `793a2c`). This entry also records the answers to [793c51 How does the Claude Code agent learn to apply delivered memory once the shim is gone?](793c_open-questions.md#793c51-how-does-the-claude-code-agent-learn-to-apply-delivered-memory-once-the-shim-is-gone) to [793c58 Does engram init deploy Pi's guidance without --with-guidance?](793c_open-questions.md#793c58-does-engram-init-deploy-pis-guidance-without---with-guidance) as [793b165 793c51: Claude Code keeps a reduced shim](#793b165-793c51-claude-code-keeps-a-reduced-shim) to [793b172 793c58: Pi's guidance stays opt-in](#793b172-793c58-pis-guidance-stays-opt-in). Checked against `793a4h Installed by engram update and active in Claude Code`, `793a2c`, [793a2n Relevant memory reaches the agent at the moments](793a_product-spec.md#793a2n-relevant-memory-reaches-the-agent-at-the-moments), `793a2k Intentional memory stays available and is never blocked` and the other requirements those entries name: each already states option (a), so none was changed.
- **Rationale:** the documentation rules require a decision made by the author to be shown to the owner, and an owner's answer to be recorded in the commit that applies it. Each choice follows a standing owner rule (keep today's behavior, delegate only what the System took over) and blocks nothing, so under R3 it is recorded as an author decision, not asked.
- **Alternatives:** leave the four decisions recorded only in [793b144 H1: Claude Code keeps delegate.md and a reduced shim, and loses the recall and learn skills and guidance](#793b144-h1-claude-code-keeps-delegatemd-and-a-reduced-shim-and-loses-the-recall-and-learn-skills-and-guidance), [793b145 H2: the install and guidance nodes get conditional operations, and the report names the imports to remove](#793b145-h2-the-install-and-guidance-nodes-get-conditional-operations-and-the-report-names-the-imports-to-remove) and [793b157 M9: recall at the dispatch moments runs at its default depth, and the tier evidence is what recall surfaces](#793b157-m9-recall-at-the-dispatch-moments-runs-at-its-default-depth-and-the-tier-evidence-is-what-recall-surfaces). Not chosen: those entries mark them in running text, where the owner would not see them in the walkthrough.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records these answers.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a2c`, `793a7g`, `793a9c`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a2d`, `793a2k`, `793a4h`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b174 Review rules R1 to R3 apply to this change

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), 2026-10-11: R1, R2 and R3, now in [Decisions and Open Questions](../../AGENTS.md#decisions-and-open-questions).
- **Choice:** R1: this change spec has had its one agent review round (the sixth, [793b143 Sixth review: findings accepted, and 793c51 to 793c58 opened](#793b143-sixth-review-findings-accepted-and-793c51-to-793c58-opened)), so it goes to the owner walkthrough without another. R2: every item known to need the owner is presented in one batch. R3: a question is raised only for a contradiction, an untestable requirement or an undecided behavior change; [793c59 Does a delivered note show its kind?](793c_open-questions.md#793c59-does-a-delivered-note-show-its-kind) to [793c62 At what depth does please's orientation recall run?](793c_open-questions.md#793c62-at-what-depth-does-pleases-orientation-recall-run) are withdrawn and recorded as author decisions in [793b173 793c59 to 793c62: author decisions shown to the owner](#793b173-793c59-to-793c62-author-decisions-shown-to-the-owner). No requirement in 793a changes.
- **Rationale:** the owner capped the review rounds and the question stream.
- **Alternatives:** keep the four as open questions. Not chosen by the owner.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that withdraws 793c59 to 793c62.

## 793b175 Memory is a dual system: automatic and intentional

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), 2026-10-11.
- **Choice:** memory is a dual system. Automatic memory: the System surfaces and captures at the moments the agent reliably misses, and everything in [793a2 Added: automatic-memory (it surfaces)](793a_product-spec.md#793a2-added-automatic-memory-it-surfaces) through [793a5 Added: automatic-memory (it is just there, and both channels stay)](793a_product-spec.md#793a5-added-automatic-memory-it-is-just-there-and-both-channels-stay) stays. Intentional memory: the working agent, or the user, may still run recall, learn, `engram query` and the other memory commands deliberately whenever they want ("remember this", `/recall`, a mid-task query). The earlier text wrongly made automatic memory exclusive by banning the agent from running memory commands. Applied as follows: [793a1 Problem Statement](793a_product-spec.md#793a1-problem-statement) reframes the problem and defines automatic and intentional memory; `793a2k Intentional memory stays available and is never blocked` is rewritten so that the System MUST NOT require the agent to run memory commands while the agent MAY run any, the System MUST NOT block or undo such a run, and a runbook note mirroring a memory skill is delivered like any other note (reversing [793b171 793c57: runbook notes that mirror the memory skills are not delivered](#793b171-793c57-runbook-notes-that-mirror-the-memory-skills-are-not-delivered)); [793a2e Nothing already in context is delivered again](793a_product-spec.md#793a2e-nothing-already-in-context-is-delivered-again) covers an item already surfaced by an intentional recall (`793a2e1m`); the Integration branches of `793a6`, `793a7` and `793a8` change from a prohibition ("MUST NOT run") to "is not required to … because the System performs it" and keep every step the System performs; the reduced shim keeps today's `engram query` wording as optional (`793a7e`, `793a7f`, `793a7g`, `793a4h Installed by engram update and active in Claude Code`); `793a6a` adds that a report which still carries a `LESSONS:` line is not an error; the answers to [793c51 How does the Claude Code agent learn to apply delivered memory once the shim is gone?](793c_open-questions.md#793c51-how-does-the-claude-code-agent-learn-to-apply-delivered-memory-once-the-shim-is-gone) and [793c57 While the Integration is installed, are runbook notes that mirror the recall and learn skills delivered?](793c_open-questions.md#793c57-while-the-integration-is-installed-are-runbook-notes-that-mirror-the-recall-and-learn-skills-delivered) are amended.
- **Rationale:** the owner's statement of the product: the System covers the moments the agent misses, and the agent and the user keep deliberate control. Banning the agent from memory commands removes the user's "remember this" and any mid-task lookup, which the System cannot anticipate.
- **Alternatives:** keep automatic memory exclusive (the previous text). Rejected by the owner.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this decision ([793b176 Dual memory: author decisions made in applying 793b175](#793b176-dual-memory-author-decisions-made-in-applying-793b175)).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites 8 IDs, each in the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction), which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason); the entry says everything in `793a2` through `793a5` stays; that decision keeps only the minimal set of requirements in those sections and removes the Modified sections `793a6` to `793a9`.

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a2k`, `793a4h`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b176 Dual memory: author decisions made in applying 793b175

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); shown to the owner in the walkthrough, who can overrule it. Each follows from [793b175 Memory is a dual system: automatic and intentional](#793b175-memory-is-a-dual-system-automatic-and-intentional) and blocks nothing.
- **Choice:**
  1. Claude Code keeps the memory skills. `/recall` and `/learn` cannot be invoked intentionally on Claude Code unless the skills are deployed there, so `793a4h Installed by engram update and active in Claude Code` now deploys them in addition to the Integration. This reverses the answer to [793c46 On Claude Code with the Integration installed, does engram update still deploy the recall and learn skills and guidance files?](793c_open-questions.md#793c46-on-claude-code-with-the-integration-installed-does-engram-update-still-deploy-the-recall-and-learn-skills-and-guidance-files) (b) and takes its option (a): the skills stay, and the guidance documents `recall.md` and `learn.md`, which tell the agent to run recall and learn at the moments the System handles, stay undeployed and are removed where an earlier install left them. `792a11h` (the Integration branch of the skills deployment) no longer differs from the canonical text, so its Modified operation `793a9a` and its scenario groups are retired. `793a9b` to `793a9e` are unchanged. Amended by [793b179 B2: Claude Code deploys the recall and learn skills with descriptions narrowed to deliberate use](#793b179-b2-claude-code-deploys-the-recall-and-learn-skills-with-descriptions-narrowed-to-deliberate-use): the deployed descriptions of the recall and learn skills are narrowed to deliberate use on Claude Code.
  2. The reduced shim is today's shim with its mandatory wording made optional, as the owner directed. That includes the sentence of `793a7g` that escalates to a matched runbook's `deep` procedure, which [793b173 793c59 to 793c62: author decisions shown to the owner](#793b173-793c59-to-793c62-author-decisions-shown-to-the-owner) had removed from the shim; it returns as optional, and the shim's phrase-shaping instructions (`793a7f`) return for a query the agent chooses to run.
  3. A report that still ends with a `LESSONS:` line is not an error (`793a6a1b`), and the capture of the lesson still comes from the subagent's session (`793a3m`).
  4. Prohibitions on the agent became "is not required to" in the Integration branches of the nodes that named the agent, the orchestrator or please as not running recall, learn, `engram query`, `engram count`, an amend, a vault search or an audit: `792a16c`, `792a16d`, `792a16e`, `792a16f`, `792a16g`, `792a17a`, `792a17b`, `792a17g`, `792a17h`, `792a17k`, `792a17l`, `792a17m`, `792a17n`, `792a17o`, `792a18c`, `792a18d`, `792a18n`, `792a18p`, `792a18q`, `792a18r`, `792a19e`, `792a14a`, `792a14o` and `792a16b`. Their canonical text is unchanged in the "not installed" branch. Obligations on the System (for example writing the evidence note and keeping the aggregate) are unchanged. One consequence: an evidence note or aggregate the orchestrator writes intentionally is not prevented, and the aggregate recount of `793a7t` recomputes tallies from the evidence notes, so a duplicate evidence note would be counted. Amended by [793b180 B3: duplicate writes are deduplicated like injections](#793b180-b3-duplicate-writes-are-deduplicated-like-injections): the System writes no second note for what an earlier note already records, whoever wrote it, and the recount counts each dispatch once, so a duplicate is not counted.
  5. Scenario assertions that "the agent ran no memory command" became "no request to the agent to run it" (or "was not asked to"). Where the scenario's point was that the System did the work, the work is still asserted.
  6. Retired scenario IDs, each replaced by a new one: `793a2k1b` by `793a2k1f`, `793a7f1a` by `793a7f1c`, `793a4h1e` by `793a4h1i`. Retitled for accuracy (IDs kept): `793a2n1f`, `793a2k1a The agent is neither required nor asked to run a memory command`, `793a7o1d`, `793a7p1a`, `793a7g1a`, `793a7h1a`, `793a7j1a`, `793a7n1a`. Headings retitled (IDs kept): `793a2k Intentional memory stays available and is never blocked`, `793a7`. New scenarios: `793a2k1c An intentional recall by the agent is allowed`, `793a2k1d An intentional learn by the agent is allowed and is not undone`, `793a2k1e An intentional engram query by the agent is allowed`, `793a2e1m`, `793a6a1b`, `793a7e1e`, `793a7e1f`, `793a7g1d`.
  7. A recall or a response that the agent produces from its own intentional run follows the canonical text unchanged (`793a7`, `793a8` introductions); `793a8b` applies to results the System delivered.
- **Rationale:** the owner directed the dual model but not these consequences; each is the smallest change that makes the model workable and consistent with the earlier owner answers it touches.
- **Alternatives:** (1) keep the skills removed, which makes `/recall` and `/learn` impossible on Claude Code and contradicts [793b175 Memory is a dual system: automatic and intentional](#793b175-memory-is-a-dual-system-automatic-and-intentional); (2) leave the escalation sentence out of the shim; (4) keep "MUST NOT" for the route bookkeeping writes, which would again ban an agent from a memory command.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this decision.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites 33 IDs, each in the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction), which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites 6 IDs, each in the ID map, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b177 Seventh review: findings accepted, and B1 to B3 decided under the owner's rules

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus, round 7); its findings accepted by the author. This is the one review round for the dual-memory rework ([793b174 Review rules R1 to R3 apply to this change](#793b174-review-rules-r1-to-r3-apply-to-this-change), [793b175 Memory is a dual system: automatic and intentional](#793b175-memory-is-a-dual-system-automatic-and-intentional)), so the change goes to the owner walkthrough after it. Its three questions were answered by the orchestrator under the owner's standing rules ([793b178 B1: the reduced shim exempts a delivered memory runbook from the follow-frame](#793b178-b1-the-reduced-shim-exempts-a-delivered-memory-runbook-from-the-follow-frame), [793b179 B2: Claude Code deploys the recall and learn skills with descriptions narrowed to deliberate use](#793b179-b2-claude-code-deploys-the-recall-and-learn-skills-with-descriptions-narrowed-to-deliberate-use), [793b180 B3: duplicate writes are deduplicated like injections](#793b180-b3-duplicate-writes-are-deduplicated-like-injections)); no question is added to `793c_open-questions.md`.
- **Choice:** the findings and where each is applied:
  - B1: a delivered runbook that mirrors a memory skill would match almost any task and, under the shim's follow-frame, make the System's delivery require the agent to run recall or learn. Applied as [793b178 B1: the reduced shim exempts a delivered memory runbook from the follow-frame](#793b178-b1-the-reduced-shim-exempts-a-delivered-memory-runbook-from-the-follow-frame).
  - B2: skills deployed unchanged have descriptions that prompt use at the System's moments, which contradicts dropping `recall.md` and `learn.md` and makes "no request to the agent" fail. Applied as [793b179 B2: Claude Code deploys the recall and learn skills with descriptions narrowed to deliberate use](#793b179-b2-claude-code-deploys-the-recall-and-learn-skills-with-descriptions-narrowed-to-deliberate-use).
  - B3: duplicate writes were asserted away by scenarios but no requirement made them happen, and the recount would double-count. Applied as [793b180 B3: duplicate writes are deduplicated like injections](#793b180-b3-duplicate-writes-are-deduplicated-like-injections).
  - C1: `792a22o` puts the pending-offer flag in every `engram query` payload, so `793a2m` and `793a7v` are limited to what the System delivers, an agent-run query's payload is left as it is, and `793a7v1b` gains the Given "the agent runs no memory command itself".
  - C2: `793a7s` discounted only knowledge "from the System's recall"; it now covers knowledge in the orchestrator's context whether the System delivered it or the orchestrator recalled it, with its rationale reworded.
  - C3: the Then-lines of `793a6a1a`, `793a6c1a` and `793a6d1a` test the instructions, not an absence of requirement.
  - N1: the links to the retired `793a4h1e` in `793c46` are code spans.
  - N2: `793a7d`, `793a8d` and `793a8e` no longer say "the System, not the agent"; an agent that does the step intentionally is not in error.
  - N3: the Given "a session whose configuration contains no memory instructions", impossible on an Integration install, is replaced in `793a2k1a The agent is neither required nor asked to run a memory command`, [793a2n1a Recall happens without the agent running it](793a_product-spec.md#793a2n1-uat-scenarios) and [793a3a1a A lesson is captured without agent action](793a_product-spec.md#793a3a1-uat-scenarios).
  - N4: `793a2d1c` says the instructions do not require another engram command, since the shim invites `engram query` and requires `engram show` for a truncated runbook.
  - N5: `793a2i` no longer says the shim makes the lookup the agent's first action.
  - N6: the solution sentences leave the problem statement of [793a1 Problem Statement](793a_product-spec.md#793a1-problem-statement) for the Automatic memory and Intentional memory terms and for `793a2k Intentional memory stays available and is never blocked` ([793b163 L6: the solution text leaves the problem statement](#793b163-l6-the-solution-text-leaves-the-problem-statement)).
  - N7: `793a7l` and `793a7m` say that a learn or recall please runs deliberately before step 2 does not breach "begins with step 2".
  - N9: already stated for Claude Code by `793a4h Installed by engram update and active in Claude Code`, but `793a9b` left the opt-in applying to Claude Code's `delegate.md` and shim; both now say the Integration deploys them on every run, whether or not `--with-guidance` is given or a config file imports guidance (`engram init` initializes everything, [793c47 Does "init" mean `engram init` or the first-time install by `engram update`?](793c_open-questions.md#793c47-does-init-mean-engram-init-or-the-first-time-install-by-engram-update)), while Pi's guidance stays opt-in ([793c58 Does engram init deploy Pi's guidance without --with-guidance?](793c_open-questions.md#793c58-does-engram-init-deploy-pis-guidance-without---with-guidance)).
- **Rationale:** the rework changed the product's model, and one review round checks it for contradictions, untestable Then-lines and undecided behavior before the owner walkthrough.
- **Alternatives:** none considered for the accepted findings; the three questions are in [793b178 B1: the reduced shim exempts a delivered memory runbook from the follow-frame](#793b178-b1-the-reduced-shim-exempts-a-delivered-memory-runbook-from-the-follow-frame) to [793b180 B3: duplicate writes are deduplicated like injections](#793b180-b3-duplicate-writes-are-deduplicated-like-injections).
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this review; the ID map is [793b181 ID map and interpretations for the seventh review](#793b181-id-map-and-interpretations-for-the-seventh-review).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites 19 IDs, each in the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction), which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a2k`, `793a2k1a`, `793a4h`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b178 B1: the reduced shim exempts a delivered memory runbook from the follow-frame

- **Decided by:** orchestrator (under owner rules; shown to the owner in the walkthrough).
- **Choice:** the reduced shim's follow-frame rules ([792a19m A matched runbook is announced and its steps are the plan](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19m-a-matched-runbook-is-announced-and-its-steps-are-the-plan) to [792a19q Linked and truncated runbooks get the same frame](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19q-linked-and-truncated-runbooks-get-the-same-frame)) exempt a delivered memory runbook (one that mirrors recall, learn, curate or write-memory, or the memory steps of please): the agent MAY follow it and is not required to. Applied as the new Modified operations `793a7w` (target `792a19l`, the umbrella of the frame) and `793a7x` (target `792a19q`, whose last sentence applies the frame to a registered skill's runbook whatever its origin, with its scenario `792a19q1e` modified), by the Memory runbook and Reduced shim terms in [793a1 Problem Statement](793a_product-spec.md#793a1-problem-statement), by the exemption sentence and the scenarios `793a2k1f`, `793a2k1g` and `793a2k1h` in `793a2k Intentional memory stays available and is never blocked`, and by `793a4h1h`. This amends [793c36 Does the shim's follow-frame apply to notes the System delivers?](793c_open-questions.md#793c36-does-the-shims-follow-frame-apply-to-notes-the-system-delivers) (a), which left those nodes unmodified ([793b106 793c36: the shim follow-frame nodes stay unmodified](#793b106-793c36-the-shim-follow-frame-nodes-stay-unmodified)); the owner's answer stands as recorded, and every other runbook keeps the frame.
- **Rationale:** the automatic channel never requires the agent to run memory work ([793b175 Memory is a dual system: automatic and intentional](#793b175-memory-is-a-dual-system-automatic-and-intentional)); a delivered runbook that mirrors recall or learn matches almost any task, so under the frame its delivery would require exactly that.
- **Alternatives:** (b) do not deliver memory runbooks, which reverses [793b175 Memory is a dual system: automatic and intentional](#793b175-memory-is-a-dual-system-automatic-and-intentional) on their delivery; (c) leave the frame applying, which makes the System's delivery require the agent to run recall or learn.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this decision.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a2k1f`, `793a2k1g`, `793a2k1h`, `793a4h1h`, `793a7w`, `793a7x`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a2k`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b179 B2: Claude Code deploys the recall and learn skills with descriptions narrowed to deliberate use

- **Decided by:** orchestrator (under owner rules; shown to the owner in the walkthrough).
- **Choice:** **a behavior change the owner has not yet seen; mark it in the walkthrough.** On Claude Code the Integration deploys the recall and learn skills with descriptions narrowed to deliberate use ("when the user asks, or when you choose to check or save mid-task"), not the moment-triggered wording; Pi keeps today's. Applied in `793a4h Installed by engram update and active in Claude Code` and its scenario `793a4h1j`. The Then-lines "no request to the agent to run a memory command" in [793a2n1a Recall happens without the agent running it](793a_product-spec.md#793a2n1-uat-scenarios), `793a2c1b`, `793a4g1d` and `793a4h1d One update serves both harnesses differently` now say "no request from the System".
- **Rationale:** intentional memory is deliberate by definition ([793b175 Memory is a dual system: automatic and intentional](#793b175-memory-is-a-dual-system-automatic-and-intentional)); a description that fires at the System's moments is the automatic channel done twice inside the agent's context, which defeats the offload of [793a1 Problem Statement](793a_product-spec.md#793a1-problem-statement).
- **Alternatives:** (a) deploy the skills unchanged ([793b176 Dual memory: author decisions made in applying 793b175](#793b176-dual-memory-author-decisions-made-in-applying-793b175) item 1), which has the agent run recall and learn at the System's moments; (c) narrow the descriptions on every harness, which would change Pi, where there is no System.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this decision. The implementation of the narrowed descriptions needs the writing-skills cycle ([793b104 The skill and guidance text changes need the writing-skills cycle](#793b104-the-skill-and-guidance-text-changes-need-the-writing-skills-cycle)).

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites `793a2c1`, `793a2c1b`, `793a4g1`, `793a4g1d`, `793a4h1j`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a4h`, `793a4h1d`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b180 B3: duplicate writes are deduplicated like injections

- **Decided by:** orchestrator (under owner rules; shown to the owner in the walkthrough).
- **Choice:** the dedup of [793a2e Nothing already in context is delivered again](793a_product-spec.md#793a2e-nothing-already-in-context-is-delivered-again) extends to writes: the System MUST NOT write a note for a lesson, conclusion, question and answer or dispatch's evidence that a note written earlier in the session already records, whoever wrote it, and the aggregate recount counts each dispatch once. The node is retitled (ID kept). Scenarios `793a2e1n`, `793a2e1o`, `793a2e1p`, `793a2e1q` and `793a2e1r` cover it. The Integration branches of [793a3a Lessons are captured without the agent](793a_product-spec.md#793a3a-lessons-are-captured-without-the-agent), `793a3p`, `793a3q Sound conclusions are persisted by the System`, `793a7q`, `793a7r` and `793a7t` (with the scenario `793a7t1d`) cite it. [793b176 Dual memory: author decisions made in applying 793b175](#793b176-dual-memory-author-decisions-made-in-applying-793b175) item 4 is amended.
- **Rationale:** the same principle as no re-injection: a second note for what a note already records adds only noise, and a duplicate evidence note would corrupt the tallies that the audit of [792a17m Counting audits the tallies and never routes](../../specs/product/route.md#792a17m-counting-audits-the-tallies-and-never-routes) maintains. Without the requirement, scenarios such as `793a2k1d An intentional learn by the agent is allowed and is not undone` and `793a6a1b` asserted a dedup that nothing made happen.
- **Alternatives:** (b) accept the double count in the recount, which was [793b176 Dual memory: author decisions made in applying 793b175](#793b176-dual-memory-author-decisions-made-in-applying-793b175) item 4's consequence; (c) dedup only the System's own earlier writes, which leaves an orchestrator's or agent's note duplicated.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this decision.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites 12 IDs, each in the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction), which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

- **Amended by:** [793b227 793a3p is removed: route's evidence write stays with the orchestrator](#793b227-793a3p-is-removed-routes-evidence-write-stays-with-the-orchestrator): the dedup no longer covers a dispatch outcome the System writes, because the System writes none.

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a2k1d`, `793a3q`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b181 ID map and interpretations for the seventh review

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** new IDs, none reused (each checked with `git log -S`; `793a7l1c` and `793a7m1c` were retired earlier, so the new scenarios of those nodes take `793a7l1d` and `793a7m1d`): [793b177 Seventh review: findings accepted, and B1 to B3 decided under the owner's rules](#793b177-seventh-review-findings-accepted-and-b1-to-b3-decided-under-the-owners-rules) to [793b181 ID map and interpretations for the seventh review](#793b181-id-map-and-interpretations-for-the-seventh-review); operations `793a7w` with `793a7w1a` and `793a7w1b`, and `793a7x` with `793a7x1a` and its canonical row `793a7x2a`; scenarios `793a2k1g`, `793a2k1h`, `793a2e1n` to `793a2e1r`, `793a2m1d`, `793a4h1j`, `793a4h1k`, `793a7l1d`, `793a7m1d`, `793a7s1d`, `793a7t1d`, `793a7v1c`, `793a9b1d`. No ID is retired. Retitled (ID kept): [793a2e Nothing already in context is delivered again](793a_product-spec.md#793a2e-nothing-already-in-context-is-delivered-again), whose links in all three files were updated. Interpretations: the Memory runbook term is defined once in [793a1 Problem Statement](793a_product-spec.md#793a1-problem-statement) and used by [793b178 B1: the reduced shim exempts a delivered memory runbook from the follow-frame](#793b178-b1-the-reduced-shim-exempts-a-delivered-memory-runbook-from-the-follow-frame); the exemption is applied on the umbrella node `792a19l` and on `792a19q` (the only other node of the frame that names runbooks whatever their origin), because the umbrella makes `792a19m` to `792a19q` apply to `kind: runbook` and the exemption modifies that scope; `793a7w1a` tests the shim's instructions, because the agent's not following a runbook is not observable as an event. `793a9b` gains the scenario `793a9b1d`.
- **Rationale:** records the new IDs and the readings made in applying [793b177 Seventh review: findings accepted, and B1 to B3 decided under the owner's rules](#793b177-seventh-review-findings-accepted-and-b1-to-b3-decided-under-the-owners-rules), for the owner to validate.
- **Alternatives:** exempt the memory runbook on `792a19m` alone, which leaves `792a19q`'s "whatever its origin" sentence contradicting it.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this decision.

- **Amended by:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory): this entry cites 22 IDs, each in the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction), which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction) gives each reason).

## 793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), 2026-10-11.
- **Choice:**
  1. The change layers the automatic memory system beside the existing intentional one. The existing system (skills, shim, guidance, please, route, the lessons contract, install) stays exactly as it is today. The sections `793a6`, `793a7`, `793a8` and `793a9` (all Modified operations) are removed entirely, so no canonical node is modified by this change. Their IDs are retired and never reused.
  2. The measurement requirements are removed: `793a3h`, `793a3i`, `793a3l`, `793a5e`, `793a5f` and `793a5g`. They are moved to issue #850 "parallel memory agent: measurement and evaluation" (https://github.com/toejough/engram/issues/850). The dropped Modified-operation content (narrowed skill descriptions, the reduced shim, please, route and LESSONS changes, and the Added requirements that existed only to support it) is moved to issue #851 "parallel memory agent: remove redundant agent-side memory steps once the System covers them" (https://github.com/toejough/engram/issues/851). One issue per independent change; both are children of #793.
  3. Technical wording (how text is phrased, which sentences of a target apply, what `engram update` writes where, the shim's contents, the wording of the instruction delivered with memory, log fields) belongs in the technical specification, not in 793a. It is stripped from 793a, which keeps only what a user or operator can verify end to end from the outside: transcripts, vault contents, what is shown on screen, and that the System is installed and active.
  4. The remaining Added requirements are merged and pruned to one requirement per user-observable promise. The surviving requirements, and the IDs they absorbed, are in [793b183 ID map for the minimal reduction](#793b183-id-map-for-the-minimal-reduction).
  5. [793a1 Problem Statement](793a_product-spec.md#793a1-problem-statement) keeps the dual-system framing and drops the terms no surviving requirement uses.
- **Rationale:** stated by the owner: reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements. Layering the automatic system beside the existing one needs no change to a canonical node, and measurement and the removal of redundant agent-side steps are independent changes of their own.
- **Alternatives:** keep the Modified operations and the measurement requirements in this change. Not chosen by the owner.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this decision. Amends [793b175 Memory is a dual system: automatic and intentional](#793b175-memory-is-a-dual-system-automatic-and-intentional) (what stays of 793a2 to 793a5), and every entry that cites a retired ID carries an "Amended by" line.
- **Amended by:** [793b237 The existing-system-as-is decision is scoped; it is not a ban on Modified operations](#793b237-the-existing-system-as-is-decision-is-scoped-it-is-not-a-ban-on-modified-operations): the sentence "so no canonical node is modified by this change" is not a rule the owner made. The decision is scoped to the existing memory system (skills, shim, guidance, please, route, the lessons contract, install), which stays as-is as the intentional channel; its Modified operations are dropped from the product spec. A change spec may modify canonical nodes wherever the change needs to.

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): the surviving requirements are re-derived from the owner's intent, and 10 remain (ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240)); the removal of the Modified operations and of the measurement requirements stands.

  - `793a4h Installed by engram update and active in Claude Code`: `793a4h1f`, `793a4h1h`, `793a4h1i`, `793a4h1j`, `793a4h1k`
  - [793a5h The user sees a summary of the memory activity](793a_product-spec.md#793a5h-the-user-sees-a-summary-of-the-memory-activity): `793a5h1d`, `793a5h1f`
- **Rationale:** [793b2 ID mapping](#793b2-id-mapping) requires every retired ID to be listed so none is reused.

## 793b184 Author decisions in applying 793b182

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation. The owner can overrule any of these in the walkthrough.
- **Choice:**
  1. `793a4i` (the update report names the imports to remove) is dropped. The Integration removes no document from Claude Code, so no import there goes stale. Moved to #851 with the rest of that content.
  2. `793a3k` (use is inferred from the transcript) is dropped as technical. It is how the System keeps a note's recency metadata, not something a user can verify from the outside, and its grader, `793a3l`, went to #850. The summary of [793a5h The user sees a summary of the memory activity](793a_product-spec.md#793a5h-the-user-sees-a-summary-of-the-memory-activity) therefore no longer counts "notes used" or "captures discarded", and it counts "items delivered" and "dispatch outcomes recorded" where it counted "items surfaced" and "evidence notes written".
  3. `793a3o` (please's lessons audit and surfacing question) is dropped with the please modifications, because please is unchanged. `793a5i` is merged into [793a5h The user sees a summary of the memory activity](793a_product-spec.md#793a5h-the-user-sees-a-summary-of-the-memory-activity) keeping only the learn skill's parent-unreachable message: please's proposal of a repository change and route's drowning report belong to the dropped please and route changes. `793a3p` keeps the per-dispatch outcome note and drops route's aggregate note, count audit and drowning check.
  4. In [793a2n Relevant memory reaches the agent at the moments](793a_product-spec.md#793a2n-relevant-memory-reaches-the-agent-at-the-moments), recall's depth, the default depth at the dispatch moments, the handling of a moment noticed late, the rule that nothing else of recall's is delivered, recall's own vault writes at deep moments and the wording of the instruction delivered with the memory are technical and moved out. The requirement keeps the user-visible promise: relevant memory reaches the agent before it acts, with an instruction to apply it, and nothing is delivered when nothing is relevant. Scenarios `793a2n1b`, `793a2n1c`, `793a2n1d` and `793a2n1f` are retired; the instruction to apply the memory is asserted in [793a2n1a Recall happens without the agent running it](793a_product-spec.md#793a2n1-uat-scenarios).
  5. `793a3q Sound conclusions are persisted by the System` points at recall's persistence rules ([792a14p A sound conclusion is persisted as a derived note](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14p-a-sound-conclusion-is-persisted-as-a-derived-note), [792a14q A cited synthesis is also captured as a question and answer](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14q-a-cited-synthesis-is-also-captured-as-a-question-and-answer)) for the cases and form of the derived note and the question-and-answer note, instead of restating the derivation labels and the depth condition.
  6. `793a2k Intentional memory stays available and is never blocked` drops the memory-runbook sentences and the Memory runbook term: with the shim unchanged, a delivered runbook is an ordinary note and any follow-frame that applies to it is today's behavior.
  7. The "uninstalled Integration is inert" scenario of `793a4g` is dropped: the uninstall command is not yet specified (#849), so the behavior cannot be verified. `793a4g` is merged into `793a4h Installed by engram update and active in Claude Code`.
  8. `793a4f` no longer requires the System to log its own sessions' ids (a log field); its scenarios are restated on the vault and the transcripts.
  9. Every scenario that asserted a log entry is reworded to what the user can see, or retired. The slow-capture scenario of `793a5g` is kept as `793a4b1d Slow capture does not stall the session`, with the rule that the System never makes the agent or the user wait for work not needed before the next step; `793a2l Indexing and curation happen without the agent` and [793a5h The user sees a summary of the memory activity](793a_product-spec.md#793a5h-the-user-sees-a-summary-of-the-memory-activity) refer to that rule.
  10. [793a3a Lessons are captured without the agent](793a_product-spec.md#793a3a-lessons-are-captured-without-the-agent) states the duplicate-write rule for all three kinds of System write (a lesson, a conclusion, a dispatch outcome), tested by the outline [793a3a1j A lesson that is already recorded is not written again](793a_product-spec.md#793a3a1-uat-scenarios). The scenarios about a LESSONS line the orchestrator saved and about the aggregate recount are retired with their subjects.
  11. Titles changed under stable IDs: [793a2n Relevant memory reaches the agent at the moments](793a_product-spec.md#793a2n-relevant-memory-reaches-the-agent-at-the-moments), `793a2d Delivered memory is attributable`, [793a2e Nothing already in context is delivered again](793a_product-spec.md#793a2e-nothing-already-in-context-is-delivered-again), `793a2k Intentional memory stays available and is never blocked`, `793a2l Indexing and curation happen without the agent`, [793a3a Lessons are captured without the agent](793a_product-spec.md#793a3a-lessons-are-captured-without-the-agent), `793a3p`, `793a3q Sound conclusions are persisted by the System`, [793a4b The session is never broken or stalled](793a_product-spec.md#793a4b-the-session-is-never-broken-or-stalled), [793a4c Memory purview only](793a_product-spec.md#793a4c-memory-purview-only), `793a4f No delivery or capture in its own sessions`, `793a4h Installed by engram update and active in Claude Code`, [793a5c Other harnesses are unchanged](793a_product-spec.md#793a5c-other-harnesses-are-unchanged), [793a5h The user sees a summary of the memory activity](793a_product-spec.md#793a5h-the-user-sees-a-summary-of-the-memory-activity). The terms Injection, Depth, Plan instruction, Chunk stub, Operator, Memory runbook and Reduced shim are dropped from [793a1 Problem Statement](793a_product-spec.md#793a1-problem-statement); Delivery replaces Injection.
  12. No new open question is raised.
- **Rationale:** each follows the owner's rule in [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory) (what a user can verify from the outside) or the standing rule to default to today's behavior; none is genuinely blocking.
- **Alternatives:** keep any of the dropped items as a requirement; ask the owner. Not chosen, per the rules in [793b174 Review rules R1 to R3 apply to this change](#793b174-review-rules-r1-to-r3-apply-to-this-change).

## 793b185 The user is warned when automatic memory is skipped

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), walkthrough of [793a4b The session is never broken or stalled](793a_product-spec.md#793a4b-the-session-is-never-broken-or-stalled), 2026-10-11.
- **Choice:** when the System fails, times out or is unavailable, the user MUST be shown a warning on the same surface as the memory-activity summary ([793a5h The user sees a summary of the memory activity](793a_product-spec.md#793a5h-the-user-sees-a-summary-of-the-memory-activity)), outside the working agent's context, saying automatic memory was skipped for that moment or turn and why (unavailable, timed out, error). The session continues unaffected, with no error to the agent and no lost input. A moment at which nothing matched is not a failure and gets no warning. New scenario `793a4b1e` (checked with `git log -S`: unused); `793a4b1a` to `793a4b1c` also assert the warning and its absence from the agent's transcript.
- **Rationale:** the owner wants to know when the automatic channel silently did nothing.
- **Alternatives:** keep failures silent (the earlier behavior). Not chosen by the owner.

## 793b186 793a4h is delivered by this change and does not depend on #795

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), walkthrough of `793a4h Installed by engram update and active in Claude Code`, 2026-10-11.
- **Choice:** 793a4h is delivered by this change and is not blocked on #795 or any other issue. It stands on today's commands: `engram update` ([792a11g The harnesses that are updated](../../specs/product/install-and-update.md#792a11g-the-harnesses-that-are-updated)) installs the Integration for Claude Code directly, with no dependence on `engram init`, and running it again changes nothing about the Integration. The paragraph saying init owns the install and the requirement is blocked on #795's widening is replaced by one sentence: when #795 later makes update run init, init takes over installing the Integration with no change to the requirement's promises (#795's change directory is linked as a note only). Scenario `793a4h1g` (init gives Claude Code the Integration) is retired, since init is #795's; its ID is never reused. New scenario `793a4h1l` (running update twice changes nothing; checked with `git log -S`: unused) is added under `793a4h1 UAT Scenarios`.
- **ID map:**

| Retired | Was | Reason |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `793a4h1g` | Init gives Claude Code the Integration | `engram init` is owned by change #795; the requirement no longer depends on it |

| New | Covers |
| --- | --- |
| `793a4h1l` | running `engram update` twice leaves the Integration unchanged |

- **Rationale:** the owner wants the System delivered by this change without a cross-issue blocker; today's `engram update` already installs per harness, so the requirement can be stated and verified without init.
- **Alternatives:** keep the dependency on #795's widening ([793b136 Change 793 depends on change 795 being widened](#793b136-change-793-depends-on-change-795-being-widened)). Not chosen by the owner.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this entry; amends [793b134 Interpretations made in applying 793c45, 793c46 and 793c47](#793b134-interpretations-made-in-applying-793c45-793c46-and-793c47), [793b136 Change 793 depends on change 795 being widened](#793b136-change-793-depends-on-change-795-being-widened), [793b141 793c48: change 795 is widened and this change builds on it](#793b141-793c48-change-795-is-widened-and-this-change-builds-on-it) and [793b148 H5: 793a4h is trimmed to the Claude Code delta, and change 795 is referenced](#793b148-h5-793a4h-is-trimmed-to-the-claude-code-delta-and-change-795-is-referenced).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): `793a4h` is now [793a5j Installed by engram update and active in Claude Code](./793a_product-spec.md#793a5j-installed-by-engram-update-and-active-in-claude-code), and its independence from #795 is a preference line of [793a1 Problem Statement](./793a_product-spec.md#793a1-problem-statement).

## 793b188 Technical spec structure: flows and components, with the scenario mapping as a table

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** [Parallel Memory Agent - Technical Spec](793d_technical-spec.md#parallel-memory-agent---technical-spec) follows the default structure of the technical spec guide, with flow specs (`793d2 Added: flows`) and component specs (`793d3 Added: memory-mod` to `793d10 Added: harness-deploy-sync (mod deploy)`) as Added operations against canonical files named in each section. The product-scenario mapping (`793d11 Product-scenario mapping`) is a table that links every 793a scenario by ID and title and maps it to the technical requirements that realize it; it does not restate the 55 scenarios.
- **Rationale:** the guide asks for each product scenario to be restated at its first mapping location, but a canonical technical requirement MUST NOT duplicate canonical product scenario text, the scenarios live unchanged in [Parallel Memory Agent - Product Spec](793a_product-spec.md#parallel-memory-agent---product-spec) in the same change directory, and the owner asked for a traceability table. Restating would add about 4,000 words that must then be kept in sync with the source.
- **Alternatives:** restate every scenario in the mapping. Not chosen for the reasons above; the owner can ask for it in the walkthrough.

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): the technical spec is the owner's two hypotheses with their scenarios; its flows, components and mapping table are retired as requirements, and the mapping is now [793d20 Traceability to the product spec](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d20-traceability-to-the-product-spec).
