## 793b189 The System is a Claude Code mod and a Go command, with the logic in Go

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe) for the premise, recorded here by Author (agent); Author (agent) for the split between the mod and the Go command, awaiting owner validation of the split.
- **Choice:** the System is one Claude Code mod (the Memory Mod, `793d3 Added: memory-mod`) and one new Go command, `engram auto run` (the Auto Job Runner, `793d4 Added: auto-job-runner`). The mod is a thin adapter: it maps Claude Code events to jobs, runs the Go command through `$.process.run`, relays the model requests the Go command asks for, applies the Go command's results to Claude Code (context, status line) and contains failures. Every decision about what to look up, what to deliver, what to write and what to count is made in Go, over the existing injected interfaces.
- **Rationale:** the owner's premise: "the whole design is based around the novel capabilities mods provide", and not on settings hooks (see also "with mods, the system can just print stuff to the user", [793c40 Does the user still see please's audit list?](./793c_open-questions.md#793c40-does-the-user-still-see-pleases-audit-list)). The repository's rule is that logic lives in Go under `internal/` with injected I/O, and that the JS adapter stays thin; a mod cannot use Node APIs, so the Go command is also the only place the existing vault operations (query, learn, amend, activate, ingest) can run.
- **Alternatives:** logic in the mod (rejected: untestable by the repository's Go gates, duplicates vault logic); a long-lived Go server the mod talks to (rejected: a new lifecycle for no need, [793b211 Option comparisons and rejected alternatives moved out of the technical spec](#793b211-option-comparisons-and-rejected-alternatives-moved-out-of-the-technical-spec)).

- **Amended by:** [793b238 Delivery has two model judgements on the cheapest tier: moment classification and a relevance filter](#793b238-delivery-has-two-model-judgements-on-the-cheapest-tier-moment-classification-and-a-relevance-filter): the logic stays in Go, but Go orchestrates and every judgement, the delivery side's included, is relayed to the cheapest capable tier; "every decision is made in Go" no longer holds.

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): the mod is now hypothesis [793d17 H1: a Claude Code mod gives a smaller implementation surface and more capability than settings hooks](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d17-h1-a-claude-code-mod-gives-a-smaller-implementation-surface-and-more-capability-than-settings-hooks), to be confirmed or disproved by its experiments; the split between the mod and the Go command is a design draft, not a requirement.
- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793d3`, `793d4`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).
- **Amended by:** [793b256 The PoC orchestrates in JavaScript inside the mod over the existing engram commands](#793b256-the-poc-orchestrates-in-javascript-inside-the-mod-over-the-existing-engram-commands): the PoC does the memory logic in the mod over the existing engram commands, not in a Go command; "all logic in Go" was the author's choice, not the owner's.


## 793b190 The mod's source lives in agent-instructions/mods and is written in JavaScript

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** the mod is the plugin directory `agent-instructions/mods/engram-memory/`, beside `agent-instructions/skills/` and `agent-instructions/guidance/`: `.claude-plugin/plugin.json`, `hooks/hooks.json` (`"modules": ["./register.js"]`), `hooks/register.js` (an ES module in plain JavaScript) and `tests/*.test.ts` run by `claude plugin test`. The Go side is `internal/auto/` with its command in `internal/cli/auto.go`.
- **Rationale:** the directory is an agent-instruction artifact that `engram update` deploys, like skills and guidance, so it belongs with them. Claude Code writes type declarations into a mod's `.claude-plugin/types/` when it loads a directory in an interactive session, which would put files into the engram-owned root of a deployed copy; plain JavaScript needs no declarations, and `claude plugin validate` still analyzes it statically.
- **Alternatives:** TypeScript source (rejected for the declarations written into the deployed copy); a separate repository for the mod (rejected: the mod and the Go command are versioned and released together by `engram update`).

## 793b191 engram update installs the mod as a skills-directory plugin

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** `engram update` deploys the mod for Claude Code only, to the engram-owned root as `~/.claude/engram/mods/engram-memory/`, and links it at `~/.claude/skills/engram-memory`, where Claude Code loads any folder with a `.claude-plugin/plugin.json` as a plugin in every session with no flag, no install step and no settings edit (plugin id `engram-memory@skills-dir`). The deploy follows the sync rules skills already follow (root ownership, link or manifest mode, dry run, idempotence). If Claude Code does not load a plugin through a link, the surface runs in the existing manifest mode (real files and a record of what was written) for that surface only.
- **Rationale:** it is a documented persistent load path ([Make a plugin load in every session](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/create.md), [Plugin loading reference](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/loading.md)) that needs no edit of `~/.claude/settings.json` and no marketplace, and it reuses the deploy machinery. A session that is already running loads it on `/reload-plugins`.
- **Alternatives:** `CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_DIRS` through `env` in `~/.claude/settings.json`, and a local-directory marketplace with `enabledPlugins` (`793d13h Install path`): both need engram to edit the user's settings file, and managed `disableSideloadFlags` rejects the first.

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793d13h`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b192 Delivery uses prompt context, tool-result context and subagent-start context

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** the mod delivers through the channels Claude Code documents for adding text the model reads and the user does not type: `context` on `prompt.submit` (a prompt, from any origin), `additionalContext` on `classic.PostToolUse` and `classic.PostToolUseFailure` (next to a tool result, so before the agent's next response or tool call), and `additionalContext` on `classic.SubagentStart` (before a subagent's first response). It never rewrites the prompt text, a tool call's arguments, a permission decision or a model, and it adds text only through these channels.
- **Rationale:** these channels leave the prompt and the tool call as the agent issued them ([793a4c Memory purview only](793a_product-spec.md#793a4c-memory-purview-only)), and put the text in the transcript as separate content. There is no documented way to add text to an in-flight turn from a background job, so a delivery is attached to the next event the engine fires for that agent. "Before the agent's next response or tool call" ([793a2n Relevant memory reaches the agent at the moments](793a_product-spec.md#793a2n-relevant-memory-reaches-the-agent-at-the-moments)) maps to the result of the event that revealed the moment, which the agent reads before its next model request.
- **Alternatives:** `793d13a Mid-turn delivery` lists rewriting the Agent prompt or a prompt's text (rejected: changes what the agent or user issued), `$.prompt.submit` (rejected: starts a turn nobody asked for) and a Stop continuation (kept as MAY, [793b207 Late delivery at stop is a MAY and nothing else is late](#793b207-late-delivery-at-stop-is-a-may-and-nothing-else-is-late)).

- **Amended by:** [793b238 Delivery has two model judgements on the cheapest tier: moment classification and a relevance filter](#793b238-delivery-has-two-model-judgements-on-the-cheapest-tier-moment-classification-and-a-relevance-filter): a step's delivery is held and added to the next tool result of the main agent that forms no job of its own (`classic.PostToolUse`, `classic.PostToolUseFailure`). No channel is added; the held delivery rides the channels this entry lists.

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793d13a`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b193 Moments are mechanical cues; the prompt lookup spans recall's four moments

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** the Moment Detector (`793d6 Added: memory-delivery`) recognizes six cues from the event alone, with no model call: a prompt (any origin), a prompt that asks for please or a Skill call of please, a Skill call of route or an Agent call by the main agent (the dispatch decision), a subagent's start, the main agent's first file edit of a turn, and a failed tool call (once per turn). The lookup at a prompt carries one phrase for each of recall's four decision moments ([792a19a Recall fires at four decision moments](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19a-recall-fires-at-four-decision-moments)) beside the prompt's own text, so a note about declaring a kind of work done reaches the agent at the prompt, before the declaration.
- **Rationale:** a model call at every step would add latency and cost to every tool result, while each cue above is a single event with a single lookup and a lookup is bounded to once per turn for an edit or a failure. The query is by embedding, so a note whose situation is "declaring a Go package rename done" matches the done-shaped phrase built from a prompt about a rename.
- **Alternatives:** [793b211 Option comparisons and rejected alternatives moved out of the technical spec](#793b211-option-comparisons-and-rejected-alternatives-moved-out-of-the-technical-spec): a classifier call on each model step, a periodic scan of the transcript, lookups on every tool result.

- **Amended by:** [793b238 Delivery has two model judgements on the cheapest tier: moment classification and a relevance filter](#793b238-delivery-has-two-model-judgements-on-the-cheapest-tier-moment-classification-and-a-relevance-filter): the cues are no longer the whole of moment detection; they are the trigger for when to look. Where the event does not name its moment (a first edit, a first failure, a step that ended with text and a tool call) a `classify` judgement on the cheapest tier decides the moment and its subject, and the lookup is made from that subject; the prompt keeps its four phrases. The rationale that a model call at every step adds latency and cost to every tool result holds for the bounded form chosen: the cues and the text-bearing steps trigger, at most one step job runs at a time, and a cue that names its moment makes no model call except the relevance filter.

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): the cue table and the classification are design drafts listed in [793d19 Design as currently drafted](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d19-design-as-currently-drafted), not requirements.
- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793d6`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b194 Subagents get a dispatch delivery and a capture at stop; moments inside a subagent are not detected

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** for a subagent the mod delivers at its start (`793d2a Moment delivery`) and captures at its stop (`793d2b Turn memory work`). The cues of `793d6a Moments and lookup` are not evaluated for events whose `agent_id` is set (a subagent's own tool calls).
- **Rationale:** `$.session.messages()` returns the main conversation only, so the mod cannot read a subagent's context to exclude what the subagent already holds ([793a2e Nothing already in context is delivered again](793a_product-spec.md#793a2e-nothing-already-in-context-is-delivered-again)); at a subagent's start its context is its task prompt and nothing else, which the mod does know. Every scenario of 793a that involves a subagent is a dispatch or a stop. This narrows "where recall fires" for subagents to their dispatch; shown to the owner in the walkthrough.
- **Alternatives:** read the subagent's transcript file for presence during its run (the file may lag the in-memory conversation and its path is not documented for a running subagent, so it stays a follow-up); deliver without the exclusion (rejected: breaks 793a2e).

- **Amended by:** [793c63 Must recall moments inside a subagent's own loop be delivered now?](./793c_open-questions.md#793c63-must-recall-moments-inside-a-subagents-own-loop-be-delivered-now): the limit to a dispatch delivery narrows [793a2n Relevant memory reaches the agent at the moments](./793a_product-spec.md#793a2n-relevant-memory-reaches-the-agent-at-the-moments) for subagents; the owner answered (a) on 2026-10-11, so this entry stands as written ([793b217 793c63 is answered (a): subagents get a dispatch delivery and a stop capture now](#793b217-793c63-is-answered-a-subagents-get-a-dispatch-delivery-and-a-stop-capture-now)).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793d2a`, `793d2b`, `793d6a`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b195 Presence is read from the live context and a delivery ledger

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** what is "already in the receiving agent's context" ([793a2e Nothing already in context is delivered again](793a_product-spec.md#793a2e-nothing-already-in-context-is-delivered-again)) is the union of the texts of `$.session.messages()` (the messages the next model request carries, so compaction and a resume are already reflected) and a ledger of the item ids the mod delivered to that agent. A subagent's is its task prompt and its ledger, plus the parent's messages for a fork. The ledger is keyed by session id and agent; it is emptied for the main agent when it is compacted (not when a subagent is) and, as after a resume, refilled with the ids of the delivery blocks found in the messages that remain.
- **Rationale:** the transcript file is written asynchronously and may lag the conversation, so it cannot prove what is present at the next event, while the live messages can. The ledger covers a delivered row that is not a message in that list (to verify, [793d1a What the hypotheses rely on in Claude Code mods](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d1a-what-the-hypotheses-rely-on-in-claude-code-mods)).
- **Alternatives:** `793d13c Presence source`: scan the transcript file; the ledger alone (loses what the agent retrieved itself).

- **Amended by:** [793b213 Author decisions in applying the review: ledger, capture input, task and curation](#793b213-author-decisions-in-applying-the-review-ledger-capture-input-task-and-curation): the ledger is keyed by session id, a subagent's compaction does not empty the main ledger, and a resume refills it.

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793d13c`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b196 Judgement runs as relayed model completions, not sessions

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** every LLM judgement the System needs (is this a lesson, is this conclusion sound, is this candidate already recorded, how is a pending offer settled) is a model request that the Go command builds and the mod relays through `$.model.complete` in the same process, in the model named by a Go constant (initially `haiku`). A request is one prompt with no tools and no history, so it is not a session: it has no transcript, runs no hooks and receives no delivery (`793a4f No delivery or capture in its own sessions` holds by construction). The prompts are built from the learn, recall and curate skills' own criteria, taken from the skill files embedded in the binary at build time, so they are the same text at the same version and do not drift from the skills.
- **Rationale:** `793d13d Judgement sessions` compares `$.model.complete`, `$.model.fork` and `claude -p`; `complete` is the only one that works for a subagent's session, chooses a cheap model, carries nothing but what Go put in the prompt, and cannot recurse. The model is a constant tuned by the measurement of #850.
- **Alternatives:** `$.model.fork` (the main conversation only, and the session's expensive model), `claude -p` (a real session with a transcript, the user's global instructions and the mod loaded again).

- **Amended by:** [793b212 The judgement model is the session's own model; a cheaper model is a follow-up under #850](#793b212-the-judgement-model-is-the-sessions-own-model-a-cheaper-model-is-a-follow-up-under-850): the model is the session's own, not a constant `haiku`; a cheaper model is a follow-up under #850.
- **Amended by:** [793b224 Judgement runs on the cheapest capable tier](#793b224-judgement-runs-on-the-cheapest-capable-tier): the model is again a cheap one, now named per request kind by a configurable table (default the `haiku` family) rather than one constant.

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a4f`, `793d13d`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b197 The Go side is stateless and advances a job one round per call

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** `engram auto run` reads one JSON job on standard input, does the Go-side work up to the next model request or to the end, and writes one JSON result: either `need` (the requests to relay and an opaque `state` to send back with the answers) or `done`. It keeps no state between calls and reads the vault, the transcript files and the skill files only through the existing injected capabilities. The mod's relay loop is the same for every job.
- **Rationale:** Go cannot call a model (the binary has no LLM client and no credentials, and with `$.model.complete` needs none), and a process cannot wait for the mod; a stateless round protocol keeps the JS a fixed loop and every decision in Go, and needs no new `cli.Primitives` field because the job arrives on the existing `Stdin`.
- **Alternatives:** a callback socket from Go to the mod (not available to a mod); one Go process per job that waits for answers (the mod cannot feed a running child, `$.process.run` takes its stdin whole).

- **Amended by:** [793b238 Delivery has two model judgements on the cheapest tier: moment classification and a relevance filter](#793b238-delivery-has-two-model-judgements-on-the-cheapest-tier-moment-classification-and-a-relevance-filter): the rationale's "every decision in Go" is replaced by "Go orchestrates, and every judgement is relayed". The protocol is unchanged and now also carries a moment job in up to three rounds.
- **Amended by:** [793b256 The PoC orchestrates in JavaScript inside the mod over the existing engram commands](#793b256-the-poc-orchestrates-in-javascript-inside-the-mod-over-the-existing-engram-commands): the PoC has no Go runner and no round protocol.


## 793b198 Relevance, item limit, chunk content and the wording of the instruction

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** the lookup is the existing query ([792b24c1 Input and defaults](../../specs/technical/query-engine.md#792b24c1-input-and-defaults)) with the moment's text and phrases, the recency channel off, and chunk content included. An item is relevant when its provenance includes `direct`, `cluster_rep` or `trigger` and its score is at least `deliveryMinScore`, initially the engine's relevance floor (0.25); at most `deliveryItemLimit` (10) are delivered, best first. The instruction delivered with the items asks the agent only to apply what bears on its next step; it names no command, including `engram show-chunk`.
- **Rationale:** `793a2k Intentional memory stays available and is never blocked` allows the System to ask the agent only to apply what was delivered, so the lazy-chunk instruction ("fetch with show-chunk") cannot be used; chunks therefore carry their content. The two constants start at today's engine behavior; raising them is a measurement for #850.
- **Alternatives:** the lazy-chunk form (rejected above); a model call to judge relevance at each lookup (rejected: one more round trip before every prompt for a judgement the engine's floor already makes).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a2k`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b199 The capture window, redaction and the System's own blocks

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** a top-level turn is judged once, at its end, over a window of the final assistant message before the turn's prompt, the prompt, and everything after it; a subagent is judged once, at its stop, over its whole transcript (read from `agent_transcript_path`, with `last_assistant_message` appended when the file lags). Before any text reaches a model request or a note, a fixed redactor replaces credentials, tokens and private keys. The blocks the System itself delivered (from the fixed line `[engram memory]` through the fixed line `[end engram memory]`) are removed from the window.
- **Rationale:** one prompt starts one turn, so the window needs no marker and survives compaction; a correction needs the assistant message it corrects. Redacting before the request means the model never sees a secret, which makes `793a4c1e A secret in a correction is not persisted` true without trusting the model. Removing the System's blocks keeps it from recording its own deliveries.
- **Alternatives:** a persistent captured-through marker (more state, breaks on compaction); trusting the model to omit secrets (rejected: unverifiable).

- **Amended by:** [793b213 Author decisions in applying the review: ledger, capture input, task and curation](#793b213-author-decisions-in-applying-the-review-ledger-capture-input-task-and-curation): a turn's window is formed from `$.session.messages()` at the turn's end and passed in the job; only a subagent's window is read from its transcript.

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a4c1e`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b200 Reconciliation is covered or absent; dispatch outcomes are deduplicated mechanically

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** each candidate lesson or conclusion is looked up in the vault by its situation and judged `covered` (an existing note already states it: reinforce that note with `engram amend --activate` and write nothing) or `absent` (write it). The `near` outcome of recall's Step 2.5 is treated as `absent`. A dispatch outcome is skipped when a note with the same slug, the same three tags and the row's duration and cost text already exists.
- **Rationale:** [793a3a Lessons are captured without the agent](793a_product-spec.md#793a3a-lessons-are-captured-without-the-agent) forbids a second note for what a note already records and says nothing about folding a claim into another note; two outcomes are the minimum that satisfies it, and an overlapping note that adds a claim is a legitimate new note. An outcome row has no judgement in it, so its duplicate check is by fields.
- **Alternatives:** the three-way covered, near, absent with a fold (more requests and `amend` forms for no required promise); skipping the dedup of outcomes and relying on the orchestrator not writing them (rejected: [793a3a1j A lesson that is already recorded is not written again](793a_product-spec.md#793a3a1-uat-scenarios) requires it).

- **Amended by:** [793b227 793a3p is removed: route's evidence write stays with the orchestrator](#793b227-793a3p-is-removed-routes-evidence-write-stays-with-the-orchestrator): the Dispatch Outcome Recorder and its mechanical dedup of outcomes are removed; reconciliation of lessons and conclusions is unchanged.

## 793b201 Curation follows curate's table, including the skill-runbook case

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** the Auto Curation component (`793d9 Added: auto-curation`) settles each pending offer with the four outcomes of the curate skill (covered, near, absent, rejected) and its amend forms, and settles a pending skill-runbook registration offer by authoring its fields and clearing the marker, never discarding it. It runs after each turn's other work, and only when a scan of the vault finds a pending offer.
- **Rationale:** `793a2l Indexing and curation happen without the agent` says "as curate does"; the skill's table is the definition.
- **Alternatives:** leave registration offers to the agent (rejected: the offer is created by `engram update` and would stay pending).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a2l`, `793d9`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b202 The chunk index is brought up to date with engram ingest --auto after the turn's other work

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** the mod runs the existing `engram ingest --auto` through `$.process.run` as the last background step before curation and the summary, with a deadline of `ingestDeadlineMs`. It adds no Go code for indexing.
- **Rationale:** `793a2l Indexing and curation happen without the agent` asks for the index to catch up with the turn; the command is the learn skill's own sweep. Running it after capture gives the transcript file time to catch up with the turn, which the hooks documentation says it may lag.
- **Alternatives:** a new command that indexes one transcript (a second way to index); indexing before capture (the lag).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a2l`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b203 Background work is one in-memory queue, and a final turn can be lost on exit

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** the mod runs all background work (captures, index, curation) in one first-in-first-out queue held in memory, one job at a time, so concurrent captures see each other's notes. If the session ends before the queue drains, the unfinished work is lost; nothing is kept for the next session.
- **Rationale:** the simplest design that satisfies [793a4b The session is never broken or stalled](793a_product-spec.md#793a4b-the-session-is-never-broken-or-stalled) and [793a3a1h Two subagents contain the same lesson](793a_product-spec.md#793a3a1-uat-scenarios). `session.end` hooks share 1.5 seconds, too little to finish a capture. The loss is limited to work still running when the user exits; a durable queue resumed by the next session (kept in `$.store`) is a follow-up, listed in `793d12a Known limits and follow-ups`.
- **Alternatives:** a durable queue now (more state and a second input form for the Go side).

- **Amended by:** [793c65 Are captures still queued when a session ends kept for the next session?](./793c_open-questions.md#793c65-are-captures-still-queued-when-a-session-ends-kept-for-the-next-session): the unfinished captures are written to `$.store` at session end and queued again at the next start, as the owner decided on 2026-10-11 ([793b219 793c65 is answered (a): unfinished captures are kept in the store for the next session](#793b219-793c65-is-answered-a-unfinished-captures-are-kept-in-the-store-for-the-next-session)); a crash, a module reload or an unreadable store still loses them.
- **Amended by:** [793b232 Backlog capture is a watermark per transcript source and a scan after each turn's own capture, and the $.store queue is dropped](#793b232-backlog-capture-is-a-watermark-per-transcript-source-and-a-scan-after-each-turns-own-capture-and-the-store-queue-is-dropped): the unfinished captures are no longer kept in `$.store` at all; the watermark scan finds what they would have captured.

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793d12a`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b204 The memory-activity line is the mod's status line

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** the summary, the warning and the parent-queued notice are shown with `$.ui.status`, the mod's one pinned line under the prompt, which a later call replaces and `undefined` clears. Counts: items delivered (one per item in a delivery), notes added (notes the System wrote with `learn`), notes amended (existing notes changed by `amend`, not by a reinforcing activation), dispatch outcomes recorded, offers curated (offers settled by any of the four outcomes).
- **Rationale:** [793a5h The user sees a summary of the memory activity](793a_product-spec.md#793a5h-the-user-sees-a-summary-of-the-memory-activity) and [793a4b The session is never broken or stalled](793a_product-spec.md#793a4b-the-session-is-never-broken-or-stalled) need a surface outside the agent's context and outside the transcript. `$.ui.log` is documented as a row in the transcript, so a warning there would appear in the transcript; `$.ui.toast` is documented to leave the transcript and the model untouched but disappears after seconds. Where nothing draws (`claude -p`, the VS Code chat panel) the line is not shown; delivery still works.
- **Alternatives:** [793b211 Option comparisons and rejected alternatives moved out of the technical spec](#793b211-option-comparisons-and-rejected-alternatives-moved-out-of-the-technical-spec).

## 793b205 Failures are classified by the mod and the runner, with named deadlines

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** a failure is `unavailable` (the `engram` program cannot start, or the runner reports the vault cannot be read), `timeout` (a named deadline passed) or `error` (anything else). Initial deadlines, constants in the mod, tuned under #850: `momentDeadlineMs` 25000 (the measured query takes about 11 seconds), `judgeDeadlineMs` 60000 for one relayed request, `roundDeadlineMs` 120000 for one runner call of a capture or curation, `ingestDeadlineMs` 300000, and at most `maxRounds` 4 rounds per job.
- **Rationale:** [793a4b The session is never broken or stalled](793a_product-spec.md#793a4b-the-session-is-never-broken-or-stalled) names the three reasons and needs a bound for "timed out". Time spent inside `$.process.run` and `$.model.complete` does not count against a hook's 10-second limit, so a bound the mod sets is what keeps a lookup from holding the agent indefinitely.
- **Alternatives:** the 30-second `$.process.run` default (too close to the measured query time to leave room for a slow vault).

## 793b206 The mod is validated and tested by claude plugin validate and claude plugin test in check-full

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** `dev/targs.go` gains a `check-mod` target that runs `claude plugin validate` and `claude plugin test` on `agent-instructions/mods/engram-memory`, and `check-full` and `check-for-fail` depend on it. The Go side is tested by the default `go test` suite as everything else is (fakes for the query, the note writer, the amender, the transcript reader and the clock). The Claude Code CLI becomes a development dependency, at least the version the mod is verified against ([793d1a What the hypotheses rely on in Claude Code mods](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d1a-what-the-hypotheses-rely-on-in-claude-code-mods)).
- **Rationale:** these are the documented ways to check a mod without a session ([Create a mod](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/mods/create.md), [Test a mod](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/mods/test.md)); `claude plugin test` runs without a sign-in or a network.
- **Alternatives:** a Go test that starts `claude` (a live session and a network); no mod tests (the adapter is the only code the Go gates do not cover).

- **Amended by:** [793b236 The mod is validated and tested through the existing integration gate, not a new check target](#793b236-the-mod-is-validated-and-tested-through-the-existing-integration-gate-not-a-new-check-target): the `check-mod` target and its place in `check-full` are withdrawn; the same two commands run from an `integration`-tagged Go test.

## 793b207 Late delivery at stop is a MAY and nothing else is late

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** the System MAY, when a main-agent turn ends with a completion claim and nothing in the vault was delivered for it, deliver at `classic.Stop` through `additionalContext` (which continues the conversation, guarded by `stop_hook_active`). This is not required by any 793a scenario, so it has no requirement and no scenario here. The other cues deliver before the agent's next response or tool call by construction.
- **Rationale:** the done moment is covered by the prompt's done-shaped phrase ([793b193 Moments are mechanical cues; the prompt lookup spans recall's four moments](#793b193-moments-are-mechanical-cues-the-prompt-lookup-spans-recalls-four-moments)); a stop continuation adds a model call to classify the claim and an extra step the user sees, for a gain no scenario tests. Recorded so the owner can ask for it.
- **Alternatives:** make it a requirement now.

## 793b208 No new open question; unverified mod behavior is listed in the technical spec

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** no entry is added to [Parallel Memory Agent - Open Questions](793c_open-questions.md#parallel-memory-agent---open-questions). Mods can deliver before the agent's next response or tool call through documented channels ([793b192 Delivery uses prompt context, tool-result context and subagent-start context](#793b192-delivery-uses-prompt-context-tool-result-context-and-subagent-start-context)), so the product-level question the brief anticipated does not arise. What this draft could not confirm from the documentation or the declarations is listed with its fallback in [793d1a What the hypotheses rely on in Claude Code mods](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d1a-what-the-hypotheses-rely-on-in-claude-code-mods), and each item is a check at implementation that does not change a product promise.
- **Rationale:** the guide allows an open question only for a contradiction, an untestable requirement or an undecided behavior change; none was found.
- **Alternatives:** none.

- **Amended by:** [793b216 The recommendations for 793c63 to 793c66 are applied meanwhile, and the claim of no open question is withdrawn](#793b216-the-recommendations-for-793c63-to-793c66-are-applied-meanwhile-and-the-claim-of-no-open-question-is-withdrawn): the claim that no open question arises is withdrawn; [793c63 Must recall moments inside a subagent's own loop be delivered now?](./793c_open-questions.md#793c63-must-recall-moments-inside-a-subagents-own-loop-be-delivered-now), [793c64 When is tier evidence delivered relative to routing?](./793c_open-questions.md#793c64-when-is-tier-evidence-delivered-relative-to-routing), [793c65 Are captures still queued when a session ends kept for the next session?](./793c_open-questions.md#793c65-are-captures-still-queued-when-a-session-ends-kept-for-the-next-session) and [793c66 Which platform cases where the System is silently inactive does the product promise cover?](./793c_open-questions.md#793c66-which-platform-cases-where-the-system-is-silently-inactive-does-the-product-promise-cover) are open for the owner.

## 793b209 The review of the technical spec (round 1): findings and where each was applied

- **Decided by:** Reviewer (opus agent), accepted by Orchestrator (Claude); awaiting owner validation. The verdict was "targeted rework". This is the one agent review round for [Parallel Memory Agent - Technical Spec](./793d_technical-spec.md#parallel-memory-agent---technical-spec) ([AGENTS.md, Decisions and Open Questions](../../AGENTS.md#decisions-and-open-questions)); the walkthrough with the owner follows.
- **Finding:** the draft was out of proportion to its product spec (26 requirements, 132 scenarios, about 19,000 words for 14 product requirements), restated the same behavior in a flow and in a component, and left some platform facts, rules and conditions inconsistent.
- **Fix applied:** in the commit that records this entry, as follows. The targets were at most 13 requirements, 60 scenarios and 8,000 words. Result: 13 added requirements plus one modified requirement (`794b4a`), 60 scenarios (59 added, one modified), and 14,267 words by `wc -w` (13,391 without table rules). The word target is not met: the 55-row scenario mapping, the ID-and-title link labels the reference rule requires and the Gherkin example tables are the bulk, and cutting further would remove content the cut list keeps.

| # | Finding | Applied in |
| --- | --- | --- |
| B1 | Three contract-only requirements restate what the flows constrain, and seven scenario pairs duplicate each other across flow and component | `793d3c`, `793d3d` and `793d3e` deleted; their reply-shape handling, deadline names, failure classes and queue order folded into `793d2a Moment delivery` and `793d2b Turn memory work`; the flow-side scenario of each pair kept |
| B2 | The failure flow restates both flows' failure branches | `793d2c` deleted; failure is an `alt` branch in the diagrams of `793d2a Moment delivery` and `793d2b Turn memory work` |
| B3 | The install flow and the two deploy requirements restate each other | `793d2d` deleted; `793d10a` and `793d10b` merged into `793d10a Mod deploy` |
| B4 | The detector is a pure function with one caller | `793d5` merged into `793d6 Added: memory-delivery` as `793d6a Moments and lookup` and `793d6b Presence, relevance and delivery text`; cue strings and limits moved to the implementation-plan note of `793d12a Known limits and follow-ups` |
| B5 to B8 | Splits of one behavior across requirements | `793d4a` and `793d4b` merged (`793d4a A job in rounds`); `793d7a` to `793d7d` merged into `793d7a Judging a window` and `793d7c Reconciling and writing`; `793d8a` and `793d8b` merged (`793d8a`, citing [792a17k Each dispatch is also written to the vault as a tagged evidence note](../../specs/product/route.md#792a17k-each-dispatch-is-also-written-to-the-vault-as-a-tagged-evidence-note)); `793d9a` and `793d9b` merged (`793d9a Settling offers`, citing the curate table) |
| B9 | Option analysis and rejected alternatives are change context | `793d13 Relative option impact` cut to the delivery-channel, presence, judgement and install-path tables; the rest and `793d14` moved to [793b211 Option comparisons and rejected alternatives moved out of the technical spec](#793b211-option-comparisons-and-rejected-alternatives-moved-out-of-the-technical-spec) |
| B10 | The reliance table held rows that no design choice needs and stale statuses | [793d1a What the hypotheses rely on in Claude Code mods](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d1a-what-the-hypotheses-rely-on-in-claude-code-mods): six rows dropped, rows 3 and 4 verified by test on 2.1.296, rows 5, 6, 13, 14 and 12 rewritten, row 6 marks `prompt.submit` context as unverified with a fallback, and the platform facts listed under N16 added |
| N6 | Subagent compaction would empty the main ledger; the ledger outlives `/clear` | `793d3b State kept for the System`: ledger keyed by `$.session.id()`; `session.compact` acts on events without `agentId` only |
| N7 | Row 10, [793b195 Presence is read from the live context and a delivery ledger](#793b195-presence-is-read-from-the-live-context-and-a-delivery-ledger) and the state requirement gave different refill rules | one rule in `793d3b State kept for the System`, row 10 and [793b195 Presence is read from the live context and a delivery ledger](#793b195-presence-is-read-from-the-live-context-and-a-delivery-ledger): refill from the delivery blocks in the remaining `$.session.messages()`, after a compaction and on `classic.SessionStart` with source `resume` |
| N8 | The capture job read the lagging transcript file | `793d2b Turn memory work`, `793d4a A job in rounds` and `793d7a Judging a window`: the window is formed from `$.session.messages()` at the turn's end and passed on stdin; only a subagent's window is read from its transcript |
| N9 | Prompt phrases used only the current prompt, which may be "ok, do that" | `793d3b State kept for the System` defines `task` (the latest human prompt of at least `taskMinChars`); `793d6a Moments and lookup` builds the phrases from it |
| N10 | The judgement model was a Go constant, `haiku` | the session's own model, [793b212 The judgement model is the session's own model; a cheaper model is a follow-up under #850](#793b212-the-judgement-model-is-the-sessions-own-model-a-cheaper-model-is-a-follow-up-under-850) |
| N12 | Interaction requirements (relay loop, queue order, runner call order) sat in component requirements | moved into the flow requirements `793d2a Moment delivery` and `793d2b Turn memory work` |
| N13 | Curation ran "whether or not" an offer existed, contradicting [793b201 Curation follows curate's table, including the skill-runbook case](#793b201-curation-follows-curates-table-including-the-skill-runbook-case); the capture request count differed from `793d7 Added: auto-capture`; an arrow lacked its ID | `793d2b Turn memory work`: the curation job runs after the index update and finishes with no request when its scan finds no offer; the operations text says one extraction request plus one per candidate; the failure arrows carry `793d2a` and `793d2b` |
| N14 | Missing scenarios | added: spawn-order fallback (`793d3b State kept for the System`), adopted or left alone in the report (`793d10a Mod deploy`), note names in the extraction prompt (`793d7a Judging a window`); the `<project>` clause of the outcome source dropped, because [792a17k Each dispatch is also written to the vault as a tagged evidence note](../../specs/product/route.md#792a17k-each-dispatch-is-also-written-to-the-vault-as-a-tagged-evidence-note) defines the source |
| N15 | Real-session checks of the capture scenarios are probabilistic | stated in `793d12 Recommended impact` (Tests); measured under #850; the scenarios here use scripted replies |
| N16 | Missed platform facts | row 9 (`session.compact` with `agentId`, `turn.complete` for an interrupted turn, module variables survive `/clear`, `/resume` and `/branch`), row 10 (newest 4096) |
| N17 | Author choices the owner may overrule | [793b215 Choices shown to the owner in the walkthrough](#793b215-choices-shown-to-the-owner-in-the-walkthrough) |
| 4f | `793a4f1a No note records the System's own work` and `793a4f1b A session of its own receives no delivery` pass trivially because `$.model.complete` starts no session | stated in `793d12 Recommended impact` |
| Q1 to Q4 | Four narrowings of approved product requirements | opened as [793c63 Must recall moments inside a subagent's own loop be delivered now?](./793c_open-questions.md#793c63-must-recall-moments-inside-a-subagents-own-loop-be-delivered-now) to [793c66 Which platform cases where the System is silently inactive does the product promise cover?](./793c_open-questions.md#793c66-which-platform-cases-where-the-system-is-silently-inactive-does-the-product-promise-cover); the recommendations are applied meanwhile ([793b216 The recommendations for 793c63 to 793c66 are applied meanwhile, and the claim of no open question is withdrawn](#793b216-the-recommendations-for-793c63-to-793c66-are-applied-meanwhile-and-the-claim-of-no-open-question-is-withdrawn)) |
| Q5 | Whether `check-mod` joins `check-full` | decided by the orchestrator, [793b214 check-mod is part of check-full and check-for-fail](#793b214-check-mod-is-part-of-check-full-and-check-for-fail) |

- **Alternatives:** none; this records a review round.

- **Amended by:** [793b227 793a3p is removed: route's evidence write stays with the orchestrator](#793b227-793a3p-is-removed-routes-evidence-write-stays-with-the-orchestrator): `793d8` and `793d8a`, which this review kept, are retired.

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites 17 IDs, each in the ID map, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b211 Option comparisons and rejected alternatives moved out of the technical spec

- **Decided by:** Author (agent), applying [793b209 The review of the technical spec (round 1): findings and where each was applied](#793b209-the-review-of-the-technical-spec-round-1-findings-and-where-each-was-applied); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** the comparisons that do not bear on the contract, and the non-normative rejected alternatives, are recorded here and not in [Parallel Memory Agent - Technical Spec](./793d_technical-spec.md#parallel-memory-agent---technical-spec). The four comparisons that bear on it stay in `793d13 Relative option impact`. The objectives are those of `793d13 Relative option impact`. Row numbers refer to [793d1a What the hypotheses rely on in Claude Code mods](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d1a-what-the-hypotheses-rely-on-in-claude-code-mods).

**Moment detection** ([793b193 Moments are mechanical cues; the prompt lookup spans recall's four moments](#793b193-moments-are-mechanical-cues-the-prompt-lookup-spans-recalls-four-moments)).

| Option | O1 | O2 | O3 | O4 | Verdict |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| A. Mechanical cues in Go; the prompt lookup spans recall's four moments | yes for every scenario: a note about declaring work done reaches the agent at the prompt | yes: no model call on the path | yes | yes | Selected |
| B. A classifier call (`$.model.classify`) on each model step | yes, and finds semantic moments | partly: about a second per step, on the path | partly | partly | Rejected for now; the cues can grow into it |
| C. A periodic scan of the transcript with delivery at the next channel | partly: late and unpredictable | partly | no: a timer, a cursor and a second delivery path | partly | Rejected |
| D. The prompt cue alone | partly: no mid-turn moment | yes | yes | yes | Rejected: misses failures and dispatches |

- **Amended by:** [793b238 Delivery has two model judgements on the cheapest tier: moment classification and a relevance filter](#793b238-delivery-has-two-model-judgements-on-the-cheapest-tier-moment-classification-and-a-relevance-filter): option B is selected in a bounded form (the cues and the steps that carry text trigger a `classify` call; the result is queried once). The owner rejected the deterministic option for the delivery side (`793d13j Moment classification and relevance`).

**Subagent coverage** ([793b194 Subagents get a dispatch delivery and a capture at stop; moments inside a subagent are not detected](#793b194-subagents-get-a-dispatch-delivery-and-a-capture-at-stop-moments-inside-a-subagent-are-not-detected)).

| Option | O1 | O2 | O3 | O4 | Verdict |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| A. Record the spawn, deliver at the subagent's start, capture at its stop from its transcript | yes: both subagent scenarios | yes | yes | partly: matching a start to its spawn, and the transcript's completeness (rows 7 and 8) | Selected |
| B. Rewrite the `agent.spawn` prompt | partly | no: changes the Agent call's arguments ([793a4c1a Tool calls and permissions unchanged](./793a_product-spec.md#793a4c1-uat-scenarios)) | yes | yes | Rejected |
| C. Rewrite the subagent's first row at `session.append` | yes | partly: holds the row while the lookup runs | partly | partly | Rejected |
| D. Also detect moments inside the subagent | yes | partly | no: needs the subagent's context | partly | Follow-up #852 |

**Late detection** ([793b207 Late delivery at stop is a MAY and nothing else is late](#793b207-late-delivery-at-stop-is-a-may-and-nothing-else-is-late)). A moment can be recognized only after the agent acted (a dispatch decided without the route skill, the declaration of done). Option A, selected: the dispatch cue delivers with the result of the call that revealed it, which the agent reads before its next response or tool call, and the done moment is covered early by the prompt's done-shaped phrase; nothing further is required. Option B: make the stop continuation required, with a classifier (rejected: it adds a model call and an extra step the user sees). Option C: start a turn with `$.prompt.submit` (rejected: it starts a turn nobody asked for).

**Summary and warning surface** ([793b204 The memory-activity line is the mod's status line](#793b204-the-memory-activity-line-is-the-mods-status-line)).

| Option | O1 | O2 | O3 | O4 | Verdict |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| A. `$.ui.status`, the mod's pinned line | yes: outside the agent's context; not a transcript row (row 14, to verify) | yes | yes | partly | Selected |
| B. `$.ui.log` | no: documented as a transcript row, so the warning would appear in the transcript ([793a4b1a Vault unavailable](./793a_product-spec.md#793a4b1-uat-scenarios)) | yes | yes | yes | Rejected |
| C. `$.ui.toast` | partly: leaves the transcript untouched, but vanishes after seconds | yes | yes | yes | The fallback of row 14 |
| D. The line under the answer from `turn.complete` | partly: tied to the turn's rendering and to main turns | yes | yes | partly | Rejected |
| E. A pane or the band above the prompt | yes | yes | no: a drawing, its state and its keys | partly | Rejected |

**What runs in JavaScript and what in Go** ([793b189 The System is a Claude Code mod and a Go command, with the logic in Go](#793b189-the-system-is-a-claude-code-mod-and-a-go-command-with-the-logic-in-go)).

| Option | O1 | O2 | O3 | O4 | Verdict |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| A. The mod adapts events, relays model requests, queues, shows the line; Go decides everything else | yes | yes: the JavaScript holds no judgement or vault logic | yes | yes | Selected |
| B. Logic in the mod | yes | partly | no: duplicates vault logic outside the Go gates | yes | Rejected |
| C. A long-lived Go server the mod talks to | yes | partly: a lifecycle to start, restart and stop | no | partly | Rejected |

**Rejected alternatives** (non-normative).

- Settings hooks (`UserPromptSubmit`, `PostToolUse`, `SubagentStart`, `Stop` in a settings file): rejected by the owner's premise ([793b189 The System is a Claude Code mod and a Go command, with the logic in Go](#793b189-the-system-is-a-claude-code-mod-and-a-go-command-with-the-logic-in-go)); they cannot draw a line outside the transcript or relay a model request in process.
- An MCP server or a skill that asks the agent to call it: puts the work back on the agent's initiative, the failure [793a1 Problem Statement](./793a_product-spec.md#793a1-problem-statement) measures.
- A model call to judge relevance at each lookup: one more round trip before every prompt for a judgement the engine's relevance floor already makes ([793b198 Relevance, item limit, chunk content and the wording of the instruction](#793b198-relevance-item-limit-chunk-content-and-the-wording-of-the-instruction)).
- Go-owned state files for markers, ledgers or queues: a new I/O capability and a state directory to specify; the window needs no marker and the ledger and queue live as long as the session ([793b203 Background work is one in-memory queue, and a final turn can be lost on exit](#793b203-background-work-is-one-in-memory-queue-and-a-final-turn-can-be-lost-on-exit)).
- Reading the deployed skill files at run time for the judgement criteria: depends on harness paths; the files embedded in the binary are the same text at the same version ([793b196 Judgement runs as relayed model completions, not sessions](#793b196-judgement-runs-as-relayed-model-completions-not-sessions)).
- A mod for Pi: out of scope; Pi is unchanged ([793a5c Other harnesses are unchanged](./793a_product-spec.md#793a5c-other-harnesses-are-unchanged)).
- **Rationale:** the technical spec guide asks for option analysis, but a spec with the comparisons that do not bear on the contract is larger than the contract it states.
- **Alternatives:** keep all comparisons in [Parallel Memory Agent - Technical Spec](./793d_technical-spec.md#parallel-memory-agent---technical-spec). Not chosen: the review found the draft out of proportion.

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793d13`, `793d13j`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b212 The judgement model is the session's own model; a cheaper model is a follow-up under #850

- **Decided by:** Author (agent), following the standing owner rule to default to today's behavior; shown in the walkthrough, where the owner can overrule it.
- **Choice:** the System's judgement requests (extraction, reconcile, curation) are relayed with `$.model.complete` in the model the session itself runs, `$.session.model()`; the Go command names no model. `haiku` for the judgements, which [793b196 Judgement runs as relayed model completions, not sessions](#793b196-judgement-runs-as-relayed-model-completions-not-sessions) chose as the initial constant, moves to the measurement of #850.
- **Rationale:** today the agent runs the learn, recall and curate skills in its own model, so the session's model is today's behavior; a cheaper model is a cost and quality change that needs the measurement #850 plans, and the decision belongs to the owner.
- **Alternatives:** `haiku` as a constant (cheaper, unmeasured for these judgements).
- **Amends:** [793b196 Judgement runs as relayed model completions, not sessions](#793b196-judgement-runs-as-relayed-model-completions-not-sessions).
- **Amended by:** [793b224 Judgement runs on the cheapest capable tier](#793b224-judgement-runs-on-the-cheapest-capable-tier): reversed; the session's own model is not used for judgement.

## 793b213 Author decisions in applying the review: ledger, capture input, task and curation

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); shown in the walkthrough, where the owner can overrule them.
- **Choice:** (1) the ledger is keyed by `$.session.id()` and each agent, because module variables outlive `/clear`, `/resume` and `/branch`; (2) a main agent's compaction empties and refills the main ledger, a subagent's does not, and a resume in a new process refills it; the refill reads the delivery blocks in `$.session.messages()`; (3) the capture window of a turn is formed from `$.session.messages()` at the turn's end and travels in the job, because the transcript file lags; (4) the moment `task` is the latest human prompt of at least `taskMinChars` characters, so a prompt such as "ok, do that" still looks up what the work is about; (5) the curation job runs after every index update and finishes with no request when its scan finds no pending offer, which is [793b201 Curation follows curate's table, including the skill-runbook case](#793b201-curation-follows-curates-table-including-the-skill-runbook-case)'s "only when a scan finds a pending offer".
- **Rationale:** each follows from a platform fact in [793d1a What the hypotheses rely on in Claude Code mods](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d1a-what-the-hypotheses-rely-on-in-claude-code-mods) (rows 9, 10 and 12) or from consistency with an earlier entry, and none changes a product promise.
- **Alternatives:** reset the ledger at `session.end` (the id keying covers `/clear` without a hook); read the transcript file for the window (lags); a curation job only when the mod has seen an offer (the mod cannot see the vault).
- **Amends:** [793b195 Presence is read from the live context and a delivery ledger](#793b195-presence-is-read-from-the-live-context-and-a-delivery-ledger), [793b199 The capture window, redaction and the System's own blocks](#793b199-the-capture-window-redaction-and-the-systems-own-blocks).

## 793b214 check-mod is part of check-full and check-for-fail

- **Decided by:** Orchestrator (under owner rules; shown in walkthrough).
- **Choice:** `check-mod` (`claude plugin validate` and `claude plugin test` on the mod) is a dependency of `check-full` and `check-for-fail`, and fails, not skips, when the Claude Code CLI is unavailable. `793d15a` (retired in 793b236) modified `794b4a` to permit it, and `Test794b4a1c` in `dev/nilaway_internal_test.go` is updated.
- **Rationale:** the repository says to use `check-full` to get all errors at once and allows no silent skips; the Claude Code CLI is already a development dependency of this repository's workflow. This is the question the review raised as Q5.
- **Alternatives:** a separate target outside the aggregates. Rejected: a silent gap in the check that is supposed to be complete.

- **Amended by:** [793b236 The mod is validated and tested through the existing integration gate, not a new check target](#793b236-the-mod-is-validated-and-tested-through-the-existing-integration-gate-not-a-new-check-target): reversed; there is no `check-mod`, `793d15a` is retired and `794b4a` is not modified.

## 793b215 Choices shown to the owner in the walkthrough

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); shown in the walkthrough, where the owner can overrule them.
- **Choice:** (1) [793b188 Technical spec structure: flows and components, with the scenario mapping as a table](#793b188-technical-spec-structure-flows-and-components-with-the-scenario-mapping-as-a-table): `793d11 Product-scenario mapping` maps and links each product scenario and does not restate it, which deviates from the guide's restatement at first mapping; (2) [793b200 Reconciliation is covered or absent; dispatch outcomes are deduplicated mechanically](#793b200-reconciliation-is-covered-or-absent-dispatch-outcomes-are-deduplicated-mechanically): a `near` verdict is treated as `absent` when capturing, which folds nothing and so differs from recall's three-way table (curation keeps all four); (3) [793b205 Failures are classified by the mod and the runner, with named deadlines](#793b205-failures-are-classified-by-the-mod-and-the-runner-with-named-deadlines): `momentDeadlineMs` is 25 seconds, which the platform allows because time inside a mods API call does not count against the hook's 10 seconds, and the lookup costs about 11 seconds, once per prompt, first edit of a turn, first failure of a turn, dispatch and subagent start; (4) [793b204 The memory-activity line is the mod's status line](#793b204-the-memory-activity-line-is-the-mods-status-line): Claude Code draws the status line prefixed with "⚠ <mod name>:" (row 14 of [793d1a What the hypotheses rely on in Claude Code mods](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d1a-what-the-hypotheses-rely-on-in-claude-code-mods)), so every summary appears with a warning sign; this is the platform's, not the mod's. (5) each requirement's `Supports` line points to `793d11 Product-scenario mapping`, whose rows link every product scenario to the requirements that realize it; the per-requirement lists are generated at promotion. This keeps the spec smaller; the owner can ask for the lists inline.
- **Rationale:** each is a place where the technical spec departs from the guide's default, from an earlier choice's phrasing or from what the user sees, and the owner should see it once.
- **Alternatives:** none; this records what the owner should see.

- **Amended by:** [793b222 Each technical requirement lists its supported scenarios again](#793b222-each-technical-requirement-lists-its-supported-scenarios-again): item (5) is overruled; each requirement carries its own `Supports` list and `793d11 Product-scenario mapping` stays as the same mapping read the other way.

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793d11`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b217 793c63 is answered (a): subagents get a dispatch delivery and a stop capture now

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe, 2026-10-11), answering [793c63 Must recall moments inside a subagent's own loop be delivered now?](./793c_open-questions.md#793c63-must-recall-moments-inside-a-subagents-own-loop-be-delivered-now) with option (a), as recommended.
- **Choice:** a subagent receives a delivery at its dispatch and a capture at its stop; its own first edit and first failure are not moments. Follow-up #852. `793d6a Moments and lookup`, `793d12a Known limits and follow-ups` and [793b194 Subagents get a dispatch delivery and a capture at stop; moments inside a subagent are not detected](#793b194-subagents-get-a-dispatch-delivery-and-a-capture-at-stop-moments-inside-a-subagent-are-not-detected) apply it as written; [793a2n Relevant memory reaches the agent at the moments](./793a_product-spec.md#793a2n-relevant-memory-reaches-the-agent-at-the-moments) says in its subagent bullet that a subagent's own first edit or first failure is not a moment.
- **Rationale:** the dispatch delivery gives the subagent the notes for its task, which is the case the product scenario for a subagent tests; a moment inside a subagent needs a verified source of its context.
- **Alternatives:** (b) require in-loop moments now (needs an undocumented way to read a subagent's context, or accepts delivering what the subagent already holds).
- **Amends:** [793b216 The recommendations for 793c63 to 793c66 are applied meanwhile, and the claim of no open question is withdrawn](#793b216-the-recommendations-for-793c63-to-793c66-are-applied-meanwhile-and-the-claim-of-no-open-question-is-withdrawn) (the answer replaces its "meanwhile").

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793d12a`, `793d6a`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b218 793c64 is answered (a): tier evidence is delivered when the orchestrator routes through route

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe, 2026-10-11), answering [793c64 When is tier evidence delivered relative to routing?](./793c_open-questions.md#793c64-when-is-tier-evidence-delivered-relative-to-routing) with option (a), as recommended.
- **Choice:** tier evidence is delivered when the orchestrator routes through the route skill; for a direct `Agent` call it reaches the subagent at its start and the orchestrator after the return. [793a2n1j Tier evidence is delivered before a dispatch is routed](./793a_product-spec.md#793a2n1-uat-scenarios) and the matching bullet of [793a2n Relevant memory reaches the agent at the moments](./793a_product-spec.md#793a2n-relevant-memory-reaches-the-agent-at-the-moments) are worded "when the orchestrator routes through route"; `793d12a Known limits and follow-ups` states the limit.
- **Rationale:** it states the promise the platform can keep: a direct `Agent` call has no event before the dispatch.
- **Alternatives:** (b) reword the moment as "at route's invocation".
- **Amends:** [793b216 The recommendations for 793c63 to 793c66 are applied meanwhile, and the claim of no open question is withdrawn](#793b216-the-recommendations-for-793c63-to-793c66-are-applied-meanwhile-and-the-claim-of-no-open-question-is-withdrawn).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793d12a`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b219 793c65 is answered (a): unfinished captures are kept in the store for the next session

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe, 2026-10-11), answering [793c65 Are captures still queued when a session ends kept for the next session?](./793c_open-questions.md#793c65-are-captures-still-queued-when-a-session-ends-kept-for-the-next-session) with option (a), as recommended.
- **Choice:** the captures still queued or running when a session ends are written to `$.store` at `session.end` and queued again at the next session start; a crash, a module reload or an unreadable store still loses them. `793d2b Turn memory work` states it as the Memory Mod's behavior; if `$.store` cannot be used the fallback is (b), accepting the loss.
- **Rationale:** it removes the common loss, exit within seconds of a lesson, for a small amount of state.
- **Alternatives:** (b) accept the loss.
- **Amends:** [793b203 Background work is one in-memory queue, and a final turn can be lost on exit](#793b203-background-work-is-one-in-memory-queue-and-a-final-turn-can-be-lost-on-exit), [793b216 The recommendations for 793c63 to 793c66 are applied meanwhile, and the claim of no open question is withdrawn](#793b216-the-recommendations-for-793c63-to-793c66-are-applied-meanwhile-and-the-claim-of-no-open-question-is-withdrawn).
- **Amended by:** [793b232 Backlog capture is a watermark per transcript source and a scan after each turn's own capture, and the $.store queue is dropped](#793b232-backlog-capture-is-a-watermark-per-transcript-source-and-a-scan-after-each-turns-own-capture-and-the-store-queue-is-dropped): the unfinished captures are no longer kept in `$.store`; the watermark scan catches an interrupted or killed session at the next session. The owner answered (a) and this generalises his answer; he can veto it.

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793d2b`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b220 793c66 is answered (a): the product promise covers the sessions in which mods run

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe, 2026-10-11), answering [793c66 Which platform cases where the System is silently inactive does the product promise cover?](./793c_open-questions.md#793c66-which-platform-cases-where-the-system-is-silently-inactive-does-the-product-promise-cover) with option (a), as recommended.
- **Choice:** `engram update` checks `claude --version` and reports with the mod's row when mods cannot load (`793d10a Mod deploy`, `793d2f Mod install`); `793a4h Installed by engram update and active in Claude Code` and [793a5h The user sees a summary of the memory activity](./793a_product-spec.md#793a5h-the-user-sees-a-summary-of-the-memory-activity) are narrowed to "every Claude Code session in which mods run".
- **Rationale:** it keeps the promise to what the platform allows and tells the user about the one case `engram update` can detect.
- **Alternatives:** (b) require the System in every session; the platform does not allow it.
- **Amends:** [793b216 The recommendations for 793c63 to 793c66 are applied meanwhile, and the claim of no open question is withdrawn](#793b216-the-recommendations-for-793c63-to-793c66-are-applied-meanwhile-and-the-claim-of-no-open-question-is-withdrawn).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a4h`, `793d10a`, `793d2f`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b221 Ownership must be unambiguous: one owner per requirement, and 793d2b is split in two

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe, 2026-10-11): ownership must be unambiguous. Every requirement of the technical spec has exactly one owning component, and a flow requirement that named two owners is split into one interaction requirement per owner.
- **Choice:** `793d2b Turn memory work` is now the Memory Mod's alone (event handling, relay, queue ordering, the summary and the session-end store), and `793d2e Job order within a round` is the Auto Job Runner's (the order in which it calls its components within a round of a capture or curation job), in the same flow spec. Each has its own scenarios; the runner's arrows in the diagram of `793d2b Turn memory work` carry `793d2e Job order within a round`. Every flow requirement (`793d2a Moment delivery`, `793d2b Turn memory work`, `793d2e Job order within a round`, `793d2f Mod install`) states "Owner: <component>". No other requirement named two owners.
- **Rationale:** the technical spec guide requires that a requirement binding two components be split into one per owner, and that each interaction requirement name its owning component, which has the direct contract test; the guide's "one interaction requirement per flow" yields to it here.
- **Alternatives:** keep one requirement naming two owners (rejected by the owner).
- **Amends:** [793b188 Technical spec structure: flows and components, with the scenario mapping as a table](#793b188-technical-spec-structure-flows-and-components-with-the-scenario-mapping-as-a-table).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793d2a`, `793d2b`, `793d2e`, `793d2f`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

The retired IDs `793d2c` and `793d2d` are not reused; the new IDs are `793d2e`, `793d2f`, `793d2e1a` to `793d2e1d` and `793d2f1a` to `793d2f1h` (checked with `git log -S`: unused), and `793b217` to `793b223`.
