## 793b224 Judgement runs on the cheapest capable tier

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe, 2026-10-12), in the walkthrough of `793d2b Turn memory work`: every System task that needs model judgement (lesson and conclusion extraction, the covered or absent reconcile, curation outcomes, the authoring of a skill-runbook note, and any other request the mod relays) MUST go to the least capable model tier that can handle that kind of judgement, not the session's model.
- **Choice:** each request the Go side builds names a `model`, taken from one configurable table, `judgeTiers`, keyed by request kind (`extraction`, which also judges the conclusions, `reconcile`, `curation`); the default for every kind is the cheapest tier available to the session (the `haiku` family), with no dated id in the spec. The mod relays `$.model.complete` with the request's model and never substitutes the session's. A kind is raised only on measured evidence that the cheapest tier fails it, and #850 measures judgement quality per kind and is the only path to a raise (`793d4a A job in rounds`, [793d1a What the hypotheses rely on in Claude Code mods](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d1a-what-the-hypotheses-rely-on-in-claude-code-mods) row 18; verified in the mods API docs and declarations that `model` takes an alias such as `haiku` or a full id).
- **Rationale:** the automatic System must be as fast and as cheap as possible; the engram eval validated a haiku-family tier at parity with a top tier on subtle lesson-sifting, and routing is by capability tier, not model name, with only measured evidence raising a tier (as route does).
- **Alternatives:** the session's own model (rejected by the owner; it was [793b212 The judgement model is the session's own model; a cheaper model is a follow-up under #850](#793b212-the-judgement-model-is-the-sessions-own-model-a-cheaper-model-is-a-follow-up-under-850)); one global constant (no per-kind raise).
- **Amends:** [793b212 The judgement model is the session's own model; a cheaper model is a follow-up under #850](#793b212-the-judgement-model-is-the-sessions-own-model-a-cheaper-model-is-a-follow-up-under-850), [793b196 Judgement runs as relayed model completions, not sessions](#793b196-judgement-runs-as-relayed-model-completions-not-sessions).

- **Amended by:** [793b238 Delivery has two model judgements on the cheapest tier: moment classification and a relevance filter](#793b238-delivery-has-two-model-judgements-on-the-cheapest-tier-moment-classification-and-a-relevance-filter): the table keys are `extraction`, `reconcile`, `curation`, `classify` and `relevance`; both new kinds default to the cheapest tier, and raising one changes only that kind's requests.

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): the cheapest tier for every judgement is now hypothesis [793d18 H2: the cheapest capable model is adequate for every System judgement, and is faster and cheaper](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d18-h2-the-cheapest-capable-model-is-adequate-for-every-system-judgement-and-is-faster-and-cheaper), to be confirmed or disproved by its experiments; the table `judgeTiers` is a design draft.
- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793d2b`, `793d4a`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b225 Defined terms link to their definitions

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe, 2026-10-12): "in specs, a defined term, when used, links back to its definition."
- **Choice:** a term defined in a change's Terms list or in [`docs/GLOSSARY.md`](../../GLOSSARY.md) MUST link to its definition at its first use in each addressable node's prose (a requirement paragraph, a rationale, orientation or flow prose, a table cell), with the term as written as the label; the definition, Gherkin and code blocks are not linked. The rule is in the "Defined Terms" subsection of `docs/AGENTS.md` and in the Shared Author Checklist. It is applied to [Parallel Memory Agent - Product Spec](./793a_product-spec.md#parallel-memory-agent---product-spec) and [Parallel Memory Agent - Technical Spec](./793d_technical-spec.md#parallel-memory-agent---technical-spec) by a script: the terms of the Terms list in [793a1 Problem Statement](./793a_product-spec.md#793a1-problem-statement) and [793d1b Terms](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d1b-terms) link to those nodes (the list items are not headings, so the link goes to the nearest heading), and the glossary terms used in these specs (skill, runbook, harness, session, transcript, subagent, offer, evidence note, reversal, confirmed approach, recency channel, targ) link to their glossary entries. Glossary words that these specs use in another sense (trigger, engram, recall, learn, candidate, fact, feedback, binary) are not linked.
- **Rationale:** a reader of one requirement should reach the meaning of a term without searching; the rule is mechanical, so it can be checked.
- **Alternatives:** link every use (noisy); link only the first use in a document (a requirement read alone after promotion would have no link).
- **Amends:** none.

Its wording is unchanged except that **round** is defined as one run of `engram auto run` that advances a job, taken from `793d4a A job in rounds`. The new ID was checked with `git log -S`: unused.

## 793b227 793a3p is removed: route's evidence write stays with the orchestrator

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe, 2026-10-12): "why are we taking over route's rules/actions with the automatic memory? This is already an action the agent performs intentionally. We should only surface a relevant routing memory to the agent if they don't already possess it, which is covered by other requirements."
- **Choice:** `793a3p` (Dispatch outcomes are recorded by the System) is removed. Route's evidence-note write ([792a17k Each dispatch is also written to the vault as a tagged evidence note](../../specs/product/route.md#792a17k-each-dispatch-is-also-written-to-the-vault-as-a-tagged-evidence-note)) stays with the orchestrator following route, as today. The automatic channel only surfaces tier evidence at a dispatch decision ([793a2n Relevant memory reaches the agent at the moments](793a_product-spec.md#793a2n-relevant-memory-reaches-the-agent-at-the-moments), unchanged) and does not deliver what the agent already holds ([793a2e Nothing already in context is delivered again](793a_product-spec.md#793a2e-nothing-already-in-context-is-delivered-again)). The dispatch outcome is also dropped from the no-duplicate rule of [793a3a Lessons are captured without the agent](793a_product-spec.md#793a3a-lessons-are-captured-without-the-agent), from the counts of [793a5h The user sees a summary of the memory activity](793a_product-spec.md#793a5h-the-user-sees-a-summary-of-the-memory-activity) and from the technical spec: the Dispatch Outcome Recorder (`793d8`) is removed, round one of a capture job in `793d2e Job order within a round` no longer calls it, and `events.outcomes` is gone from the job result and the memory-activity line. The product scope is now 13 requirements.
- **Rationale:** writing route's evidence notes is an action the agent already performs on purpose; having the System take it over added a second writer and a parser of route's report rows for no promise the user needs. Surfacing the relevant routing evidence to an agent that does not hold it is already covered by other requirements.
- **Alternatives:** keep `793a3p` and the recorder (rejected by the owner).
- **Amends:** [793b109 793c38: the System writes route's evidence notes](#793b109-793c38-the-system-writes-routes-evidence-notes) and the entries that kept `793a3p` or `793d8`, each of which carries an Amended by line to this entry.

| the `793a3a1j` example row "a dispatch of work-kind K whose outcome note the orchestrator wrote itself" | none | no System-written outcome note exists to duplicate; the other rows stay |
| `793d8`, `793d8a`, `793d8a1`, `793d8a1a`, `793d8a1b` | none | the Dispatch Outcome Recorder is removed |

## 793b228 An intentional learn after an automatic capture may write a duplicate; the fix is filed as an issue

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe, 2026-10-12)
- **Choice:** an intentional learn that follows an automatic capture of the same lesson may write a duplicate note. This is accepted for this change. The fix, a covered / near / absent reconcile inside `engram learn`, is filed as [issue #853](https://github.com/toejough/engram/issues/853).
- **Rationale:** the existing learn system stays as-is in this change. Ordering decides whether the gap appears. Agent-first (the agent writes, then the System captures) is handled by the covered judgement of `793d7c`. System-first (the System captures, then the agent learns) is the gap: learn never checks the vault for a near-sibling, and `793a2k` forbids the System from undoing an intentional run, so it cannot fold the duplicate.
- **Alternatives:** fix learn in this change (rejected: the existing system stays as-is); the System folds agent-written duplicates (rejected: `793a2k` never undoes an intentional run).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a2k`, `793d7c`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b230 Definitions added to 793d1b Terms and the rewordings

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** [793d1b Terms](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d1b-terms) gains Window, Task, `judgeTiers`, `need`, `done`, `state`, `events` and `notices`, and its Job entry now names the three kinds (moment job, capture job, curation job) instead of "a moment, a capture, a curation". Each definition is drawn from the existing uses, which agree: Window (`793d7a`, `793d2b`, `793d2e`): the messages one capture job judges; Task (`793d3b`, `793d4a`): what the receiving agent is working on, as a moment job carries it; `judgeTiers` (`793d4a`, `793d9a`): the kind-to-model table; `need`, `done`, `state`: the runner's result fields. "Moment job", "capture job" and "curation job" are kinds of the defined Job, so they need no separate entry. Two uses carried a second meaning and are reworded: in `793d2e` and `793d4a` the prose "the events and notices" and "the events already counted" (the job's counts, not Claude Code events) became the `events` and `notices` terms; in `793d2b` "produced no event, notice or warning" became "counted no `events` and produced no notice or warning". "Events" otherwise keeps its one meaning, a Claude Code event. Terms with fewer than three uses (report row, context channel) and the named deadline constants (defined where 793d lists its named constants) were left as they are. The uses are linked.
- **Rationale:** each term's uses share one meaning, so one definition serves them; the only divergence was the word "events", kept for Claude Code events and reserved, as `events`, for the job's counts.
- **Alternatives:** a separate entry for each job kind (rejected: they are the Job entry's three cases).
- **Amends:** [793b226 The technical spec's own terms are a node, 793d1b Terms](#793b226-the-technical-specs-own-terms-are-a-node-793d1b-terms).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites 6 IDs, each in the ID map, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b231 Backlog capture: the System captures lessons from any swept session transcript after the fact

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe, 2026-10-12), reviewing [793a5c Other harnesses are unchanged](793a_product-spec.md#793a5c-other-harnesses-are-unchanged). Recorded as [793c67 Review answer: sessions of harnesses without the Integration may be captured after the fact](793c_open-questions.md#793c67-review-answer-sessions-of-harnesses-without-the-integration-may-be-captured-after-the-fact). In his words: "if I have a pi session that runs and is interrupted before the intentional agent can learn from it, the next time I run claude with the integration, I would expect the integration to autonomously pick up that leftover pi session and ingest it, spinning out the relevant chunks and learning the relevant lessons."
- **Choice:** the System MAY capture from sessions of harnesses without the Integration, after the fact; delivery into such sessions stays forbidden, and the session's agent still runs recall and learn. [793a5c Other harnesses are unchanged](793a_product-spec.md#793a5c-other-harnesses-are-unchanged) is amended: such a session's lessons are captured as backlog once the session is no longer running, and its transcript is never changed; scenario `793a5c1b` no longer says that no note is written from the session except by the Pi agent's own learn, but that none is written while the session runs. [793a3a Lessons are captured without the agent](793a_product-spec.md#793a3a-lessons-are-captured-without-the-agent) is amended: it covers the lessons in any session transcript engram sweeps ([792a5d Automatic sweep roots](../../specs/product/chunk-indexing.md#792a5d-automatic-sweep-roots)), captured beginning when a Claude Code session with the Integration next completes a turn, and the provenance "the session and turn it came from" becomes "the source transcript and the position in it"; the rule against writing a duplicate note is kept and extended to a note written at any time before the System reached a backlog session. [793a5h The user sees a summary of the memory activity](793a_product-spec.md#793a5h-the-user-sees-a-summary-of-the-memory-activity) counts the notes of a backlog capture. Indexing of the same transcripts into the chunk index is unchanged (`793a2l Indexing and curation happen without the agent`, [792a5d Automatic sweep roots](../../specs/product/chunk-indexing.md#792a5d-automatic-sweep-roots)).
- **Rationale:** a session can end before its agent learns from it (a Pi session interrupted; a Claude Code session killed mid-turn). Capture only reads a file the harness already wrote, so it needs nothing installed in that harness, and the next Claude Code session with the Integration is the first moment the System runs again.
- **Alternatives:** keep [793a5c Other harnesses are unchanged](793a_product-spec.md#793a5c-other-harnesses-are-unchanged) as written, with no capture from sessions of other harnesses (replaced by the owner's decision).
- **Amends:** [793b156 M8: 793a5c says "installed without the Integration"](793b_decision-log.md#793b156-m8-793a5c-says-installed-without-the-integration).
- **Amended by:** [793b234 Intentional ingestion in other harnesses continues; the System coexists with it through the write lock](#793b234-intentional-ingestion-in-other-harnesses-continues-the-system-coexists-with-it-through-the-write-lock): the System's presence does not change what a harness without the Integration does; its intentional ingestion and learn continue, and the System coexists with them through the write lock.

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): capture from sessions of other harnesses is a preference of [793a1 Problem Statement](./793a_product-spec.md#793a1-problem-statement) covered by [793a3a1k A lesson in a session that ended before it was saved is captured later](./793a_product-spec.md#793a3a1-uat-scenarios); the mechanism is a design draft.
- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793a2l`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b232 Backlog capture is a watermark per transcript source and a scan after each turn's own capture, and the $.store queue is dropped

- **Decided by:** Orchestrator (shown in walkthrough); awaiting owner validation. It applies the owner's rules of [793b231 Backlog capture: the System captures lessons from any swept session transcript after the fact](793b_decision-log.md#793b231-backlog-capture-the-system-captures-lessons-from-any-swept-session-transcript-after-the-fact).
- **Choice:**
  - The System keeps a capture watermark per transcript source (every session transcript `engram ingest --auto` discovers: Claude Code session logs and Pi session files). The store is on the Go side, in engram's own state beside the ingest manifest, not in `$.store`, so it does not depend on the harness (`793d7e The backlog scan and the watermark`).
  - After a turn's own capture, a scan job finds the transcript sources with content beyond their watermark that are not live (no running session on them) and the mod queues one backlog capture job for each, at most `backlogPerTurn` (initial 1) per turn, so the queue never grows without bound. The watermark advances when the job finishes without a warning. For Claude Code the mod's own session is excluded; any other source is live until its file has been unchanged for `backlogQuietMs`.
  - The new requirement is owned by Auto Capture, not by the Auto Job Runner: the scan, the window and the watermark are capture state, and the runner keeps nothing between calls (`793d4a A job in rounds`).
  - This subsumes the `$.store` queue of [793c65 Are captures still queued when a session ends kept for the next session?](793c_open-questions.md#793c65-are-captures-still-queued-when-a-session-ends-kept-for-the-next-session): an interrupted or killed Claude Code session's uncaptured content is caught by the watermark scan at the next session, so the persist-and-drain requirement, its scenario and the `session.end` handler are dropped. The owner answered (a) to that question ([793b219 793c65 is answered (a): unfinished captures are kept in the store for the next session](793b_decision-log.md#793b219-793c65-is-answered-a-unfinished-captures-are-kept-in-the-store-for-the-next-session)) and this generalises his answer; he can veto it.
  - Technical changes: `793d2b Turn memory work`, `793d2e Job order within a round`, `793d4a A job in rounds`, `793d3a Events, channels and pass-through`, `793d7 Added: auto-capture` with `793d7e The backlog scan and the watermark`, the new requirement `793d16a Reading a transcript from a time` of the Transcript Reader, the terms and the platform table ([793d1a What the hypotheses rely on in Claude Code mods](793d_technical-spec.md#793d1a-what-the-hypotheses-rely-on-in-claude-code-mods)), the mapping and the impact.
- **Rationale:** one mechanism covers both gaps, an interrupted session of another harness and a Claude Code session whose queued work was lost, and it rests on files Go reads instead of an unverified platform store (row 15 of [793d1a What the hypotheses rely on in Claude Code mods](793d_technical-spec.md#793d1a-what-the-hypotheses-rely-on-in-claude-code-mods)) and a 1.5 second `session.end` hook that a killed session never runs.
- **Alternatives:** keep the `$.store` queue and add a separate backlog scan (two mechanisms for one gap, one of them unverified); keep the watermark in `$.store` (harness-dependent, unverified); make the Auto Job Runner own the scan (it keeps nothing between calls).
- **Amends:** [793b219 793c65 is answered (a): unfinished captures are kept in the store for the next session](793b_decision-log.md#793b219-793c65-is-answered-a-unfinished-captures-are-kept-in-the-store-for-the-next-session), [793b203 Background work is one in-memory queue, and a final turn can be lost on exit](793b_decision-log.md#793b203-background-work-is-one-in-memory-queue-and-a-final-turn-can-be-lost-on-exit).

ID map for this decision (retired IDs are never reused):

| Retired | Replaced by | Why |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `793d2b1j` | `793d2b1p` | the scenario wrote the unfinished captures to `$.store`; the new one states that the queue is not carried into a later session |
| the `session.end` row of the table in `793d3a Events, channels and pass-through` | none | the handler is removed |
| the clause of the `classic.SessionStart` row of that table that queued the captures kept in `$.store` | records the main agent's `transcript_path` | nothing is kept in `$.store` |
| the `$.store` participant and its two arrows in the diagram of `793d2b Turn memory work` | the scan, Transcript Reader and Durable File Store arrows | the queue is not kept |
| row 15 of [793d1a What the hypotheses rely on in Claude Code mods](793d_technical-spec.md#793d1a-what-the-hypotheses-rely-on-in-claude-code-mods) | kept as a retired row, so the numbers of the rows after it stay | `$.store` is no longer relied on |
| the third bullet of `793d12a` (captures kept in `$.store`) | the backlog bullet | the queue is not kept |

Changed in place under stable IDs: `793a1` (the Integration term, and the terms Transcript source and Backlog), [793a3a Lessons are captured without the agent](793a_product-spec.md#793a3a-lessons-are-captured-without-the-agent), scenarios `793a3a1i` and `793a3a1j`, [793a5c Other harnesses are unchanged](793a_product-spec.md#793a5c-other-harnesses-are-unchanged), scenario `793a5c1b`, [793a5h The user sees a summary of the memory activity](793a_product-spec.md#793a5h-the-user-sees-a-summary-of-the-memory-activity), `793d1`, `793d1a`, `793d1b`, `793d2b Turn memory work`, `793d2e Job order within a round`, `793d3a Events, channels and pass-through`, `793d3f`, `793d4a A job in rounds`, `793d7` (its header), `793d7a`, `793d7c`, `793d11`, `793d12` and `793d12a`.

New IDs (each checked with `git log -S`: unused): `793a3a1k` to `793a3a1o`, `793a5c1c`, `793a5h1i`, `793b231` to `793b233`, `793c67`, `793d7e` with `793d7e1a` to `793d7e1l`, `793d16` with `793d16a` and `793d16a1a` to `793d16a1e`, `793d2b1n`, `793d2b1p`, `793d2b1q`, `793d2b1r`, `793d2e1f`, `793d2e1g`, `793d3f1g`, `793d4a1i`, `793d4a1j`, `793d7a1k`, `793d7c1j` and row 19 of [793d1a What the hypotheses rely on in Claude Code mods](793d_technical-spec.md#793d1a-what-the-hypotheses-rely-on-in-claude-code-mods).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites 32 IDs, each in the ID map, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b233 Choices made in applying 793b232, shown to the owner

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation. The owner can overrule any of these in the walkthrough.
- **Choice:**
  - Liveness applies to every source other than the mod's own session, Claude Code sessions included: a session running in another terminal is quiet-checked like a Pi session, so it is not captured while it runs. `backlogQuietMs` starts at 900000 (15 minutes).
  - A source with no recorded watermark starts at the time the state file was first written, so the existing history of session logs is not mined (that would be an unbounded model cost nobody asked for). A Pi session interrupted before that first write is not captured. The alternative is to capture all history.
  - The scan returns the most recently modified sources first, since the freshest leftover is the one the user most likely has in mind.
  - A watermark is a time, and the window is read from it with a byte budget (`backlogWindowBytes`, initial 100000), so a long transcript is captured over several turns. The Transcript Reader's current realization already reads from a start time with a budget, which its canonical requirements did not state, so `793d16a Reading a transcript from a time` adds that requirement.
  - A turn's capture advances the watermark of its own session's transcript to the end of the turn, and a subagent's capture advances its transcript's, so a session already captured is not captured again as backlog. That needs the main agent's transcript path from `classic.SessionStart` (row 19 of [793d1a What the hypotheses rely on in Claude Code mods](793d_technical-spec.md#793d1a-what-the-hypotheses-rely-on-in-claude-code-mods), To verify); without it a turn's notes name the session id and its transcript may be captured again after it ends, where the reconcile step finds its notes covered.
  - A note's source ends with `; written automatically (source <path>, position <time>)`, the transcript and the position the owner asked for.
  - The scan is a fourth job kind (`scan`), and a backlog capture job is a capture job whose input is a source path, so the runner's order of rounds is unchanged.
  - The warning step for the scan and the backlog capture jobs is named `backlog`.
  - A source whose backlog capture job keeps failing is retried at every turn, with a warning each time. That is a known limit, not a fix.
- **Rationale:** each choice follows the owner's rules of [793b231 Backlog capture: the System captures lessons from any swept session transcript after the fact](793b_decision-log.md#793b231-backlog-capture-the-system-captures-lessons-from-any-swept-session-transcript-after-the-fact) with the least extra state and the fewest new model requests.
- **Alternatives:** stated with each choice.
- **Amends:** [793b232 Backlog capture is a watermark per transcript source and a scan after each turn's own capture, and the $.store queue is dropped](793b_decision-log.md#793b232-backlog-capture-is-a-watermark-per-transcript-source-and-a-scan-after-each-turns-own-capture-and-the-store-queue-is-dropped).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793d16a`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b234 Intentional ingestion in other harnesses continues; the System coexists with it through the write lock

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe, 2026-10-12), reviewing [793a5c Other harnesses are unchanged](793a_product-spec.md#793a5c-other-harnesses-are-unchanged). Recorded as [793c68 Review answer: the System's automatic ingestion does not prevent intentional ingestion in other harnesses](793c_open-questions.md#793c68-review-answer-the-systems-automatic-ingestion-does-not-prevent-intentional-ingestion-in-other-harnesses). In his words: "the addition of automatic ingestion in Claude Code should not prevent the intentional ingestion in harnesses without the integration."
- **Choice:** [793a5c Other harnesses are unchanged](793a_product-spec.md#793a5c-other-harnesses-are-unchanged) states that in a harness without the Integration the working agent's own ingest and learn continue as before and the System MUST NOT block, delay, undo or duplicate them; the System's ingest and backlog capture coexist with them through the vault write lock and `.manifest.lock` ([792b8c1 Exclusive hold](../../specs/technical/vault-write-lock.md#792b8c1-exclusive-hold)). The earlier "MUST NOT capture from the session while it is running" becomes the positive form: backlog capture takes a source only once it is quiet, and a lesson the harness's own agent already saved is covered by the duplicate rule of [793a3a Lessons are captured without the agent](793a_product-spec.md#793a3a-lessons-are-captured-without-the-agent), which names a note another harness's agent wrote. `793d2b Turn memory work` and `793d7e The backlog scan and the watermark` state that the System's commands take the locks any engram command takes and take none of their own, and that an ingest that outlasts `ingestDeadlineMs` waiting for `.manifest.lock` is a warning that leaves the index and the vault as they were, not a corruption. New scenarios: `793a5c1d`, `793a5c1e`, `793a3a1p`, `793d2b1s`, `793d2b1t`, `793d7e1m`.
- **Rationale:** the System is an addition in Claude Code; it must not take anything away from a harness that lacks the Integration. The locks that already serialize concurrent ingest, learn, amend and activate ([vault-write-lock](../../specs/technical/vault-write-lock.md)) are what make the two paths safe together, so no new mechanism is needed.
- **Alternatives:** have the System skip a source an intentional ingest is touching (rejected: the lock already serializes them and the quiet period already keeps capture off a running session).
- **Amends:** [793b231 Backlog capture: the System captures lessons from any swept session transcript after the fact](793b_decision-log.md#793b231-backlog-capture-the-system-captures-lessons-from-any-swept-session-transcript-after-the-fact) and [793b232 Backlog capture is a watermark per transcript source and a scan after each turn's own capture, and the $.store queue is dropped](793b_decision-log.md#793b232-backlog-capture-is-a-watermark-per-transcript-source-and-a-scan-after-each-turns-own-capture-and-the-store-queue-is-dropped).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): coexistence with intentional ingestion is a preference of [793a1 Problem Statement](./793a_product-spec.md#793a1-problem-statement); the lock is a design draft.
- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites 8 IDs, each in the ID map, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b235 The product spec states promises, not mechanisms

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe, 2026-10-12): "The product spec shouldn't include technical details like .manifest.lock or even referencing a lock file at all."
- **Choice:** [793a5c Other harnesses are unchanged](793a_product-spec.md#793a5c-other-harnesses-are-unchanged) states only the promise: when an intentional ingest or learn in such a harness runs at the same time as the System's indexing or capture, both complete and neither corrupts the index or the vault. The vault write lock and `.manifest.lock` stay in the technical spec (`793d2b`, `793d7e`). A sweep of 793a for other mechanism wording made these rewrites:
  - 793a1 Transcript source: "`engram ingest --auto` finds and indexes" to "engram's automatic indexing finds and indexes".
  - 793a5c requirement: the vault-write-lock link and `.manifest.lock` removed; "the System's own ingest and backlog capture ... through the lock" to "indexing and backlog capture ... both MUST complete" (same promise); "chunk index" to "index"; `engram ingest --auto` kept only as "the user-run" command.
  - 793a5c1d: "engram ingest --auto" (Pi agent) to "its own ingest"; "the System running engram ingest --auto and a backlog capture" to "indexing and running a backlog capture"; "chunk index" to "index".
  - 793a4h: "in which mods run" to "that supports the Integration" (mod API name).
  - 793a5h: "in which mods run" to "that has the Integration".
  - 793a4h1a: "~/.claude exists, ~/.pi does not" to "Claude Code is installed, Pi is not"; "~/.pi is not created" to "no Pi configuration is created".
  - 793a4h1d: "~/.claude and ~/.pi both exist; ~/.pi/agent/AGENTS.md imports ..." to "Claude Code and Pi are both installed; Pi is configured with the recall, learn and shim guidance".
  - Kept: `engram update`, `engram ingest --auto` as a user-run command, `/recall`, `/learn`; "backlog" and "content it has not captured before". No scenario needed the mechanism to be stated.
- **Rationale:** the owner's rule that a product spec states what the user can observe, and the mechanism belongs in the technical spec.
- **Alternatives:** keep the lock as a named means (rejected by the owner).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites 6 IDs, each in the ID map, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b236 The mod is validated and tested through the existing integration gate, not a new check target

- **Decided by:** Orchestrator (under owner rules; shown in walkthrough).
- **Choice:** `793d15a` (the Modified operation on `794b4a`) and the `check-mod` target are removed. The Memory Mod is validated and tested by one Go integration test (build tag `integration`) that runs `claude plugin validate` and `claude plugin test` on `agent-instructions/mods/engram-memory/` (the second running the mod's own `*.test.ts` files) and fails, not skips, when a command fails or the `claude` CLI is unavailable. It is the requirement `793d3g Validated and tested through the existing checks` under the Memory Mod component. It supports no product scenario: it is a development gate.
- **Rationale:** the existing targets already cover every Go file the change adds, so the only new thing to check is the JavaScript mod. A Go integration test keeps that under the existing integration gates, like other tests that need an external tool, with no new target and no change to any aggregate. The nil-check requirement `794b4a` was touched only because it is where `check-full`'s dependency list lives; a Modified operation on it was disproportionate, and with it gone no canonical node is modified by this change ([793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory)). The owner asked why the spec touched the nil-check target and why the existing test commands were not enough.
- **Alternatives:** keep `check-mod` in `check-full` and `check-for-fail` ([793b214 check-mod is part of check-full and check-for-fail](#793b214-check-mod-is-part-of-check-full-and-check-for-fail), reversed here; [793b206 The mod is validated and tested by claude plugin validate and claude plugin test in check-full](#793b206-the-mod-is-validated-and-tested-by-claude-plugin-validate-and-claude-plugin-test-in-check-full), reversed here); a separate target outside the aggregates (a silent gap).
- **Amends:** [793b206 The mod is validated and tested by claude plugin validate and claude plugin test in check-full](#793b206-the-mod-is-validated-and-tested-by-claude-plugin-validate-and-claude-plugin-test-in-check-full), [793b214 check-mod is part of check-full and check-for-fail](#793b214-check-mod-is-part-of-check-full-and-check-for-fail).
- **Amended by:** [793b237 The existing-system-as-is decision is scoped; it is not a ban on Modified operations](#793b237-the-existing-system-as-is-decision-is-scoped-it-is-not-a-ban-on-modified-operations): the rationale's "no canonical node is modified by this change" cited a rule the owner did not make. `793d3g` stands on the reasons in [793b237 The existing-system-as-is decision is scoped; it is not a ban on Modified operations](#793b237-the-existing-system-as-is-decision-is-scoped-it-is-not-a-ban-on-modified-operations).
- **ID map:** retired, not reused: `793d15`, `793d15a`, `793d15a1`, `793d15a1a` (the Modified operation on `794b4a` and its scenarios; the scenario `794b4a1c` stays as the canonical text says). Added (checked with `git log -S`: unused): `793d3g`, `793d3g1`, `793d3g1a` to `793d3g1c`, `793b236`. No row of `793d11 Product-scenario mapping` changes: `793d3g` supports no product scenario.

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): the validation gate is a design draft, not a requirement.
- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793d11`, `793d3g`, `793d3g1`, `793d3g1a`, `793d3g1c`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b237 The existing-system-as-is decision is scoped; it is not a ban on Modified operations

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe), 2026-10-12.
- **Choice:** the owner's correction: "no canonical node is modified by this change" is not a rule he made. His decision [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory) was scoped: leave the existing memory system (skills, shim, guidance, please, route, the lessons contract, install) as-is as the intentional channel, and drop the product-spec Modified operations on those nodes. A change spec may modify canonical nodes wherever the change genuinely needs to. `793d3g Validated and tested through the existing checks` (one Go integration test under the existing integration gate) stands on its own merits: no new target, no change to `check-full`'s dependency list, and the existing integration gate already covers tests that need an external tool. It does not rest on a "no canonical node" rule.
- **Rationale:** stated by the owner. The universal form was the agent's over-generalization of [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory).
- **Alternatives:** keep the universal statement. Rejected by the owner.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this decision. Amends [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory) and [793b236 The mod is validated and tested through the existing integration gate, not a new check target](#793b236-the-mod-is-validated-and-tested-through-the-existing-integration-gate-not-a-new-check-target); [793a1 Problem Statement](793a_product-spec.md#793a1-problem-statement) intro prose reworded to the scoped statement.
- **ID map:** `793b237` added (checked with `git log -S`: unused).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793d3g`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b238 Delivery has two model judgements on the cheapest tier: moment classification and a relevance filter

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe, 2026-10-12), option (a) of the question how the delivery side decides when to look and what to deliver. Recorded as [793c69 Review answer: delivery gets two model judgements on the cheapest tier](793c_open-questions.md#793c69-review-answer-delivery-gets-two-model-judgements-on-the-cheapest-tier). As relayed to the author, in his words: the delivery side gets model judgement on the cheapest tier, like capture already has; judgements for the autonomous parts cannot all be deterministic; and the spec's "every decision is made in Go" is wrong.
- **Choice:** two model judgements are added to delivery, both relayed by the mod on the cheapest tier ([793b224 Judgement runs on the cheapest capable tier](#793b224-judgement-runs-on-the-cheapest-capable-tier)) and both keys of `judgeTiers` ([793d1b Terms](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d1b-terms)). (1) **Moment classification** (`classify`): the mechanical cues stay as the trigger for when to look, and at a judged cue (the main agent's first edit and first failure of a turn, and each step of the main agent that ended with text and a tool call) the runner builds a `classify` request over the recent texts: is the agent about to endorse or rank an approach, declare work done, diagnose a failure it cannot explain, or start building a new approach, and on what. The reply names zero or more moments with a one-line subject each; the lookup is made from the subjects, one query for all; no moment, no query. The prompt keeps today's four phrases. (2) **Relevance filter** (`relevance`): after the query and the presence check, when candidates remain, a `relevance` request over the candidates and the moment says which bear on it; only those are delivered and `events.delivered` counts them; no candidate, no request. The moment job is therefore multi-round like capture: the relay loop applies to it, and `momentDeadlineMs` (25 s, an initial value tuned under #850) covers the lookup (about 11 s) and up to two judgement calls of at most `momentJudgeDeadlineMs` (5 s) each. A step's job runs in the background and its delivery is held for the next tool result, so mid-turn delivery rides the next context channel ([793b192 Delivery uses prompt context, tool-result context and subagent-start context](#793b192-delivery-uses-prompt-context-tool-result-context-and-subagent-start-context)). A failed classification skips the moment with a warning and the event continues; a failed relevance filter delivers nothing, with a warning. Applied in `793d6a Moments and lookup`, `793d6b Presence, relevance and delivery text`, `793d4a A job in rounds`, `793d2a Moment delivery`, `793d3a Events, channels and pass-through`, `793d3b State kept for the System`, [793d1a What the hypotheses rely on in Claude Code mods](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d1a-what-the-hypotheses-rely-on-in-claude-code-mods) (row 20), [793d1b Terms](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d1b-terms), `793d12 Recommended impact`, `793d12a Known limits and follow-ups` and `793d13j Moment classification and relevance`; the orientation sentence "Every decision is made in Go" now reads "Go orchestrates over the existing injected interfaces, and every judgement is relayed to the cheapest capable tier".
- **Rationale:** a mechanical cue table cannot tell what the agent is about to do or about what, and a cosine score cannot tell whether a near note bears on the moment; both are judgements. The standing finding is that detection is LLM reasoning, not embedding retrieval: the model detects, the embedder only retrieves. Recall already judges relevance from content and not from cosine alone ([792a14j Coverage is judged by the agent and written once per cluster](../../specs/product/recall.md#792a14j-coverage-is-judged-by-the-agent-and-written-once-per-cluster), [792a19a Recall fires at four decision moments](../../specs/product/ambient-guidance.md#792a19a-recall-fires-at-four-decision-moments)); this moves the same judgement from the agent to the System. The product promise ([793a2n Relevant memory reaches the agent at the moments](793a_product-spec.md#793a2n-relevant-memory-reaches-the-agent-at-the-moments)) is unchanged: it already says "where recall fires", and the product spec states no mechanism.
- **Options presented:** as presented to the owner on 2026-10-12; the owner chose (a). (a) Add both judgements (moment classification and relevance filter) on the cheapest tier, keeping the cue table as the trigger for when to look; recommended, and what [793a2n Relevant memory reaches the agent at the moments](793a_product-spec.md#793a2n-relevant-memory-reaches-the-agent-at-the-moments) promises ("where recall fires"). (b) Add only the relevance filter and keep the mechanical moments. (c) Keep delivery deterministic and file both judgements under #850. Rationale given with the options: the deterministic design contradicted the owner's standing finding "detection is LLM reasoning, not embedding retrieval". Cost stated: one cheap-tier call per assistant text step plus one per delivery with candidates. Options (b) and (c) are rejected by the choice. The deterministic alternative (the design as it stood, with the cue table and a score threshold deciding) is recorded as rejected in `793d13j Moment classification and relevance`.
- **Cost:** per prompt, `please`, dispatch or subagent start, one more cheap call when a candidate remains; per first edit or first failure of a turn, one more cheap call before the query and one after; per text-bearing step, one background `classify` call, and a query and a `relevance` call only when a moment is named (never more than one step job at a time). The first edit and first failure now wait up to 21 s (a query of about 11 s and two calls of at most 5 s) where they waited about 11 s (`793d12 Recommended impact`). Judgement quality is probabilistic and measured under #850.
- **Alternatives:** keep the delivery deterministic (rejected by the owner; `793d13j Moment classification and relevance`); a `classify` call on every tool call (rejected: the cost [793b193 Moments are mechanical cues; the prompt lookup spans recall's four moments](#793b193-moments-are-mechanical-cues-the-prompt-lookup-spans-recalls-four-moments) named); a classification at every cue, including those that name their moment (not chosen by the author, [793b239 Author choices in applying 793b238, shown to the owner](#793b239-author-choices-in-applying-793b238-shown-to-the-owner)).
- **Amends:** [793b193 Moments are mechanical cues; the prompt lookup spans recall's four moments](#793b193-moments-are-mechanical-cues-the-prompt-lookup-spans-recalls-four-moments), [793b224 Judgement runs on the cheapest capable tier](#793b224-judgement-runs-on-the-cheapest-capable-tier), [793b192 Delivery uses prompt context, tool-result context and subagent-start context](#793b192-delivery-uses-prompt-context-tool-result-context-and-subagent-start-context), [793b211 Option comparisons and rejected alternatives moved out of the technical spec](#793b211-option-comparisons-and-rejected-alternatives-moved-out-of-the-technical-spec), [793b197 The Go side is stateless and advances a job one round per call](#793b197-the-go-side-is-stateless-and-advances-a-job-one-round-per-call), [793b189 The System is a Claude Code mod and a Go command, with the logic in Go](#793b189-the-system-is-a-claude-code-mod-and-a-go-command-with-the-logic-in-go).
- **ID map:** added (checked with `git log -S`: unused): `793b238`, `793b239`, `793c69`, `793d13j`, `793d2a1h` to `793d2a1l`, `793d3a1g`, `793d3b1f`, `793d3b1g`, `793d4a1k` to `793d4a1p`, `793d6a1h` to `793d6a1l`, `793d6b1g` to `793d6b1j`; row 20 of [793d1a What the hypotheses rely on in Claude Code mods](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d1a-what-the-hypotheses-rely-on-in-claude-code-mods). The title of `793d6b` changed to "Presence, relevance and delivery text" (its ID and requirement stay) and the links to it were updated. The scenarios `793d4a1a`, `793d6a1b` and `793d6a1e` kept their IDs and changed text; the moments `edit` and `failure` of the old cue table are `judged` cues now.

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): the two judgements are design drafts, and `classify` and `relevance` are two of the five kinds H2 tests ([793d18 H2: the cheapest capable model is adequate for every System judgement, and is faster and cheaper](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d18-h2-the-cheapest-capable-model-is-adequate-for-every-system-judgement-and-is-faster-and-cheaper)).
- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites 23 IDs, each in the ID map, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b239 Author choices in applying 793b238, shown to the owner

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation. Each choice applies [793b238 Delivery has two model judgements on the cheapest tier: moment classification and a relevance filter](#793b238-delivery-has-two-model-judgements-on-the-cheapest-tier-moment-classification-and-a-relevance-filter); the owner can overrule any of them.
- **Choice:**
  - **Where classification runs.** "At the existing cues" is read as the cues whose event does not name the moment: the first edit, the first failure and the new step trigger. `prompt`, `please`, `dispatch` and `subagent` keep their direct lookup, because [793a2n Relevant memory reaches the agent at the moments](793a_product-spec.md#793a2n-relevant-memory-reaches-the-agent-at-the-moments) promises delivery at them whatever a classifier would say (for example [793a2n1j Tier evidence is delivered before a dispatch is routed](793a_product-spec.md#793a2n1-uat-scenarios)), and the prompt keeps its four phrases as the owner said. The relevance filter applies to all of them.
  - **The step event.** The trigger is the `turn.step` result, not its input: the input carries no text (only `messageCount`, `index`, `model`, `effort`, `agentId`), and the result carries `answer` and `toolUses` (row 20 of [793d1a What the hypotheses rely on in Claude Code mods](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d1a-what-the-hypotheses-rely-on-in-claude-code-mods); the declarations and the docs verify the fields; that a pass-through generator costs nothing visible and that the step's message is in `$.session.messages()` when `next(e)` returns are marked To verify, with a fallback). Only a step with text and at least one tool call starts a job, because a text-only step is the final answer and has no later tool result to ride; a subagent's steps (`agentId` set) start none, as [793b194 Subagents get a dispatch delivery and a capture at stop; moments inside a subagent are not detected](#793b194-subagents-get-a-dispatch-delivery-and-a-capture-at-stop-moments-inside-a-subagent-are-not-detected) and [793c63 Must recall moments inside a subagent's own loop be delivered now?](./793c_open-questions.md#793c63-must-recall-moments-inside-a-subagents-own-loop-be-delivered-now) stand.
  - **The recent window.** The last `classifyWindowMessages` (6) user and assistant message texts of `$.session.messages()`, no tool results, the oldest cut first to `classifyWindowChars` (6000) characters in all; for an edit or a failure the event's own text is added to the request. Both are initial values tuned under #850.
  - **Delivery of a step job.** Held, and added to the first later main-agent tool result that forms no job of its own; dropped at `turn.complete`, when `momentDeadlineMs` passed before the job finished, or when any of its ids entered the ledger meanwhile (it would otherwise repeat what the agent holds, against [793a2e Nothing already in context is delivered again](793a_product-spec.md#793a2e-nothing-already-in-context-is-delivered-again)). One step job at a time bounds the cost. Its `events` are counted when it is added, not when it is dropped.
  - **One query for several moments.** The lookup stays one query (`793d6a Moments and lookup`): the named subjects joined as the text, one phrase per named moment, because a query is about 11 s and two would overrun the deadline.
  - **Fail closed.** A failed classification queries nothing; a failed relevance filter delivers nothing, rather than delivering the unfiltered candidates, so that a judgement failure never adds noise to the agent's context.
  - **Budget.** `momentDeadlineMs` stays 25 s and covers query, classify and relevance; each judgement call is bounded by the new `momentJudgeDeadlineMs` (5 s) instead of `judgeDeadlineMs`: 11 s + 5 s + 5 s = 21 s, leaving 4 s for the runner's process starts. The deadline is initial and tuned under #850.
  - **Term and heading.** The title of `793d6b` now names relevance; `classify request`, `relevance request`, `recent texts`, `judged cue`, `step job` and `held delivery` are defined in [793d1b Terms](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d1b-terms).
- **Rationale:** each choice keeps a promise of the product spec that a judgement could break, or keeps a bound the spec already had (one query, the deadline, the ledger).
- **Alternatives:** classification at every cue (breaks the dispatch and subagent promises if the classifier names none); an unfiltered fallback when relevance fails (adds noise on a failure); a job for text-only steps (no channel to ride before the answer ends).

- **Amended by:** [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them): this entry cites `793d6a`, `793d6b`, which that decision retired (its subject was removed or merged; the entry itself is left as written, and the ID map [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) gives each reason).

## 793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe, 2026-10-12), in his words as relayed to the author: "Take your summary of the intent, and make that a simplified product spec. Take items 1&2 and make them a simple tech hypothesis spec. ... My tech items are both hypotheses to be tested. Take the docs, figure out what gherkin scenarios can cover the necessary UAT of the prod spec items, and what scenarios can prove or disprove my assumptions on the implementation side, and make those the scenarios in each doc." Defaults that need no elaboration, from the same direction: do not change what is not specified to change and does not need to (the existing memory system stays as the intentional channel). Preferences, not essential: install delivered in this change by `engram update`; backlog capture from other harnesses; warn the user when automatic memory is skipped; coexist with intentional ingestion.
- **The intent** (the summary the owner approved as the product spec's content): "Engram remembers for the agent, so the agent doesn't have to remember to remember. Memory has two channels, and both stay: (1) Intentional memory, which exists today: the agent or the user run recall, learn, engram query and the rest deliberately, whenever they want. Nothing about this changes, in any harness. (2) Automatic memory, new, on Claude Code only. A System runs beside the working agent, out of its context, and does the memory handling the agent reliably forgets. It surfaces: at the moments where recall should fire, relevant vault memory arrives in front of the agent before it acts, with an instruction to apply it; nothing it already has is repeated. It captures: every lesson in any session, subagents, interrupted sessions and sessions from other harnesses included, lands in the vault without the agent being asked and without duplicating what is already there. It keeps house: indexing and curation happen after the turn, never making anyone wait. It handles memory, not the task: the agent still does all the applying (planning, synthesis, reconciliation), and the System never touches tools, permissions, the model or the prompt. It never gets in the way: if it fails, the session continues as if it were not there, and the user is told; its judgements run on the cheapest capable model, so it is fast and cheap. It is just there: `engram update` installs it, it is active in every session from then on, and the user sees a one-line summary of what it did. Success test: a user who never thinks about memory still gets the right notes at the right moments, and nothing worth keeping is lost."
- **Choice:**
  1. [Parallel Memory Agent - Product Spec](./793a_product-spec.md#parallel-memory-agent---product-spec) is re-derived from that intent: one requirement per bullet of the intent, each with the UAT scenarios mined from the earlier spec, and nothing that the intent does not state. The result is 10 requirements. Where a surviving requirement is the same promise its ID is kept; a requirement that moved to another section of the intent's order has a new ID under its section; the rest are retired ([793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240)).
  2. [Parallel Memory Agent - Technical Spec](./793d_technical-spec.md#parallel-memory-agent---technical-spec) is reduced to the owner's two assumptions about the implementation, stated as hypotheses to be tested: [793d17 H1: a Claude Code mod gives a smaller implementation surface and more capability than settings hooks](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d17-h1-a-claude-code-mod-gives-a-smaller-implementation-surface-and-more-capability-than-settings-hooks) (a mod over settings hooks) and [793d18 H2: the cheapest capable model is adequate for every System judgement, and is faster and cheaper](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d18-h2-the-cheapest-capable-model-is-adequate-for-every-system-judgement-and-is-faster-and-cheaper) (the cheapest capable model for every judgement). Each has the owner's reason, what confirms it, what disproves it, and the Gherkin scenarios that are runnable experiments or contract tests. A short section points to the decision log for every other author choice ([793d19 Design as currently drafted](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d19-design-as-currently-drafted)), which moves to the implementation plan later, and a table traces each product scenario to the hypothesis scenario or design note that realises it.
  3. The earlier technical requirements, flows, components, option comparisons and impact analysis are retired as requirements. The decision log keeps their rationale, and the mod, the round protocol, the ledger, the watermark and the constants stay recorded there as design drafts.
  4. No new open question ([793c_open-questions.md](./793c_open-questions.md) is unchanged except for the "Amended by" lines and the plain-text form of links to retired IDs).
- **Rationale:** the specs had accumulated author, reviewer and orchestrator design choices as requirements, with owner preferences and observations mixed in as if they were all essential, so that the owner could not recognise his intent. The product spec is the intent as user-verifiable promises; the technical spec is the owner's implementation asks, framed as the assumptions they are.
- **Alternatives:** keep the 13-requirement product spec and the full technical spec. Not chosen by the owner.
- **Amends:** [793b182 Reduce 793a to the minimum set of end-to-end, user-verifiable requirements; layer automatic memory beside intentional memory](#793b182-reduce-793a-to-the-minimum-set-of-end-to-end-user-verifiable-requirements-layer-automatic-memory-beside-intentional-memory), [793b187 The product spec is approved after the owner's walkthrough](#793b187-the-product-spec-is-approved-after-the-owners-walkthrough), [793b188 Technical spec structure: flows and components, with the scenario mapping as a table](#793b188-technical-spec-structure-flows-and-components-with-the-scenario-mapping-as-a-table), [793b189 The System is a Claude Code mod and a Go command, with the logic in Go](#793b189-the-system-is-a-claude-code-mod-and-a-go-command-with-the-logic-in-go), [793b193 Moments are mechanical cues; the prompt lookup spans recall's four moments](#793b193-moments-are-mechanical-cues-the-prompt-lookup-spans-recalls-four-moments), [793b224 Judgement runs on the cheapest capable tier](#793b224-judgement-runs-on-the-cheapest-capable-tier), [793b238 Delivery has two model judgements on the cheapest tier: moment classification and a relevance filter](#793b238-delivery-has-two-model-judgements-on-the-cheapest-tier-moment-classification-and-a-relevance-filter), and the entries listed in [793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240) that cite a retired ID.
- **Status:** applied in the commit that records this decision.
