## 793b242 Author choices in applying 793b240, shown to the owner

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation. Each choice applies [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them); the owner can overrule any of them.
- **Choice:**
  - **Retired because they are not in the intent.** Attribution of delivered items (`793a2d`), persisting the agent's sound conclusions (`793a3q`), the secrets clause of `793a4c`, the provenance record of `793a3a`, the parent-unreachable message of `793a5h` and the rule about the System's own sessions (`793a4f`). Each was approved earlier in the owner's walkthrough; the owner can reinstate any of them with one line. The secrets clause is the one most worth a second look, because a lesson that contains a key would reach the vault.
  - **Moved IDs.** The product spec follows the intent's order, so three requirements moved to the section the intent puts them in and have new IDs there (`793a2l` to [793a4j Indexing and curation happen after the turn](./793a_product-spec.md#793a4j-indexing-and-curation-happen-after-the-turn), `793a4h` to [793a5j Installed by engram update and active in Claude Code](./793a_product-spec.md#793a5j-installed-by-engram-update-and-active-in-claude-code), `793a2k` to [793a5k Intentional memory is never blocked or changed](./793a_product-spec.md#793a5k-intentional-memory-is-never-blocked-or-changed)); the lifecycle guide gives a node placed under the wrong parent a new ID beneath the right one.
  - **Preferences.** Each of the four preferences has one line in the problem statement of [793a1 Problem Statement](./793a_product-spec.md#793a1-problem-statement). Where a scenario needs the behavior (a session that ended before its lessons were saved, the user's warning, the install by `engram update`, the System not blocking intentional ingestion), the requirement keeps it without the earlier detail: no quiet period, no watermark, no lock, no concurrency scenario.
  - **"Fast and cheap".** The intent says the System's judgements run on the cheapest capable model so that it is fast and cheap. That is a mechanism and a measure, not an observable promise, so it is not a product requirement; the problem statement points to [793d18 H2: the cheapest capable model is adequate for every System judgement, and is faster and cheaper](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d18-h2-the-cheapest-capable-model-is-adequate-for-every-system-judgement-and-is-faster-and-cheaper).
  - **Scenarios.** Scenarios that differed only in their input are merged into outlines, and each kept requirement has at most six scenarios ([793b241 ID map for 793b240](#793b241-id-map-for-793b240)). [793a2n Relevant memory reaches the agent at the moments](./793a_product-spec.md#793a2n-relevant-memory-reaches-the-agent-at-the-moments) has six because its five moments and its "nothing relevant" branch each need one.
  - **Numbers in the hypotheses.** The owner gave none. The author chose, as initial values the owner can change: a margin of 0.05, 3 runs per fixture per tier, at least 40 fixtures per kind (at least 10 of them with the gold "none" or "absent") and at most 200, a 50 ms step-delay budget, and a 5 s bound for the moment judgements taken from the earlier draft. A gap inside the spread of the session model's own runs is "inconclusive", not a tie.
  - **Gold labels.** They come from the transcripts and the vault's own history (what the agent did next, what learn recorded, what curation decided) and from the owner's labels for relevance, never from the output of either tier under test.
  - **The evidence ledger** keeps the rows the hypotheses use, each with the scenario that settles it; the fallbacks of the earlier table stay recorded in the decision log.
  - **Stability of the mod** (early access) is recorded with the H1 result but neither confirms nor disproves it, because the owner's hypothesis is about surface and capability.
  - **Entries that cite retired IDs.** Each gets an "Amended by" line, and a link to a retired ID is written as plain text, in both this log and the open-questions file.
- **Rationale:** each choice keeps to the owner's direction that defaults and preferences are noted, not elaborated, and that author choices stay out of the specs.
- **Alternatives:** keep the retired requirements as MAY (rejected: the owner asked for the intent only); give the three moved requirements their old IDs (rejected: the IDs would sit under the wrong section).


## 793b243 The harness is godog over scenarios extracted from the product spec at run time

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation. It applies the owner's direction of 2026-10-12: "Build the harness that tests the product spec scenarios. Then build a PoC that makes them pass. Use whatever language, libraries, and tools you want, optimizing for minimum repo-owned code and conciseness and clarity of expression of intent."
- **Choice:** the harness is the Go package `dev/acceptance`, built on godog (`github.com/cucumber/godog`, one new module dependency). A small extractor reads the `gherkin` blocks of [Parallel Memory Agent - Product Spec](./793a_product-spec.md#parallel-memory-agent---product-spec) at run time and hands them to godog as in-memory feature contents; the only text it adds is a `Feature:` line per `UAT Scenarios` heading. No `.feature` file is committed, so the spec is the test source and the scenario IDs in the scenario names are the trace. The spec path is a parameter, repointed at the canonical product spec when the change is promoted.
- **Rationale:** Go is the repository language and targ runs `go test`; godog parses `Scenario Outline` and `Examples` natively and reports one subtest per scenario, so one dependency and a few hundred lines of step code replace a runner of our own, and the scenario text runs as written.
- **Alternatives:** pytest-bdd (a Python toolchain beside targ, outside the `go test` gate; rejected); a hand-written Gherkin interpreter (more owned code); committed `.feature` files copied from the spec (a second copy that drifts, and the extractor is no larger); a separate Go module for the harness (isolates the dependency but leaves the `integration` pattern the owner asked for).
- **Amended by:** [793b275 The harness and the PoC are JavaScript; only the update's mod copies stay Go](#793b275-the-harness-and-the-poc-are-javascript-only-the-updates-mod-copies-stay-go): the harness is JavaScript (cucumber-js) and the owner replaced godog.

## 793b244 Acceptance runs are TestAcceptance under the integration tag, run by targ acceptance, not by check-full

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation. The owner asked that the harness sit under the existing integration/e2e build-tag pattern so that targ can run it.
- **Choice:** the harness files carry the existing build tag `integration`, which already exempts them from the lint, coverage and nil gates (no new gate). The live-session suite's top-level test is `TestAcceptance`, with one subtest per scenario named `<scenario ID> <title>` (an ID-bearing subtest, as the implementation plan guide allows). It is not named `TestIntegration...`, because the shared `test-integration` target runs `-run TestIntegration` inside `check-full`, which would put about fifty live sessions, real credentials and their cost into every check. `dev/targs.go` registers one target, `acceptance [filter]`, that runs `go test -tags=integration -run TestAcceptance/<filter> ./dev/acceptance/`. The cheap, deterministic checks (the extractor test, and `claude plugin validate` and `claude plugin test` of the mod per [793b236 The mod is validated and tested through the existing integration gate, not a new check target](#793b236-the-mod-is-validated-and-tested-through-the-existing-integration-gate-not-a-new-check-target)) are named `TestIntegration...` and do run in `check-full`.
- **Rationale:** the live suite needs credentials, the network and tens of minutes; the gate that every change runs must not.
- **Alternatives:** name every test `TestIntegration...` (every `check-full` then needs credentials); a new build tag `acceptance` (a new tag for no gain over the name); a script outside targ (not the repository's convention).
- **Amended by:** [793b260 The live suite has its own build tag, acceptance, and check-full never runs it](#793b260-the-live-suite-has-its-own-build-tag-acceptance-and-check-full-never-runs-it): the live suite has its own build tag, `acceptance`; the "no gain" reasoning above was wrong, because the integration tag is selected by tag with no `-run` filter and so `check-full` would run the suite.


## 793b245 Scenarios run real claude sessions in an isolated home with credentials from the environment

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** each scenario gets its own temporary HOME, XDG directories, vault, workspace and GOBIN, and a PATH with the engram binary built for the run first. `engram update` runs in local-clone mode against that HOME (so the install under test is the real install path), and nothing under the real `~/.claude`, `~/.pi`, vault or GOBIN is read or written. The embedder's model cache is the one shared, read-only thing (`XDG_CACHE_HOME`), because its files are content-addressed and unpacking them costs about 11 seconds per process. Observed while drafting this plan on Claude Code 2.1.296: `claude -p` in a temporary HOME answers "Not logged in", so the suite takes its credentials from `CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN` or `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` in the environment and fails with that message when neither is set. It never copies or reads stored credentials.
- **Rationale:** the owner's isolation rule (temporary HOMEs only) and the scenarios' claims about what a real session sees.
- **Alternatives:** copy the stored login into the temporary HOME (reads the real `~/.claude`; forbidden); share the real HOME (forbidden); a mocked model (the owner: flaky-by-nature scenarios get retries, not mocks).
- **Amended by:** [793b261 The World installs by running engram update in local mode, without the go install leg](#793b261-the-world-installs-by-running-engram-update-in-local-mode-without-the-go-install-leg): the World runs `engram update` with the worktree as working directory, `ENGRAM_UPDATE_REEXEC=1` and a per-scenario TMPDIR, so the `go install` leg is skipped.
- **Amended by:** [793b267 The OAuth token comes from a file the harness reads only when the variable is unset](#793b267-the-oauth-token-comes-from-a-file-the-harness-reads-only-when-the-variable-is-unset): the credential source includes the owner's token file, read only when the variable is unset.
- **Amended by:** [793b276 The harness is cucumber-js in plain JavaScript over the Agent SDK, run by targ acceptance](#793b276-the-harness-is-cucumber-js-in-plain-javascript-over-the-agent-sdk-run-by-targ-acceptance): the session is an Agent SDK query with an explicit environment; the credentials rule is unchanged.

## 793b246 Assertions read the transcript, the vault and a harness-only seam log; only preconditions are retried

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** four observables. (1) The session transcript JSONL that Claude Code writes under the temporary HOME, and the subagent transcripts beside it: delivery, attribution, absence of requests, tool calls, permission decisions. (2) The vault files and `engram query` and `engram show` output. (3) The install tree under the temporary HOME. (4) A seam log: when the environment variable `ENGRAM_AUTO_SEAM_LOG` names a file, the mod appends there every line it would show the user (the summary and the warnings, the strings it passes to `$.ui.status`) and an `idle` line each time its queue drains. `claude -p` draws no interface, so the scenarios of [793a5h The user sees a summary of the memory activity](./793a_product-spec.md#793a5h-the-user-sees-a-summary-of-the-memory-activity) and [793a4b The session is never broken or stalled](./793a_product-spec.md#793a4b-the-session-is-never-broken-or-stalled) assert on the seam log and say so in their catalog rows; the seam is the only harness-only code in the product (about six lines of JavaScript). Scenarios whose precondition is the agent's own behaviour (it declares the rename done, dispatches the subagent, runs the skill, states a cause that is overturned) retry their Given and When up to three times until the precondition is observed in the transcript; the Then is never retried. A precondition not observed in three attempts fails the scenario.
- **Rationale:** the owner asked that flaky-by-nature scenarios get retries, not mocks, and that the status and warning be captured through a mod-written log in `-p`. Retrying only the precondition keeps a real miss of the System from being retried away.
- **Alternatives:** retry the whole scenario (hides misses); an LLM judge for the Then (a second source of error); no seam, assert on screen scrapes (not possible in `-p`).
- **Amended by:** [793b266 Non-blocking review fixes in the plan](#793b266-non-blocking-review-fixes-in-the-plan): the precondition retries live inside the When step and rebuild the World (N5), "identical behaviour" is defined (N6), and the seam is the `$.ui.status` call site (N7).


## 793b247 Scenario data lives in one fixtures file keyed by scenario ID, so step definitions stay generic

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** `dev/acceptance/testdata/scenarios.yaml` (`go.yaml.in/yaml/v3` is already a dependency) holds, per scenario ID, the notes to seed (kind, situation, a body carrying a unique sentinel token), the workspace files, the fixed prompt script and the keywords that identify a lesson. About thirty step definitions, parameterised by the step text and that data, cover the roughly 170 step lines of the 43 scenarios. Notes are written through the real write path (`engram learn`), never by hand-written frontmatter.
- **Rationale:** a fixed prompt that reliably produces the behaviour is data, not code; a unique sentinel makes "the note's content was delivered" a string match; keywords make "a note stating the rule exists" a deterministic check on a paraphrase the model writes.
- **Alternatives:** one step definition per distinct sentence; one Go function per scenario.
- **Amended by:** [793b263 godog runs strict with anchored steps, and the scenario data holds inputs only](#793b263-godog-runs-strict-with-anchored-steps-and-the-scenario-data-holds-inputs-only): scenario data holds inputs only; the step definitions are 60 to 90 anchored ones and hold every expected outcome; godog runs strict.
- **Amended by:** [793b276 The harness is cucumber-js in plain JavaScript over the Agent SDK, run by targ acceptance](#793b276-the-harness-is-cucumber-js-in-plain-javascript-over-the-agent-sdk-run-by-targ-acceptance): the data file is `scenarios.js` and holds inputs only.

## 793b248 The session driver streams input and waits for the System's idle marker

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** sessions run `claude -p --input-format stream-json --output-format stream-json`, which keeps the session open between turns. The System's work after a turn then is not cut off by the process exiting ([793b203 Background work is one in-memory queue, and a final turn can be lost on exit](#793b203-background-work-is-one-in-memory-queue-and-a-final-turn-can-be-lost-on-exit)), "the user submits the next prompt immediately" ([793a4j1e Slow housekeeping does not stall the session](./793a_product-spec.md#793a4j1-uat-scenarios)) is a real second message, and compaction and resume have a session to act on. The driver ends the session only after the seam log shows `idle` (or a deadline). Spike SP13 verifies this on the platform; if `-p` still stops the module when the turn's result is written, the fallback is an interactive session under tmux for the scenarios that need background work.
- **Rationale:** the drafted design does its work after `turn.complete`; a one-shot `-p` run ends when the answer is written.
- **Alternatives:** one-shot `-p` runs with a sleep (races); an interactive session for every scenario (slower, needs a pty).
- **Amended by:** [793b268 The PoC is minimal and targeted: environments the owner does not use and everything no scenario needs are cut](#793b268-the-poc-is-minimal-and-targeted-environments-the-owner-does-not-use-and-everything-no-scenario-needs-are-cut): the tmux fallback for a failing SP13 is cut; a failing SP13 stops for the owner.
- **Amended by:** [793b276 The harness is cucumber-js in plain JavaScript over the Agent SDK, run by targ acceptance](#793b276-the-harness-is-cucumber-js-in-plain-javascript-over-the-agent-sdk-run-by-targ-acceptance): sessions are Agent SDK queries with streaming input; the idle marker is unchanged.

## 793b249 Scenarios whose prompt origin cannot be produced in -p get a contract-test substitute

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** the rows of [793a2n1h A prompt from any source receives relevant notes](./793a_product-spec.md#793a2n1-uat-scenarios) for a task notification, a scheduled trigger and a message from another session try a live driver first (spike SP9: a background command's completion notice through streaming input; a message from a second session; a scheduled trigger if the session has one). A row with no live driver runs as a `claude plugin test` test of the mod that fires the event with that origin and asserts the context the mod adds, and is marked `Substitute` in its catalog row. The same applies to a Pi session if Pi cannot run headless in an isolated home (spike SP14): the Pi transcript is then a fixture file written in the format the Transcript Reader parses, and the row says so.
- **Rationale:** the owner: scenarios not runnable in `-p` get a documented substitute assertion.
- **Alternatives:** leave the rows red (the harness would then never reach green); fake the origin with the mod's own `$.prompt.submit` (it does not carry that origin).
- **Amended by:** [793b264 Results include Substituted, and Pi-agent behaviour is not faked with fixtures](#793b264-results-include-substituted-and-pi-agent-behaviour-is-not-faked-with-fixtures): a substitute ends `Substituted: product promise unverified`, and a Pi-agent behaviour is not faked with a fixture.
- **Amended by:** [793b266 Non-blocking review fixes in the plan](#793b266-non-blocking-review-fixes-in-the-plan): the live driver for the non-human origins is spike SP8, not SP9 as the choice above says.


## 793b250 The PoC is the drafted design cut to what the 43 scenarios need

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation. It applies "the drafted design, minimal; only what the 43 scenarios need; nothing speculative" and the design of [793b189 The System is a Claude Code mod and a Go command, with the logic in Go](#793b189-the-system-is-a-claude-code-mod-and-a-go-command-with-the-logic-in-go), [793b197 The Go side is stateless and advances a job one round per call](#793b197-the-go-side-is-stateless-and-advances-a-job-one-round-per-call), [793b192 Delivery uses prompt context, tool-result context and subagent-start context](#793b192-delivery-uses-prompt-context-tool-result-context-and-subagent-start-context) and [793b195 Presence is read from the live context and a delivery ledger](#793b195-presence-is-read-from-the-live-context-and-a-delivery-ledger).
- **Choice:** kept: the mod events `prompt.submit` (context), `classic.PostToolUse` for the Skill call of route (the route cue), `classic.SubagentStart` (context), `classic.SubagentStop` and `turn.complete` (capture, then `engram ingest --auto`, then curation, then the summary), `session.compact` (ledger); `$.model.complete` on `haiku` for the kinds `relevance`, `extraction`, `reconcile` and `curation` from one `judgeTiers` table; the `need`/`done` round protocol of `engram auto run`; one in-memory first-in-first-out queue; presence from the live messages plus a ledger; a watermark scan for backlog capture. Cut because no scenario needs it: the `classify` judgement and the judged cues (first edit, first failure, text-bearing step), the `turn.step` generator and held delivery, secrets redaction, the tool-result channel for anything but the route cue, `$.store`, the dispatch outcome recorder and the late delivery at stop. The consequence for H2 (the `classify` kind has no realisation) is handled in [793b253 The hypothesis scenarios are updated to the PoC before they are run; unrealised kinds are recorded, not built](#793b253-the-hypothesis-scenarios-are-updated-to-the-poc-before-they-are-run-unrealised-kinds-are-recorded-not-built). No new `cli.Primitives` field is expected: the Go side reads and writes the vault through the in-process query, learn and amend functions and reads transcripts through the existing file-system capability; node [793e8b Delivery at the moments](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8b-delivery-at-the-moments) confirms this by reading the call chains and adds a field per the DI rule if an I/O appears that none provides.
- **Rationale:** the product spec's scenarios are the scope; the cues that the 43 scenarios do not reach would be speculative code.
- **Alternatives:** a JavaScript-only mod that shells to `engram` through `$.process.run` (smaller: no runner, no coverage obligation; not chosen because the owner's direction asks for the drafted design and H1 and H2 measure that design; it stays the smaller fallback if the owner prefers); the full drafted design (speculative).
- **Amended by:** [793b256 The PoC orchestrates in JavaScript inside the mod over the existing engram commands](#793b256-the-poc-orchestrates-in-javascript-inside-the-mod-over-the-existing-engram-commands): the `need`/`done` round protocol and `engram auto run` are dropped; one small Go command, `engram backlog`, stays.
- **Amended by:** [793b268 The PoC is minimal and targeted: environments the owner does not use and everything no scenario needs are cut](#793b268-the-poc-is-minimal-and-targeted-environments-the-owner-does-not-use-and-everything-no-scenario-needs-are-cut): the cuts that the owner added to the PoC's scope.
- **Amended by:** [793b279 Judgement is one richer prompt per kind, and capture judges extraction and reconciliation in one call](#793b279-judgement-is-one-richer-prompt-per-kind-and-capture-judges-extraction-and-reconciliation-in-one-call): the judgement kinds are `relevance`, `capture` and `curation`.

## 793b251 The mod deploys through the skills subtree and links, for Claude Code only

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation. It changes the root path of [793b191 engram update installs the mod as a skills-directory plugin](#793b191-engram-update-installs-the-mod-as-a-skills-directory-plugin); the owner can overrule.
- **Choice:** the mod's source is `agent-instructions/mods/engram-memory/`. `engram update` plans its copies like skill copies, but only for a harness flagged to take mods (Claude Code), with the destination under that harness's skills directory. The existing machinery then gives real files at `~/.claude/engram/skills/engram-memory` and the link `~/.claude/skills/engram-memory` that Claude Code loads as a skills-directory plugin, with dry run, idempotence, sync deletion and stray reporting, and no new managed subtree. Pi receives nothing and no Pi directory is created.
- **Rationale:** [793b191 engram update installs the mod as a skills-directory plugin](#793b191-engram-update-installs-the-mod-as-a-skills-directory-plugin) wanted a separate `mods/` subtree of the engram root; that adds sync, prune and dangling-link code for one directory. The load path Claude Code uses (a folder with `.claude-plugin/plugin.json` under `~/.claude/skills`) is the same.
- **Alternatives:** a new `mods/` subtree (more update code); the mod under `agent-instructions/skills/` (Pi would receive it, against [793a5j Installed by engram update and active in Claude Code](./793a_product-spec.md#793a5j-installed-by-engram-update-and-active-in-claude-code)). Risk carried to node [793e8e Install by engram update](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8e-install-by-engram-update): skill-registration scanning may see the directory; the node's tests show it does not offer it as a skill or sync-delete it.

## 793b252 The owner's node 5 is two nodes, the backlog node follows capture, and the hypothesis nodes follow the PoC

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** the owner's node list maps to plan nodes as (1) [793e8a Harness skeleton and platform spikes](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8a-harness-skeleton-and-platform-spikes), (2) [793e8b Delivery at the moments](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8b-delivery-at-the-moments), (3) [793e8c Capture at turn end](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8c-capture-at-turn-end), (4) [793e8d Housekeeping, purview and failure isolation](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8d-housekeeping-purview-and-failure-isolation), (5) [793e8e Install by engram update](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8e-install-by-engram-update) install and [793e8f Summary, intentional memory and other harnesses](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8f-summary-intentional-memory-and-other-harnesses) summary, intentional memory and other harnesses, (6) [793e8g Backlog capture](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8g-backlog-capture), and the owner's additions (7) [793e8h Hypothesis scenarios updated to the PoC](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8h-hypothesis-scenarios-updated-to-the-poc), (8) [793e8i H1 probe harness and verdict](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8i-h1-probe-harness-and-verdict) for H1 and [793e8j H2 eval and verdict](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8j-h2-eval-and-verdict) for H2. The scenarios of an outline whose examples follow different flows are split across the nodes that own each flow ([793a3a1j A lesson that is already recorded is not written again](./793a_product-spec.md#793a3a1-uat-scenarios): its first example in [793e8c Capture at turn end](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8c-capture-at-turn-end), the other two in [793e8g Backlog capture](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8g-backlog-capture)).
- **Rationale:** the implementation plan guide separates independently executable work: install needs only capture, the summary needs curation, backlog needs only capture, and H1 and H2 do not depend on each other.
- **Alternatives:** the owner's node list as written (one node 5, one node 8); one row per outline holding all its examples (hides that two examples need the backlog scan).
- **Amended by:** [793b266 Non-blocking review fixes in the plan](#793b266-non-blocking-review-fixes-in-the-plan): install follows delivery (N2), so the graph is a, b, e, c, then d and g, then f, h, i and j.


## 793b253 The hypothesis scenarios are updated to the PoC before they are run; unrealised kinds are recorded, not built

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe, 2026-10-12) for the two closing nodes: after the PoC passes, validate or update the 793d hypothesis scenarios against what the PoC does (fixtures, margins and measurement points, IDs kept, changes recorded here), then run them (an H1 probe harness over the mod and settings-hook surfaces with the evidence ledger, and an H2 eval with gold-labelled fixtures per kind, the cheapest tier against the session model, latency, cost and noise, as a labelled criteria table with units) and end with a verdict per hypothesis. Author (agent) for the following expectations, awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** node [793e8h Hypothesis scenarios updated to the PoC](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8h-hypothesis-scenarios-updated-to-the-poc) reviews every scenario of [793d17 H1: a Claude Code mod gives a smaller implementation surface and more capability than settings hooks](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d17-h1-a-claude-code-mod-gives-a-smaller-implementation-surface-and-more-capability-than-settings-hooks) and [793d18 H2: the cheapest capable model is adequate for every System judgement, and is faster and cheaper](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d18-h2-the-cheapest-capable-model-is-adequate-for-every-system-judgement-and-is-faster-and-cheaper) against the PoC and changes only what cannot run as written. Known now: (a) the `classify` kind has no PoC realisation ([793b250 The PoC is the drafted design cut to what the 43 scenarios need](#793b250-the-poc-is-the-drafted-design-cut-to-what-the-43-scenarios-need)), so H2 is run for the four kinds the PoC has and `classify` is recorded as not built unless the owner asks for it; (b) the latency and cost scenarios replay each fixture through `claude -p --model <alias> --output-format json` and read `duration_api_ms` and `total_cost_usd`, because both tiers need the same request text and the same measurement, and the report records the difference from a relayed `$.model.complete` call; (c) rows that need an environment this machine lacks (a cloud provider and a gateway in 793d17c1a, Claude Code 2.1.200 in 793d17d1b, managed policy, WSL and the VS Code panel in 793d17d1c) are recorded as not run, which leaves H1 inconclusive for that condition under the spec's own rule; (d) the screen check of 793d17b1a runs the session under tmux. Gold labels come from this repository's transcripts and vault history and are copied into the repository only after the owner approves the sample ([793d18 H2: the cheapest capable model is adequate for every System judgement, and is faster and cheaper](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d18-h2-the-cheapest-capable-model-is-adequate-for-every-system-judgement-and-is-faster-and-cheaper) already requires the owner's confirmation); the real `~/.claude` and vault are off limits to the runs. The H2 eval reuses the patterns of `dev/eval` (dependency struct, run loop, JSONL results writer, aggregation and scoring).
- **Rationale:** a hypothesis scenario that cannot run as written gives an inconclusive result by accident; changing it before the run, with the change recorded, keeps the verdict honest.
- **Alternatives:** run the scenarios as written and record the failures; build `classify` for the eval only (code with no product scenario).
- **Amended by:** [793b265 H2 is replayed by a small mod over fixtures, and gold comes from history or the owner](#793b265-h2-is-replayed-by-a-small-mod-over-fixtures-and-gold-comes-from-history-or-the-owner): item (b): the replay is a small mod over `$.model.complete`, not `claude -p --output-format json`.
- **Amended by:** [793b268 The PoC is minimal and targeted: environments the owner does not use and everything no scenario needs are cut](#793b268-the-poc-is-minimal-and-targeted-environments-the-owner-does-not-use-and-everything-no-scenario-needs-are-cut): item (c): the rows for environments the owner does not use are removed from the technical spec, not recorded as not run.


## 793b254 The CLI absent skips, anything else missing fails

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation. It follows the owner's direction of 2026-10-12: skip with a reason only when the `claude` CLI is absent, fail otherwise.
- **Choice:** the acceptance suite and the mod's contract test skip with a reason when `claude` is not on PATH, and fail, with the missing item named, when `claude` is older than 2.1.287, when neither credential variable is set (acceptance only), or when `go`, `git` or the model asset is missing. This amends [793b236 The mod is validated and tested through the existing integration gate, not a new check target](#793b236-the-mod-is-validated-and-tested-through-the-existing-integration-gate-not-a-new-check-target), which said the contract test fails when the CLI is unavailable.
- **Rationale:** the owner's later and more specific direction; a machine without Claude Code then has a green `check-full` with a visible skip.
- **Alternatives:** keep the failure of 793b236 (`check-full` red on a machine without Claude Code).
- **Amended by:** [793b267 The OAuth token comes from a file the harness reads only when the variable is unset](#793b267-the-oauth-token-comes-from-a-file-the-harness-reads-only-when-the-variable-is-unset): the credential source includes the owner's token file, read only when the variable is unset.


ID map for 793b243 to 793b254 (each checked with `git log -S`: unused): `793b243` to `793b254`, and the implementation plan's own IDs `793e1` to `793e9` with the nodes `793e8a` to `793e8j`. Nothing is retired.
- **Amended by:** [793b276 The harness is cucumber-js in plain JavaScript over the Agent SDK, run by targ acceptance](#793b276-the-harness-is-cucumber-js-in-plain-javascript-over-the-agent-sdk-run-by-targ-acceptance): `targ acceptance` fails, naming the program, when `claude` or `node` is absent; the mod contract test and the extractor test still skip.

This is the one agent review round for [793e1 Purpose](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e1-purpose) ([AGENTS.md, Decisions and Open Questions](../../AGENTS.md#decisions-and-open-questions)); the owner walkthrough follows.

## 793b256 The PoC orchestrates in JavaScript inside the mod over the existing engram commands

- **Decided by:** Orchestrator (under owner rules; shown in walkthrough).
- **Choice:** the [mod](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d1b-terms) does the memory logic itself. It runs the existing commands `engram query`, `learn`, `amend`, `activate`, `ingest --auto`, `show` and the commands of curate through `$.process.run`, and calls `$.model.complete` directly on the model of the kind's `judgeTiers` row. There is no `internal/auto` Go runner and no `need`/`done` round protocol. One small Go command stays, `engram backlog`: the backlog scan needs the stripped transcript text and the default source roots, which exist only in Go (`internal/context` strip, about 800 lines, and the roots of `engram ingest --auto`); a JavaScript copy would duplicate them. The command lists the newest non-live transcript source with content beyond its watermark, prints the stripped text of a given transcript, and advances a watermark when told. Everything else the Go side was to do is JavaScript. The owned-code estimate is in [793e2f Estimated owned code](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e2f-estimated-owned-code).
- **Rationale:** the owner's brief is minimum owned code. "All logic in Go" was the author's [793b189 The System is a Claude Code mod and a Go command, with the logic in Go](#793b189-the-system-is-a-claude-code-mod-and-a-go-command-with-the-logic-in-go), not the owner's, and H1 and H2 measure surfaces and models, not the split between languages. JavaScript orchestration removes the runner, its protocol, its coverage obligation and its imptest suite.
- **Alternatives:** keep the Go runner (more owned code, the reason of [793b189 The System is a Claude Code mod and a Go command, with the logic in Go](#793b189-the-system-is-a-claude-code-mod-and-a-go-command-with-the-logic-in-go) is the author's); JavaScript for the backlog as well (a second transcript stripper).
- **Amends:** [793b189 The System is a Claude Code mod and a Go command, with the logic in Go](#793b189-the-system-is-a-claude-code-mod-and-a-go-command-with-the-logic-in-go) and [793b197 The Go side is stateless and advances a job one round per call](#793b197-the-go-side-is-stateless-and-advances-a-job-one-round-per-call) for the PoC, and [793b250 The PoC is the drafted design cut to what the 43 scenarios need](#793b250-the-poc-is-the-drafted-design-cut-to-what-the-43-scenarios-need) where it names `engram auto run` and the round protocol.
- **Amended by:** [793b275 The harness and the PoC are JavaScript; only the update's mod copies stay Go](#793b275-the-harness-and-the-poc-are-javascript-only-the-updates-mod-copies-stay-go): the owner decided that the PoC, harness and tooling are JavaScript and the Go command is subject to [793b278 The backlog scan reads the ingest manifest if SP15 shows it suffices; engram backlog stays only if it does not](#793b278-the-backlog-scan-reads-the-ingest-manifest-if-sp15-shows-it-suffices-engram-backlog-stays-only-if-it-does-not).

## 793b257 H1 is scoped to this environment, and rows for other environments are not run

- **Decided by:** Orchestrator (under owner rules; shown in walkthrough).
- **Choice:** H1 is run in a macOS terminal and with `-p` only. Rows for cloud providers and gateways, WSL, managed policy and the VS Code panel are "not run (environment)", and the verdict wording is "confirmed here / inconclusive elsewhere".
- **Rationale:** the machine and the owner's use decide what can be run.
- **Alternatives:** run the conditions on other environments (not available).
- **Amended by:** [793b268 The PoC is minimal and targeted: environments the owner does not use and everything no scenario needs are cut](#793b268-the-poc-is-minimal-and-targeted-environments-the-owner-does-not-use-and-everything-no-scenario-needs-are-cut): the owner removed those rows and scoped the hypotheses' statements to this machine, so the "inconclusive elsewhere" wording is dropped.

## 793b258 H1's verdict stays after the PoC, and the platform spikes are H1's mod-column probes

- **Decided by:** Orchestrator (under owner rules; shown in walkthrough).
- **Choice:** H1's verdict stays after the PoC, as the owner ordered. The spikes SP2 to SP9, SP12 and SP13 of [793e8a Harness skeleton and platform spikes](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8a-harness-skeleton-and-platform-spikes) are H1's mod-column probes: each is built once under `dev/acceptance/probes/`, run in the spike, and run again unchanged by [793e8i H1 probe harness and verdict](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8i-h1-probe-harness-and-verdict), which adds only the settings-hook column, the stand-in command and the report.
- **Rationale:** the same probe would otherwise be written twice; the spike result is already the mod column's evidence.
- **Alternatives:** separate H1 probes written after the PoC (duplicated work).

## 793b259 A whole-plan cost estimate is presented to the owner before the PoC is built

- **Decided by:** Orchestrator (under owner rules; shown in walkthrough).
- **Choice:** after SP11, the plan produces one estimate of the whole plan's cost at published prices and presents it to the owner before [793e8b Delivery at the moments](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8b-delivery-at-the-moments) starts: the planned full-suite runs times the per-session cost, plus the H2 calls of the four models times usage times price. No cap interrupts a run; `--max-budget-usd` is a generously sized per-session guard only. The last Work row of [793e8a Harness skeleton and platform spikes](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8a-harness-skeleton-and-platform-spikes) is this estimate, so the node is not complete until the owner has it.
- **Rationale:** the owner's rule is to estimate and confirm cost up front and let a run finish.
- **Alternatives:** a budget ceiling that stops runs (against that rule); no estimate.
- **Amended by:** [793b268 The PoC is minimal and targeted: environments the owner does not use and everything no scenario needs are cut](#793b268-the-poc-is-minimal-and-targeted-environments-the-owner-does-not-use-and-everything-no-scenario-needs-are-cut): the estimate is one table, without a model of retries and arms.

## 793b260 The live suite has its own build tag, acceptance, and check-full never runs it

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** the harness files carry the build tag `acceptance`, not `integration`. `targ acceptance [filter]` runs `go test -tags=targ,acceptance -timeout=<explicit>` with the ID-prefix filter as the run pattern. The cheap extractor test and `claude plugin validate` and `claude plugin test` of the mod stay under `integration` and run in `check-full`. A gate-reading Work row in [793e8a Harness skeleton and platform spikes](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8a-harness-skeleton-and-platform-spikes) records what the lint, coverage and nil gates do to `dev/acceptance` under the new tag.
- **Rationale:** `testDevIntegration` in `dev/targs.go` runs `go test -tags=targ,integration ./dev/...` and selects by build tag with no `-run` filter, as its comment says. A test named `TestAcceptance` under the `integration` tag would therefore still run in `check-full`, and `check-for-fail` too. Only a separate tag keeps about fifty live sessions, credentials and cost out of every check.
- **Alternatives:** the `integration` tag with a name-based exclusion (the selection is by tag, so no name protects it); a script outside targ (not the repository's convention).
- **Amends:** [793b244 Acceptance runs are TestAcceptance under the integration tag, run by targ acceptance, not by check-full](#793b244-acceptance-runs-are-testacceptance-under-the-integration-tag-run-by-targ-acceptance-not-by-check-full), whose "a new build tag for no gain" reasoning was wrong.
- **Amended by:** [793b276 The harness is cucumber-js in plain JavaScript over the Agent SDK, run by targ acceptance](#793b276-the-harness-is-cucumber-js-in-plain-javascript-over-the-agent-sdk-run-by-targ-acceptance): the live suite is not a Go build tag; it is the `targ acceptance` target and `check-full` still never runs it.

## 793b261 The World installs by running engram update in local mode, without the go install leg

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** the World runs `engram update` with the worktree as its working directory (local mode) and `ENGRAM_UPDATE_REEXEC=1`, which `Updater.Run` reads as "re-exec child" and so skips the `go install` leg (`resolveSource` receives `skipInstall`). GOMODCACHE and GOCACHE point at the real Go caches only if the build or update needs them. TMPDIR is per scenario, so the update's deterministic clone directory (`$TMPDIR/engram-update-clone`) is never shared. Acceptance therefore does not exercise the `go install` leg; it is verified by the unit tests of `internal/update` (`reexec_test.go`, `downgrade_test.go`, `runner_test.go`) and `internal/cli` (`reexec_test.go`, `update_test.go`). The setup row is in [793e8a Harness skeleton and platform spikes](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8a-harness-skeleton-and-platform-spikes).
- **Rationale:** the binary under test is the one the harness built; `go install` would write the real GOBIN, which is forbidden, and a shared clone directory would couple scenarios.
- **Alternatives:** let the update install into a temporary GOBIN (it also rebuilds the binary for each scenario); remote mode (a network clone per scenario).
- **Amends:** [793b245 Scenarios run real claude sessions in an isolated home with credentials from the environment](#793b245-scenarios-run-real-claude-sessions-in-an-isolated-home-with-credentials-from-the-environment), which said "local-clone mode against that HOME" without saying where `go install` goes.

## 793b262 Green-all for an acceptance row is the row plus the scenarios already green in its node

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** `Green-all` for an acceptance row means the row's scenario and every scenario already green in its node pass live, with `targ test` and `targ check-full`. The whole live suite runs once when a node completes and at each convergence point; refactor checkpoints run `targ check-full` plus the node's scenarios. [793e4 Test status](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e4-test-status) says so.
- **Rationale:** "the whole acceptance suite" cannot be green while later nodes' scenarios are undefined, and about fifty sessions per row is not affordable.
- **Alternatives:** the whole suite per row (unreachable, then unaffordable).

## 793b263 godog runs strict with anchored steps, and the scenario data holds inputs only

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** godog runs with `Strict: true` and fails on an ambiguous step. Each distinct assertion meaning has one anchored step definition: about 60 to 90 definitions over about ten helpers, for the roughly 170 step lines of the 43 product scenarios and the H1 steps. `scenarios.yaml` holds inputs only (seeds, prompts, keywords), never expected outcomes. The re-estimate is in [793e2f Estimated owned code](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e2f-estimated-owned-code).
- **Rationale:** a definition that matches two sentences, or an expected outcome in a data file, lets a scenario pass or fail for a reason the Gherkin does not say; about thirty generic steps could not carry the variety.
- **Alternatives:** thirty generic steps with outcomes in the data (the finding); one Go function per scenario.
- **Amends:** [793b247 Scenario data lives in one fixtures file keyed by scenario ID, so step definitions stay generic](#793b247-scenario-data-lives-in-one-fixtures-file-keyed-by-scenario-id-so-step-definitions-stay-generic).
- **Amended by:** [793b277 The Gherkin step lines of the 43 product scenarios use a fixed vocabulary of 27 steps](#793b277-the-gherkin-step-lines-of-the-43-product-scenarios-use-a-fixed-vocabulary-of-27-steps): the definitions are 27 phrasings, not 60 to 90.

## 793b264 Results include Substituted, and Pi-agent behaviour is not faked with fixtures

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** a scenario run ends Green, Red, `Not run (reason)` or `Substituted: product promise unverified` ([793e2h Results](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e2h-results)). A contract-test stand-in for a prompt origin that `-p` cannot produce ends Substituted, and the product promise counts as verified only for Green rows. There is no Pi fixture for a Then about a Pi agent's behaviour: [793a5j1d One update serves both harnesses differently](./793a_product-spec.md#793a5j1-uat-scenarios) and [793a5c1b A Pi session on a machine whose Claude Code has the System](./793a_product-spec.md#793a5c1-uat-scenarios) are `Not run` unless SP14 shows a headless Pi. Pi transcripts stay as fixture inputs where the Then is about the Claude Code System ([793a3a1j A lesson that is already recorded is not written again](./793a_product-spec.md#793a3a1-uat-scenarios), [793a3a1k A lesson in a session that ended before it was saved is captured later](./793a_product-spec.md#793a3a1-uat-scenarios), [793a5c1c A captured Pi session's transcript is left unchanged](./793a_product-spec.md#793a5c1-uat-scenarios)). The completion gate [793e9 Completion gates](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e9-completion-gates) is reworded.
- **Rationale:** a fixture that writes what the Pi agent is supposed to do tests the fixture, not the promise.
- **Alternatives:** count substitutes as green (hides the gap); write Pi behaviour fixtures.
- **Amends:** [793b249 Scenarios whose prompt origin cannot be produced in -p get a contract-test substitute](#793b249-scenarios-whose-prompt-origin-cannot-be-produced-in--p-get-a-contract-test-substitute).

## 793b265 H2 is replayed by a small mod over fixtures, and gold comes from history or the owner

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** the replay is a small mod that loops `$.model.complete` over the fixtures for both tiers and logs `usage` and wall time per call, with the request text built by the PoC's own request builders; cost is `usage` times the published price. Gold for `reconcile` and `curation` is read mechanically from the vault's history; gold for `relevance` and `extraction` is drafted by fable and confirmed by the owner for every fixture ([793b270 793c71 is answered: a third model drafts the gold, fable here, and the owner confirms every label](#793b270-793c71-is-answered-a-third-model-drafts-the-gold-fable-here-and-the-owner-confirms-every-label)). [793e8h Hypothesis scenarios updated to the PoC](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8h-hypothesis-scenarios-updated-to-the-poc) sizes each kind and arm for the margin: a paired bootstrap over fixtures plus the run-to-run spread; at 40 fixtures the standard error is about 0.063, above the 0.05 margin, so the verdict arm takes about 200 fixtures ([793b272 H2 arm sample sizes are inversely proportional to per-token price, anchored at the full set for sonnet](#793b272-h2-arm-sample-sizes-are-inversely-proportional-to-per-token-price-anchored-at-the-full-set-for-sonnet)), as a spec update to [793d18 H2: the cheapest capable model is adequate for every System judgement, and is faster and cheaper](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d18-h2-the-cheapest-capable-model-is-adequate-for-every-system-judgement-and-is-faster-and-cheaper) made in that node.
- **Rationale:** the PoC relays `$.model.complete`, so the replay should make the same call; `claude -p --output-format json` adds a process and a session around it.
- **Alternatives:** replay through `claude -p` (the call differs); owner-labelled gold from scratch ([793c71 Who labels the relevance and extraction gold?](./793c_open-questions.md#793c71-who-labels-the-relevance-and-extraction-gold) asks).
- **Amends:** [793b253 The hypothesis scenarios are updated to the PoC before they are run; unrealised kinds are recorded, not built](#793b253-the-hypothesis-scenarios-are-updated-to-the-poc-before-they-are-run-unrealised-kinds-are-recorded-not-built), its item (b).

## 793b267 The OAuth token comes from a file the harness reads only when the variable is unset

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe, 2026-10-12), as relayed by the orchestrator: the token file is `~/.config/claude-key.env` (it sets `CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN`; mode 0600); the harness sources it only when the variable is unset; it never prints the token.
- **Choice:** [793e2a Toolchain](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e2a-toolchain), [793e2d Environment the suites need](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e2d-environment-the-suites-need) and the credentials row of [793e8a Harness skeleton and platform spikes](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8a-harness-skeleton-and-platform-spikes) say so. The token reaches a session through its environment only, and the harness fails, naming the variable and the file, when neither it nor `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is available.
- **Rationale:** the owner's fact; the isolated HOME holds no stored login.
- **Alternatives:** copy a stored login into the temporary HOME (reads the real `~/.claude`; forbidden).
- **Amends:** [793b245 Scenarios run real claude sessions in an isolated home with credentials from the environment](#793b245-scenarios-run-real-claude-sessions-in-an-isolated-home-with-credentials-from-the-environment) and [793b254 The CLI absent skips, anything else missing fails](#793b254-the-cli-absent-skips-anything-else-missing-fails), whose credential source was the environment alone.

## 793b269 793c70 is answered: the gold is mined read-only from the real transcripts and vault

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe, 2026-10-12): yes, read-only. Recorded as [793c70 May the H2 gold be mined read-only from the real transcripts and vault?](./793c_open-questions.md#793c70-may-the-h2-gold-be-mined-read-only-from-the-real-transcripts-and-vault).
- **Choice:** the H2 fixtures are drawn by a one-off read-only mining of the real transcripts and the vault's history; nothing in the real `~/.claude`, `~/.pi` or vault is written, and a fixture is copied into the repository only after the owner confirms it. The fixture row of [793e8j H2 eval and verdict](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8j-h2-eval-and-verdict) says so.
- **Rationale:** real inputs are what the parity claim of [793d18 H2: the cheapest capable model is adequate for every System judgement, and is faster and cheaper](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d18-h2-the-cheapest-capable-model-is-adequate-for-every-system-judgement-and-is-faster-and-cheaper) is about.
- **Alternatives:** synthetic fixtures; vault history only (the other options of [793c70 May the H2 gold be mined read-only from the real transcripts and vault?](./793c_open-questions.md#793c70-may-the-h2-gold-be-mined-read-only-from-the-real-transcripts-and-vault)).

## 793b270 793c71 is answered: a third model drafts the gold, fable here, and the owner confirms every label

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe, 2026-10-12) for the answer: a third model drafts, the owner confirms every fixture; recorded as [793c71 Who labels the relevance and extraction gold?](./793c_open-questions.md#793c71-who-labels-the-relevance-and-extraction-gold). As relayed, since sonnet, opus and fable are all comparison arms no candidate is "neither tier under test", so the plan records which model drafted and treats the owner's confirmation of every label as what makes it gold, and prefers drafting with the arm whose bias matters least. Author (agent) for the choice of drafter, awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** fable drafts the `relevance` and `extraction` labels. The owner confirms every label; the share of drafts he changes is reported with the H2 table. Gold for `reconcile` and `curation` is read mechanically from the vault's history and needs no drafter.
- **Rationale:** a drafter can favour its own style, so the arm whose result matters least should draft. Haiku is the tier under test and sonnet is the verdict arm on the full set; opus has the next largest sample and fable the smallest, coarsest one ([793b272 H2 arm sample sizes are inversely proportional to per-token price, anchored at the full set for sonnet](#793b272-h2-arm-sample-sizes-are-inversely-proportional-to-per-token-price-anchored-at-the-full-set-for-sonnet)). Every label is the owner's after his confirmation, which bounds the bias to the first draft.
- **Alternatives:** opus as drafter (its arm has the larger sample); the owner labelling from scratch (more of his time); drafting with sonnet or haiku (biases the verdict).

## 793b271 793c72 is answered: acceptance sessions on sonnet, and H2 compares three cost-scaled arms

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe, 2026-10-12): the acceptance sessions are pinned to sonnet. The H2 comparison arms are sonnet on the full fixture set, opus on a sample and fable on a sample, all presented in the criteria table beside haiku, which runs the full set, because the owner's main-agent model shifts. Refinement the same day: the sample sizes are cost-proportional throughout; opus's is scaled down from sonnet's by the sonnet:opus published price ratio, and fable's from opus's by the opus:fable ratio. Recorded as [793c72 Which model do the acceptance sessions run on, and which are H2's comparison arms?](./793c_open-questions.md#793c72-which-model-do-the-acceptance-sessions-run-on-and-which-are-h2s-comparison-arms).
- **Choice:** `--model sonnet` for every acceptance session ([793e8a Harness skeleton and platform spikes](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8a-harness-skeleton-and-platform-spikes)); the arms, the rule, the ratios and the sizes are [793b272 H2 arm sample sizes are inversely proportional to per-token price, anchored at the full set for sonnet](#793b272-h2-arm-sample-sizes-are-inversely-proportional-to-per-token-price-anchored-at-the-full-set-for-sonnet).
- **Rationale:** the owner's.
- **Alternatives:** opus or the account default for the sessions; a single comparison arm; the full set on every arm.
- **Amends:** [793d18 H2: the cheapest capable model is adequate for every System judgement, and is faster and cheaper](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d18-h2-the-cheapest-capable-model-is-adequate-for-every-system-judgement-and-is-faster-and-cheaper)'s single "session model", by a spec update made in [793e8h Hypothesis scenarios updated to the PoC](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8h-hypothesis-scenarios-updated-to-the-poc).

## 793b272 H2 arm sample sizes are inversely proportional to per-token price, anchored at the full set for sonnet

- **Decided by:** Author (agent) for the arithmetic and the margins; the rule is the owner's ([793b271 793c72 is answered: acceptance sessions on sonnet, and H2 compares three cost-scaled arms](#793b271-793c72-is-answered-acceptance-sessions-on-sonnet-and-h2-compares-three-cost-scaled-arms)). Awaiting owner validation of the numbers.
- **Choice:** the rule is: an arm's sample size is inversely proportional to its per-token price, anchored at the full set for sonnet. Haiku, the arm under test, and sonnet run the full set. Published per-token prices (first-party, as listed on 2026-10-06; the estimate row of [793e8a Harness skeleton and platform spikes](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8a-harness-skeleton-and-platform-spikes) re-checks them): sonnet (Claude Sonnet 5.5) 2 US dollars per million input tokens and 10 output, opus (Claude Opus 5.5) 4 and 20, fable (Claude Fable 5.1) 10 and 50, haiku (Claude Haiku 5.5) 0.10 and 0.50. The input and output ratios are the same: sonnet:opus is 1:2 and opus:fable is 1:2.5, so sonnet:fable is 1:5. Opus therefore takes one half of the full set and fable one fifth. With the full set of N = 200 fixtures per kind, which [793e8h Hypothesis scenarios updated to the PoC](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8h-hypothesis-scenarios-updated-to-the-poc) confirms: sonnet and haiku 200, opus 100, fable 40, each non-haiku arm costing the same (200, 100 times 2 and 40 times 5 sonnet-fixture units). Margins: using the per-fixture difference spread of about 0.4 implied by the review's standard error of 0.063 at 40 fixtures, the one-sided 95% bound is 1.645 times 0.4 over the square root of n: about 0.047 at 200 (inside the 0.05 margin, so a verdict-level arm), about 0.066 at 100 (opus), and about 0.10 at 40 (fable). The opus and fable samples are stratified (the subtle stratum and the none/absent fixtures included) and indicative: they can show a haiku shortfall larger than their margin, and cannot show parity at 0.05. They are reported with a column "margin the sample supports", and an indicative arm that shows a shortfall beyond its margin is flagged to the owner, not counted as a disproof. The verdict per kind comes from the sonnet arm, and the criteria table shows all four models. The sizes are recomputed from the rule if [793e8h Hypothesis scenarios updated to the PoC](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8h-hypothesis-scenarios-updated-to-the-poc) changes N or the prices differ on the day. The update to [793d18 H2: the cheapest capable model is adequate for every System judgement, and is faster and cheaper](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d18-h2-the-cheapest-capable-model-is-adequate-for-every-system-judgement-and-is-faster-and-cheaper) is made in [793e8h Hypothesis scenarios updated to the PoC](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8h-hypothesis-scenarios-updated-to-the-poc).
- **Rationale:** the owner's cost-proportional rule makes every arm cost the same; the coarser margins are stated rather than implied.
- **Alternatives:** the full set on every arm (opus costs twice and fable five times sonnet's); a wider margin for all arms; one comparison arm.

## 793b275 The harness and the PoC are JavaScript; only the update's mod copies stay Go

- **Decided by:** Owner (Joe, 2026-10-12): the harness and the PoC are JavaScript or TypeScript, not Go; only the deploy of the mod by `engram update` stays Go.
- **Choice:** the harness (`dev/acceptance/`), its step definitions, the spike probes, the PoC [mod](./793d_technical-spec.md#793d1b-terms) and the H1 and H2 tooling are JavaScript. The mod's contract tests are TypeScript, because `claude plugin test` runs `*.test.ts` files. The Go code of this change is the `engram update` mod copies with its old-Claude-Code report, the small `targ acceptance` target, and `engram backlog` only if [793b278 The backlog scan reads the ingest manifest if SP15 shows it suffices; engram backlog stays only if it does not](#793b278-the-backlog-scan-reads-the-ingest-manifest-if-sp15-shows-it-suffices-engram-backlog-stays-only-if-it-does-not) says so.
- **Rationale (the owner's):** the same language as the mod under test; no dependency-injection plumbing; cucumber-js equals godog; the Agent SDK drives sessions with typed message and hook access instead of parsing `claude -p` JSONL by hand; `claude plugin test` already runs JavaScript tests.
- **Alternatives:** the author's earlier choice, godog and a Go harness ([793b243 The harness is godog over scenarios extracted from the product spec at run time](#793b243-the-harness-is-godog-over-scenarios-extracted-from-the-product-spec-at-run-time)), replaced by the owner's decision.
- **Amends:** [793b243 The harness is godog over scenarios extracted from the product spec at run time](#793b243-the-harness-is-godog-over-scenarios-extracted-from-the-product-spec-at-run-time), [793b245 Scenarios run real claude sessions in an isolated home with credentials from the environment](#793b245-scenarios-run-real-claude-sessions-in-an-isolated-home-with-credentials-from-the-environment), [793b247 Scenario data lives in one fixtures file keyed by scenario ID, so step definitions stay generic](#793b247-scenario-data-lives-in-one-fixtures-file-keyed-by-scenario-id-so-step-definitions-stay-generic), [793b248 The session driver streams input and waits for the System's idle marker](#793b248-the-session-driver-streams-input-and-waits-for-the-systems-idle-marker), [793b256 The PoC orchestrates in JavaScript inside the mod over the existing engram commands](#793b256-the-poc-orchestrates-in-javascript-inside-the-mod-over-the-existing-engram-commands), [793b260 The live suite has its own build tag, acceptance, and check-full never runs it](#793b260-the-live-suite-has-its-own-build-tag-acceptance-and-check-full-never-runs-it) and [793b263 godog runs strict with anchored steps, and the scenario data holds inputs only](#793b263-godog-runs-strict-with-anchored-steps-and-the-scenario-data-holds-inputs-only); the choices that follow from the language are in [793b276 The harness is cucumber-js in plain JavaScript over the Agent SDK, run by targ acceptance](#793b276-the-harness-is-cucumber-js-in-plain-javascript-over-the-agent-sdk-run-by-targ-acceptance).

## 793b276 The harness is cucumber-js in plain JavaScript over the Agent SDK, run by targ acceptance

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation. It applies [793b275 The harness and the PoC are JavaScript; only the update's mod copies stay Go](#793b275-the-harness-and-the-poc-are-javascript-only-the-updates-mod-copies-stay-go) and the owner's rule of minimum owned code. The Agent SDK facts below were read from its TypeScript reference and from the declarations of the installed package (`@anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk` 0.3.296, which bundles Claude Code 2.1.296).
- **Choice:**
  - **Runner.** cucumber-js 13 (`@cucumber/cucumber`) in plain JavaScript as ES modules: no build step and no `tsconfig`, and node runs the files as written. TypeScript would add a compile or type-stripping step to about two thousand lines that have no consumer of their types. Undefined, pending and ambiguous steps fail the run (strict); the step vocabulary is checked by a cucumber dry run before any session starts.
  - **Package.** `dev/acceptance/` holds `package.json`, a committed `package-lock.json`, `run.js`, `lib/`, `steps/`, `test/` and `scenarios.js`; `node_modules` is already ignored. The dev dependencies are `@cucumber/cucumber` and `@anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk`, pinned exactly.
  - **Features.** The extractor writes the `gherkin` blocks of the product spec (and of the H1 section of the technical spec), with a `Feature:` line per `UAT Scenarios` or `Experiments` heading, to a per-run temporary directory that the run removes, because cucumber-js reads feature files from paths. No `.feature` file is committed; the scenario IDs in the scenario names are the trace.
  - **Sessions.** One Agent SDK `query()` per scenario session, with an async-iterable prompt (streaming input) so that further prompts, compaction and resume reach the same session; `env` replaced by an explicit list (the SDK replaces, not merges, the subprocess environment, so no variable of the calling session leaks in); `cwd` the workspace; `model: 'sonnet'`; `pathToClaudeCodeExecutable` the `claude` on PATH, so the suite exercises the installed Claude Code; the `stderr` callback for the fail-fast on `hooks module ... not loaded`; `maxBudgetUsd` as a generous per-session guard. The typed message stream (the init message carries `session_id`, `plugins`, `apiKeySource` and `claude_code_version`) is a second observable beside the transcript JSONL that Claude Code writes under the temporary HOME.
  - **Data.** `dev/acceptance/scenarios.js`, an ES module keyed by scenario ID, holds inputs only (scripts for the turns, seeds, keywords that identify a lesson). It is JavaScript rather than YAML because a parser would be one more dependency and the prompts are multi-line strings.
  - **Running.** `targ acceptance [filter]` is a Go target in `dev/targs.go` that checks for `node`, `npm` and `claude`, runs `npm ci` when `node_modules` is missing, and runs `node dev/acceptance/run.js`; `--dry-run` runs the vocabulary check without a session. A missing program fails with a message that names it. The live suite is not a Go build tag any more: it stays outside `check-full` because only this target runs it.
  - **What `check-full` runs.** The extractor's `node:test` suite (no dependency installed), through one `dev` integration test that skips with a reason when `node` is absent, as [793b254 The CLI absent skips, anything else missing fails](#793b254-the-cli-absent-skips-anything-else-missing-fails) does for `claude`.
- **Rationale:** the owner's reasons in [793b275 The harness and the PoC are JavaScript; only the update's mod copies stay Go](#793b275-the-harness-and-the-poc-are-javascript-only-the-updates-mod-copies-stay-go); the failure of the live target on a missing program (instead of a skip) is the owner's direction, and a skip there would only hide a missing install because nothing else runs the suite.
- **Alternatives:** TypeScript for the harness; YAML scenario data; the `claude -p` stream-json protocol parsed by hand; in-memory feature contents (not offered by cucumber-js, whose programmatic API takes feature paths).
- **Amends:** [793b254 The CLI absent skips, anything else missing fails](#793b254-the-cli-absent-skips-anything-else-missing-fails) for `targ acceptance` (a missing `claude` fails there; the mod's contract test and the extractor test still skip), and [793b260 The live suite has its own build tag, acceptance, and check-full never runs it](#793b260-the-live-suite-has-its-own-build-tag-acceptance-and-check-full-never-runs-it) (no Go build tag; the principle that `check-full` never runs the live suite stands).

## 793b277 The Gherkin step lines of the 43 product scenarios use a fixed vocabulary of 27 steps

- **Decided by:** Author (agent), with the owner's approval of the lever (2026-10-12): wording only, no change of meaning, IDs kept.
- **Choice:** the step lines of the 43 scenarios of [Parallel Memory Agent - Product Spec](./793a_product-spec.md#parallel-memory-agent---product-spec) are rewritten onto 27 step definitions, whose exact phrasings are the catalog [793e2i Step catalog](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e2i-step-catalog). Every scenario keeps its ID, its title and its intent. Where a rewording is more than a phrasing, it is listed here so that the owner can overrule it:

| Scenario | Rewording | Why it keeps the meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [793a2n1a Recall happens without the agent running it](./793a_product-spec.md#793a2n1-uat-scenarios), [793a2n1h A prompt from any source receives relevant notes](./793a_product-spec.md#793a2n1-uat-scenarios), [793a2e1a Note delivered earlier is not delivered again](./793a_product-spec.md#793a2e1-uat-scenarios), [793a3a1k A lesson in a session that ended before it was saved is captured later](./793a_product-spec.md#793a3a1-uat-scenarios) | The lines "the note is not yet in the transcript", "the session has not been compacted" and "no note states that lesson" are dropped | Each restates the state of a fresh session and an unseeded vault, which the harness guarantees; the ended-session step of the catalog asserts that the lesson is not recorded |
| [793a2n1a Recall happens without the agent running it](./793a_product-spec.md#793a2n1-uat-scenarios), [793a2n1i Please's orientation receives relevant notes](./793a_product-spec.md#793a2n1-uat-scenarios), [793a2n1j Tier evidence is delivered before a dispatch is routed](./793a_product-spec.md#793a2n1-uat-scenarios) and every delivery Then | "before the declaration", "before please's first response or tool call", "before the orchestrator routes the unit" become "before the agent's next response or tool call" | It is the wording of the requirement [793a2n Relevant memory reaches the agent at the moments](./793a_product-spec.md#793a2n-relevant-memory-reaches-the-agent-at-the-moments); each named point is a response or tool call of the agent |
| [793a4c1a Tool calls and permissions unchanged](./793a_product-spec.md#793a4c1-uat-scenarios), [793a4c1b The prompt is unchanged and the note is separate](./793a_product-spec.md#793a4c1-uat-scenarios), [793a4c1c The only effect on the working agent's context is what is delivered](./793a_product-spec.md#793a4c1-uat-scenarios), [793a4b1c No error reaches the agent and the user is warned](./793a_product-spec.md#793a4b1-uat-scenarios), [793a5h1c The summary is not in the agent's context](./793a_product-spec.md#793a5h1-uat-scenarios) | The When "the session transcript is inspected" is removed and the inspection is the Then's own check; the three Thens of 4c1a and the two of 4c1b are each one catalog step | An inspection is not an event; every observable of the original Thens is in the step |
| [793a4j1d The agent is not asked to curate](./793a_product-spec.md#793a4j1-uat-scenarios), and the Thens that say "no request to the agent to ..." in 2n1a, 2n1i, 2n1j, 3a1a, 3a1g, 3a1k, 4c1f | One fixed phrase covers "no memory command to run and no note to evaluate, approve, write or curate" | It is the list of [793a4c Memory purview only](./793a_product-spec.md#793a4c-memory-purview-only); the check is the same for every scenario |
| [793a5h1e The counts cover the events since the previous summary](./793a_product-spec.md#793a5h1-uat-scenarios) | The Then "no event counted in the previous summary is counted again" is dropped | The remaining Then fixes the other counts at zero, which a recount would break |
| [793a5k1c An intentional recall by the agent is allowed](./793a_product-spec.md#793a5k1-uat-scenarios), [793a5k1e An intentional engram query by the agent is allowed](./793a_product-spec.md#793a5k1-uat-scenarios) | The recall's results gain "and no error" | 5k1e already says it; "allowed" means the run is not an error |
| [793a5j1b An existing install gains the System and keeps what it had](./793a_product-spec.md#793a5j1-uat-scenarios), [793a5j1c A preview installs none of the System](./793a_product-spec.md#793a5j1-uat-scenarios), [793a5j1d One update serves both harnesses differently](./793a_product-spec.md#793a5j1-uat-scenarios), [793a5c1b A Pi session on a machine whose Claude Code has the System](./793a_product-spec.md#793a5c1-uat-scenarios), 4j1c, 4j1d, 4c1* | A Given seeds the note that "a prompt that matches a note" presupposes | The prompt cannot match a note that is not there |
| [793a5j1d One update serves both harnesses differently](./793a_product-spec.md#793a5j1-uat-scenarios) | The Pi session that its Then ran implicitly is a When step | The same session, made explicit |
| [793a5c1a Unsupported harness](./793a_product-spec.md#793a5c1-uat-scenarios) | "A harness without support" is "the System is not installed" | The reading already recorded in [793b273 The implementation-plan audit of the Gherkin scenarios](#793b273-the-implementation-plan-audit-of-the-gherkin-scenarios) |
| [793a3a1a A lesson is captured without agent action](./793a_product-spec.md#793a3a1-uat-scenarios), [793a3a1k A lesson in a session that ended before it was saved is captured later](./793a_product-spec.md#793a3a1-uat-scenarios) | The cells "that rule" and "that releases are tagged from main" state the rule | The cell named the rule of the example's own "said" text |
| [793a3a1j A lesson that is already recorded is not written again](./793a_product-spec.md#793a3a1-uat-scenarios) | Each example cell is a complete catalog phrase of the history, and a final neutral turn is the When | The three flows differ, so the cell holds the flow; the Then is checked after the System's work for the last turn |
| [793a2e1g A resumed session's restored content counts as present](./793a_product-spec.md#793a2e1-uat-scenarios) | "the resumed session's transcript shows X's content restored" is a Given that reuses the delivery step | The same observable |

- **Rationale:** a step that matches two sentences, and an expected outcome in a data file, are what [793b263 godog runs strict with anchored steps, and the scenario data holds inputs only](#793b263-godog-runs-strict-with-anchored-steps-and-the-scenario-data-holds-inputs-only) found to be the cost of free wording; about 170 free step lines needed 60 to 90 definitions, and 27 phrasings carry the same scenarios. Every outcome is in a step; the data holds inputs.
- **Alternatives:** keep the free wording and write 60 to 90 definitions (the previous plan, [793b263 godog runs strict with anchored steps, and the scenario data holds inputs only](#793b263-godog-runs-strict-with-anchored-steps-and-the-scenario-data-holds-inputs-only)); split the outline of 793a3a1j into three scenarios (it would add IDs and change the run counts of the plan).
- **Amends:** [793b263 godog runs strict with anchored steps, and the scenario data holds inputs only](#793b263-godog-runs-strict-with-anchored-steps-and-the-scenario-data-holds-inputs-only) (the number of definitions) and the step wording of [793b273 The implementation-plan audit of the Gherkin scenarios](#793b273-the-implementation-plan-audit-of-the-gherkin-scenarios)'s rows (their findings and resolutions stand).

## 793b278 The backlog scan reads the ingest manifest if SP15 shows it suffices; engram backlog stays only if it does not

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation. It follows the owner's direction of 2026-10-12: drop `engram backlog` in favour of deriving "not yet captured" from the ingest manifest if a spike shows it suffices, else keep a minimal Go command.
- **Choice:** spike SP15 of [793e2e Platform claims to verify first](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e2e-platform-claims-to-verify-first) asks whether the mod can list the transcript sources with content not yet captured, and read their stripped text, from what `engram ingest --auto` already leaves on disk (the manifest of the chunks directory, the chunk files, and the `source` of the notes capture writes), without a new Go command. Its outcome is recorded in the spike table and decides the mod's backlog design in [793e8g Backlog capture](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8g-backlog-capture); if the manifest does not suffice, the minimal command of [793b256 The PoC orchestrates in JavaScript inside the mod over the existing engram commands](#793b256-the-poc-orchestrates-in-javascript-inside-the-mod-over-the-existing-engram-commands) stays, its flags fixed in that node.
- **Rationale:** the owner's minimum-owned-code rule, and the Go command exists only because of the stripped text and the sweep roots; if the ingest output already holds both, a second implementation of either is waste.
- **Alternatives:** keep the command unconditionally (the previous plan); drop it without the spike (the manifest records what was ingested, not what was captured, so the claim needs evidence).
- **Amends:** [793b256 The PoC orchestrates in JavaScript inside the mod over the existing engram commands](#793b256-the-poc-orchestrates-in-javascript-inside-the-mod-over-the-existing-engram-commands) (one small Go command stays) and [793b232 Backlog capture is a watermark per transcript source and a scan after each turn's own capture, and the $.store queue is dropped](#793b232-backlog-capture-is-a-watermark-per-transcript-source-and-a-scan-after-each-turns-own-capture-and-the-store-queue-is-dropped) where it places the watermark on the Go side (a design draft in any case, [793b240 The product spec is the owner's intent; the technical spec is his two hypotheses with the scenarios that test them](#793b240-the-product-spec-is-the-owners-intent-the-technical-spec-is-his-two-hypotheses-with-the-scenarios-that-test-them)).

## 793b279 Judgement is one richer prompt per kind, and capture judges extraction and reconciliation in one call

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation. It follows the owner's direction of 2026-10-12: one richer haiku prompt per turn where that collapses extraction and reconciliation.
- **Choice:** the PoC's judgement kinds are `relevance`, `capture` and `curation`. The `capture` request carries the turn's window and the nearest notes of the whole window (one `engram query` over the window text) and returns the lessons the window contains that no note covers, with the covered ones marked for reinforcement; it replaces the `extraction` request and one `reconcile` request per candidate. Each kind has one `judgeTiers` row, defaulting to `haiku`.
- **Rationale:** one call per turn instead of one plus one per candidate, which is fewer calls to wait for and to pay for; the mod's queue runs one job at a time, so the saving is wall time after the turn.
- **Alternatives:** keep `extraction` and `reconcile` as separate kinds (the drafted design, and the kinds of H2 as written). The risk of the merged call is that the nearest notes of the window miss the note that covers one lesson; [793a3a1j A lesson that is already recorded is not written again](./793a_product-spec.md#793a3a1-uat-scenarios) is the scenario that shows it.
- **Amends:** [793b250 The PoC is the drafted design cut to what the 43 scenarios need](#793b250-the-poc-is-the-drafted-design-cut-to-what-the-43-scenarios-need) (the kinds of `judgeTiers`). H2's `extraction` and `reconcile` kinds are then unrealised as such; [793e8h Hypothesis scenarios updated to the PoC](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8h-hypothesis-scenarios-updated-to-the-poc) maps them to `capture` as [793b253 The hypothesis scenarios are updated to the PoC before they are run; unrealised kinds are recorded, not built](#793b253-the-hypothesis-scenarios-are-updated-to-the-poc-before-they-are-run-unrealised-kinds-are-recorded-not-built) says.

- **Decided by:** Author (agent); awaiting owner validation.
- **Choice:** added (each checked with `git log -S`: unused): `793b275` to `793b280`, `793e2i` and spike `SP15`. Retired: none. No heading is retitled. The Work rows of [793e8a Harness skeleton and platform spikes](./793e_implementation-plan.md#793e8a-harness-skeleton-and-platform-spikes) are reworded for the JavaScript harness; no row ID exists to retire.
- **Rationale:** the guide: removed IDs are not reused.
- **Alternatives:** none.
